111 My heart burned with sorrow for you after reading these words, for you have relied on precisely the same fanciful arguments and ambiguous statements by which the seminary students now establish their claims in their schools. You yourself regard the people of the Qurʾán as deluded, yet your own delusion is seen to exceed theirs a hundred thousandfold. How long will you lie asleep upon the carpet? Raise your head from sleep, then open your eyes to behold the sun shining at its noonday height. Set these words aside and look toward the One who revealed them. Cast away delusion and cling to the Sovereign of certitude. What a waste of hearing and sight that they should be occupied with looking at and listening to these words!

112 First, what you have written is the reverse of the truth. Moreover, He says: “Do you believe in part of the Book and disbelieve in part?” How have you accepted as indisputable these words, whose reference is not known at all, while the whole Bayán declares that at the time of the Manifestation you must cling to nothing other than Him? This utterance of the All-Merciful goes unheard, while fabricated words must now be listened to!

113 There has never been, nor is there now, any mention in the Bayán of legateeship or succession. In your own words, these are the terms of the people of the Qurʾán. What is mentioned in the Bayán is Letters and Mirrors, and throughout it He states that the Mirrors have not been, and will not be, restricted to a fixed number. Any soul who faces the radiance of the divine Sun is counted among the Mirrors, and this station endures so long as that soul does not turn away from facing it. After that turning away, the light returns to its seat, and the creature reverts to its own kind and likeness.

114 Throughout the Bayán He specifically and explicitly declares: “On the day of the Manifestation, never look toward anyone other than Him,” for that Ancient Beauty cannot be recognized through the eyes of anyone other than Himself, nor has He been. Now the Primal Point Himself declares that what you write is erroneous and that your arguments refer back to the selves of the deluded. The True One has appeared with His sovereignty, His proof—His verses—and His manifestation—His own Self. Die, then, in your rage, you company of the deluded!

115 You have mentioned outsiders, insiders, and common report, writing all these words out of sheer delusion and pure conjecture. As for outsiders, they have had no knowledge at all. As for insiders, some have ascended to the Most Exalted Companion, while those who are still present you have branded as liars. Following your own desires, you have put together fabrications so that you might deprive the helpless and weak of the unassailable True One, just as others have done before. If only it were understood who gave rise to that common report and what the wisdom behind it was! None knows it except those endowed with [corrupted original text].

116 This servant is bewildered as to the grounds on which you claim to have knowledge, for you have never been within this Cause. Indeed, before long unsound and false reports will emerge from the realm of the lies of idolatrous souls. Those reports will certainly be furnished with chains of transmission, ending with Siyyid Muḥammad-i-Iṣfahání, or Ḥusayn, known as Súkhtih, who has arrived in these days and is under the shadow of Satan, or Mírzá Ḥusayn-i-Qummí, who spat in the face of the Beloved to preserve his own vile self, or the lame man who disavowed God while on the pulpit. You yourself know that none of these souls had any knowledge, yet your desire has led you to affirm someone in whom there is no truthfulness. Fear God, do not say what you do not know, and do not be among those who fabricate falsehoods.

117 Even if all who are on earth today were to become refined mirrors and testify to a matter, this would not suffice, for the testimony of all depends on the Manifestation Himself. Thus the Point of the Bayán—may the souls of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—says concerning the other Manifestation, in His words, exalted be His mention: “If all who are on earth testify to a matter and He testifies to something other than what they have testified to, His testimony is like the sun, and theirs is like an image of the sun that has not been placed facing it; otherwise, it would accord with His testimony. I swear by the most holy Essence of God that one line of His words is better than the words of all who are on earth. Indeed, I ask forgiveness for this use of the comparative. How could the effects of suns in mirrors ever become like the effects of the sun in heaven? The former is at the level of nothingness, and the latter at the level of the One who brings things into being, by God, mighty and glorious is He.” End of quotation.

118 Now, what you suppose to be mentioned and widely known among people has never had, and will never have, any standing before God. Everything is conditional upon and dependent on the confirmation of this most wondrous, most unassailable, most holy Manifestation, if you only knew. Even supposing that what you have mentioned is acknowledged, He says—exalted be His mention—that at the time of the other Manifestation the people of Sinai become dead and as nothing, the Letters and the guides of light remain veiled, and the tree of affirmation is reckoned among negation. He likewise says: “Many a soul who in one Manifestation is among the highest of the inhabitants of paradise becomes, at the time of the next Manifestation, among the lowest of the inhabitants of the fire.”

119 Say to those who turn away and harbor malice: “You who lack sight and have deprived and debarred yourselves from the Kawthar of meanings, the Tasním of knowledge, and the Salsabíl of utterance! Reflect upon ‘the highest,’ so that you may perhaps drink a drop from the Most Great Ocean.” Likewise, in another passage He says—exalted be His mention—that the highest inhabitants of the former paradise come to dwell in the lowest depths of the fire. Pure sight is now needed to reflect upon these utterances of the Manifestation of the Cause. At the time of each Manifestation, the idolaters have always clung to His previous words and raised objections against the Manifestation of the divine Self, just as you today have clung to His previous words and have raised, and continue to raise, objections against His own Self. Woe to them, to you, and to those who follow you, from the punishment of a tremendous day!

120 You have become so heedless that you send to the most pure, most unassailable Seat words fit for the most discerning of the idolaters to examine. On this subject the previous Manifestation—may the souls of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—made a statement. It is quoted solely for the sake of God, so that those who turn away may perhaps be awakened and not remain veiled from the Sun of guidance. His words, exalted be His mention: “The likeness of those days is that of the sun in relation to the stars, and the likeness of the people of that Manifestation in relation to the One who appears is likewise the same. For this reason, in that Manifestation those who recognize Him do not set their pens in motion, and feel shame at their own compositions, writings, and literary productions, and their own manifestations and appearances.” End of quotation. If only they would listen to this one counsel of the Sovereign of Oneness and refrain from committing what they have been forbidden! I do not know what path they walk or what ways they follow. They adduce the verses of God as evidence, then disbelieve in the One who revealed them.

121 As for your statement that the Báb endorsed Mírzá Yaḥyá: first, you do not understand that endorsement or what its purpose and intention were. For the heavenly maidens of divine meanings, within the chambers of words endowed with divine infallibility, have always been and will remain concealed from the [corrupted original text] of creation. No one has [corrupted original text] except the Manifestation Himself and those who pointed to Him on the day of His appearance. By God, He endorsed none other than this Beauty, who has fallen among the claws of the idolaters, beneath the talons of the deniers, and then beneath the tongues of the heedless and the malicious. Even supposing this claim to be true, if you examine it with discerning sight, you will see that this endorsement applies to everyone. For He says, exalted be His mention, that there has been no doubt that all souls who have believed in the Bayán have been and are prophets, saints, and chosen ones. Then, elsewhere, He says, exalted be His mention: “All this is so that, on the day of the Manifestation, you may not remain veiled by names, but may instead look to that through which the names of all things subsist, even the designation ‘Prophet’; for that name is created through Him by what God reveals.” End of quotation.

122 It is astonishing that you are still stopped at the obstacle of names. Well done! How well you have carried out the divine counsel! You have become so heedless that you worship names and remain devoted to them, as you continually declare, while at every moment you have drawn, and continue to draw, the sword of malice against Him through whom the names subsist. You do not perceive what you know.

123 All the arguments and descriptions you have offered or may offer concerning any person whatsoever refer back to the Bayán. Throughout the Bayán it is expressly stated that the Bayán and all it contains have been and will remain dependent on the will of that Sovereign of Oneness. That ancient Beauty has the power to negate all His creation with the word “No” and consign them to the lowest depths of the fire, or to draw them all beneath the shelter of affirmation with the word “Yes” and establish them on the loftiest summit of the paradise of recognition. He is not questioned about what He does, while everyone is questioned about everything.

124 If only you understood whom He endorsed! By God, besides whom there is no god, He passed no morning except in remembrance of Him, no evening except in His praise; He made no movement except out of love for Him and drew no breath except in His Cause. Yet you have turned away from Him, opposed Him, and clung to one who, in God’s sight, is more abject than you, if you only knew. You have turned away from the Beloved of all existence, concerning whom the Point of the Bayán says, exalted be His mention, “Had it not been for His Book, that Book would not have been revealed; and had it not been for His own Self, God would not have manifested Me.” Yet you write that He praised a certain person in the Bayán, although you have neither understood the praise nor recognized the one who praised or the one who was praised. By God, if you knew what you had done, you would weep for yourself. If you recognized this Manifestation and possessed enough gold and silver to fill the heavens and the earth, you would spend it to enter His shadow and hear a single melody among His melodies. But because you have remained veiled from this, you have been deprived of the breezes of God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting.

125 O my brother! By God, those who have disbelieved today have suffered deprivation upon deprivation, loss upon loss, abasement upon abasement, and diminishment upon diminishment. Yet they will not understand until the angels of punishment come to them and their eyes move under the assaults of death. Then they will cry out in anguish and say, “Is there any way open to us?” Then the angels will strike their mouths with the hands of subjugation and return them to their dwelling in the depths of the fire. Thus has the matter been decreed by God, the All-Powerful, the All-Subduing. As for you, erase what you possess, then inscribe what is with God upon the tablet of your breast, so that you may find it polished and illumined by the light of the suns of wisdom and meanings. Thus does this servant counsel you, so that you may be among those who listen.

126 As for your statement that He said, “Whatever the office of executor and successor may be, after Me it belongs to him”: by Him who alone possesses might and majesty and who created all things without a model through His exalted Word, if anyone had inhaled a single breeze from the Riḍván of the Bayán, he would not utter such words. Rather, he would flee from those who utter them. You have not yet understood the wondrous resurrection, the wondrous spirit, or the wondrous words. The Cause of the Bayán is sanctified above those things that came before. What you formerly possessed has been rolled up, and another expanse has been spread out by the permission of God, the Exalted, the Most Manifest. Yet you have understood neither its beginning nor its end. Praise be to the Beloved, who has so utterly deprived the malicious that they are now mentioned and seen before Him as more abject than the religious communities of the past. It is as though they have put on a garment made from a single piece of iron, so that they are utterly incapable of movement; so too are their hearts and inmost beings. They have taken a step only to set it in the footprints of the people of the Qur’an.

127 Listen, break down these obstructing barriers, and enter the wondrous, boundless expanse of the Lord of the Day of Judgment, so that you may behold worlds other than your own. Do not be so hard on yourselves. Have mercy on yourselves and on the weak among His servants. You have now clung to the remnants of delusion left by the people of the Qur’an. At times you speak of a fabricated successorship and a falsely claimed executorship; at other times you invent words and attribute them to the Primal Point—may the souls of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him. Hear the call of the Lord, the Exalted, the Most High, who says, addressing the Mirrors:

128 “When the Sun of Bahá shines from the horizon of eternity, present yourselves before the Throne. Beware of sitting in His presence or asking what you have not been permitted to ask. Fear God, O Mirrors, all of you without exception. Ask for the wonders of His grace, so that He may reveal to you whatever He wishes and desires; for all grace on that day circles His Throne and proceeds from Him, if you only knew. Remain silent before the Throne, for silence on that day is better than all that has been created between the heavens and the earth. Do not be among those who were previously given knowledge and, because of it, behaved arrogantly toward God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting. For on that day, above everyone possessed of knowledge, He is the All-Knowing, the Knowing, the Most Learned; above everyone possessed of power, He is the All-Powerful, the Omnipotent, the Mighty; above everyone possessed of greatness, He is the Supremely Great, the Most Majestic, the Great; above everyone possessed of elevation, He is the Supreme Exalter, the Exalted, the Lofty; above everyone possessed of might, He is the All-Mighty, the Supremely Glorious, the Mighty; above everyone possessed of judgment, He is the Supreme Judge, the Ruler, the Wise; above everyone possessed of generosity, He is the All-Bountiful, the Bestower, the Generous; above everyone possessed of command, He is the Supreme Ordainer, the Commander, the Sovereign; and above everyone possessed of grace, He is the All-Gracious, the Bestower of Grace, the Gracious.

129 All of you were created to meet Him and to be present at that most great and mighty gathering. He sends down from the heaven of His bounty what is best for you, and whatever descends from Him will surely make you independent of all the worlds. Today, no one’s knowledge will benefit him, nor anyone’s grace, greatness, power, remembrance, deeds, bowing, prostration, turning toward God, honor, lofty lineage, exalted standing, utterance, light, or name. For all these things, and everything you have known and understood, were created through His saying, ‘Be,’ and it is. If He wished to raise up all contingent beings through a word from Himself, He would assuredly have the power; and beyond even that, He is indeed the All-Powerful, the Omnipotent, the Mighty.

130 Beware, O Mirrors, lest names deceive you on that day. Know that you, all that is above you, and all that is below you were created for that day. Fear God, and do not commit what would grieve His heart and make you among the heedless. He may appear in truth while you lie asleep upon your seats, and His messengers may come to you with Tablets of manifest glory, while you behave arrogantly, pass judgments against Him that you would not pass against yourselves, and say, ‘This is not from God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting.’

131 Glory be to You, O God, my God! You know that I have conveyed Your word and have not fallen short in what You commanded Me. I ask You to protect the people of the Bayán on that day, so that they may neither oppose You nor dispute [corrupted original text]. Preserve them, O my God, through Your power, which You have exercised over all the worlds.”

132 At this point, these words have been deemed sufficient. No one has examined most of the divine utterances revealed concerning this most unassailable, most holy Manifestation. Some have distorted them out of their proper context, concealed others, and changed the outward wording of still others. In truth, however, whatever portion of the Bayán anyone possesses is sufficient to remind him of this most perfect, most great Word. God is a witness to this and knows it.

133 And as for your having written that He said, “The Cause will devolve upon the name of the Unique One, because his appearance is itself a proof and he has no need of My explicit designation”: by God, when you wrote these words, all things wept, the inhabitants of the Most Exalted Assembly lamented, and the foundations of existence, both unseen and visible, were shaken, because, to establish the claim of an enemy of God, you belittled God’s station, greatness, and majesty. By God, the one you have taken as a lord apart from God is in need of everything, whereas the One whom all need appeared in truth in the year sixty and then ascended His throne in truth. Blessed, then, is God, the best of creators.

134 Fear God, and do not wield your pen in pursuit of your own self and desires. Do not disbelieve in the One through a gesture of whose Pen the religions of the worlds were established. The one for whose love you have risen in hypocrisy used to stand before the Presence and have the verses recited to him, and he took pride in this. Yet you have followed him of your own accord and, through him, opposed God, who created all things by a command from Himself. There is no God but Him, the All-Victorious, the All-Subduing, the All-Powerful, the Most Exalted, the Almighty, the Beloved.

135 Of the very person whom you yourselves describe as a mirror—and everyone endowed with sight knows that a mirror has never had, and will never have, any independent reality, its existence having been for the purpose of being dust from the radiances of the Sun—you have asserted that the Primal Point, may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him, said that he is himself a proof and has no need of My explicit designation. By God, your desire lends strength to your tongues, and from it emerges whatever it wishes, not what has been ordained by God, the Almighty, the All-Powerful. You have denied this Manifestation, whose verses and revelations have encompassed the whole world, and demanded an explicit designation, even though the whole Bayán was revealed explicitly and expressly concerning this most wondrous, most unassailable Manifestation. Moreover, this Cause has always been, and will forever remain, sanctified above evidence, allusion, expression, and indication. His manifestation is itself a proof, and His verses are God’s demonstration to all the worlds.

136 If only you had examined the Seven Proofs, which is among the early writings of the Point of the Bayán. As is apparent, you have had, and still have, no purpose other than turning away from the Truth and opposing Him. Even if God were to come to you with every sign, you would not believe in Him. Thus did the Point of the Bayán, may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him, foretell these days. Examine, then, the words of God, that you may be among those who take heed.

137 And as for your having written that He said, “The Cause will devolve upon the name of the Unique One”: by God, besides whom there is no God, you have neither recognized the Unique One nor understood who was intended. Even if we concede that by the Unique One was intended the one meant by the foremost of those who turn away from God, why have you not considered the passage that follows, where He says, “If God should bring forth in your days one like you, this is the one who shall be made heir to the Cause by God, the One, the Unique”? Despite this, has this Manifestation, who has appeared with every divine attribute, not been worth even a single mirror, that you have all [corrupted original text] gathered around Him with the claws of malice and hatred, and at every moment busied yourselves with a new scheme to undermine His Cause? Shame on those who have turned away from all the verses, taken a single letter from the Book, and adduced [corrupted original text] in support of what they possess, and disbelieved in God, at whose command the scrolls of noble glory and the tablets of unassailable holiness were revealed.

138 This is why the Point of the Bayán, may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him, says that the people of the Bayán will not count that Most Great Manifestation even as one of the believers. For if they regarded Him as even a believer, they would not consent to inflict on Him what they would not accept for themselves. God, the Most High, the Most Great, has spoken the truth.

139 Beyond all this, the word of the Truth is a single word: what has been revealed by Him, if you are among those who recognize. He said—and His word is the truth—“Beware, beware, in the days of His manifestation, lest you be veiled by the Váḥid of the Bayán, for that Váḥid was created in His presence. Beware, beware lest you be veiled by the words revealed in the Bayán, for they are His own words in the Temple of His former manifestation.” End of quotation. And

140 Everything they have written, if acknowledged and accepted, has been but a spray from the surging ocean of divine utterance. A hundred thousand souls like these have been, and are being, created by a word. Yet, fabricating a falsehood against God, lying about Him, transgressing against His presence, rebelling against His glory, and vindicating His enemies, you write that His Holiness the Most Exalted said that he has no need of My explicit designation. Through these words, everything that has issued from the Pen throughout the realms of God, the All-Powerful, the Most High, the All-Knowing, has disowned you.

141 Today all the most beautiful names have returned to their own kingdom, and none understands this except those possessed of certitude, knowledge, and insight. Whatever has been revealed in the Bayán, whether laws or descriptions revealed concerning the Letters and the Mirrors, benefits no one unless they acknowledge this Manifestation. Thus has the breath of the All-Merciful breathed within the Bayán itself, if you are among those who recognize. Today, since those who turn away have not been, and are not, capable of recognizing the most exalted likeness, which is God’s proof and demonstration, and are seen to be incapable of grasping the subtleties of the divine words, a lesser analogy is given here, so that receptive souls may enter the garden of inner meanings.

142 A sovereign appoints someone as governor and grants him authority in all affairs, just as some governors who rule regions on behalf of a sovereign are seen to have authority in all affairs. Nevertheless, can the sovereign dismiss them or not? If you deny it, you are a liar, for it is evident that he can. Likewise, after a second sovereign ascends the throne, are the decrees and authority under which the governors exercised their powers on behalf of the former sovereign subject to the sanction of the second sovereign’s rule or not? There is no doubt that all depend upon, and are subject to, the command and permission of the succeeding sovereign. This has been mentioned concerning outward governors and sovereigns so that you may not deny His power to that all-powerful Sovereign who has ever been, and will ever be, manifest in a new state at every moment, who has always done whatever He wills and decreed whatever He desires, and so that you may not regard the hand of God as fettered. At least consider His power equal to that of outward sovereigns.

143 By God, if anyone regarded that Sovereign of eternity as powerful, he would not raise objections such as these against the Creator of names and attributes. You who have no share of the Kawthar of the All-Merciful’s knowledge! Let us concede your assertion that someone has been adorned with the robe of one of the names between earth and heaven: is God not able to strip it away? Woe to you! By God, all existence, unseen and visible, gives you the lie. Did God—exalted be His mention—ever consult anyone when bestowing a gift, that He should consult anyone when taking it away? The Pen is ashamed to mention what you have busied yourselves with in these days before the face of the all-knowing King. Fear God, then abandon what you possess and take what is with God. This is better for you than all that has been created between the heavens and the earths.

144 And as for the distinction you made, saying that the Point of the Bayán, may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him, made this statement for the imitators and the common people, and made another statement for those endowed with sight and understanding: this is a transgression on your part against God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting. This assertion is utterly vain and meaningless. The Truth Himself has appeared with clear verses, and these are indeed a proof from God for every soul, if you are among those who recognize. God’s proof has always been one, and He has appointed those same revealed verses as a proof for everyone. Had there been no sign within creation of the capacity to recognize and understand the verses, He would certainly not have appeared with verses. In this there is indeed wisdom for those endowed with [corrupted original text], and none recognizes it except those endowed with sight.

145 Moreover, it is unclear whom you have regarded as imitators and common people, and whom as those endowed with understanding and sight. You have devised a matter for yourselves and through it have gone astray and acted unjustly, yet you do not know. Know that those endowed with sight and those who are not are identified and distinguished by their acknowledgment or denial. Every soul enabled to say “Yes” in this most unassailable, most holy, most exalted Manifestation is, in God’s sight, among the righteous and those endowed with sight; all others are among the wicked and the ungodly. Of course, those endowed with sight in your estimation are ignorant people who have claimed knowledge and wisdom. Listen to the utterance of the Primal Point and Universal Manifestation, who said, “The wheat-sifter of the land of Ṣád put on the robe of leadership, although he had not studied a single letter of learning, whereas Shaykh Muḥammad Ḥasan, who wrote five hundred thousand verses on acting in accordance with God, gained not a single letter’s benefit that would save him from the fire on the Day of Resurrection.”

146 Likewise, in another passage He explicitly states concerning the meaning of those endowed with sight—His words, exalted be His praise: “At every Manifestation, the pure Truth becomes indistinguishable from all else except to those endowed with sight, who behold the realities of things as they are and always look toward the Sun of Truth and His manifestation. The limitations of the mirrors do not veil them from the One manifest in them. These are truly the God-fearing in the Book of God, before and after; these are the rightly guided.” End of quotation.

147 This is the utterance of the Point of the Bayán, may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him. Now reflect, so that you may recognize those endowed with sight. As you observe, the limitations of the mirrors have never kept those sanctified souls from the shore of the ocean of oneness, and they regard a hundred thousand such mirrors as utter nothingness before the radiant revelation of the Sun of Truth. These are the souls known to God and the Manifestations of His Cause as those endowed with sight, not those who have fashioned a mirror according to their own fancy and through it remained veiled from the unassailable Truth. Woe, then, to those who have neglected what was sent down in the Book, taken a word from it, interpreted it according to the inclinations of their own selves and desires, and used it to object to God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting. If only these present leaders of the Bayán possessed even outward learning! No, by Him in whose hand is my soul! Nothing but lies, slander, turning away from the Truth, and fabrication has ever been found among these souls, nor will it ever be. Thus has God made every matter clear in a lucid Book.

148 You also wrote: “The intention is that He emphasized this matter and stated it explicitly for those who cannot discern the verses. But for those who can discern the verses, He said that he himself is the possessor of verses, and throughout His writings He has thanked and praised the Lord, saying that He has made My successor manifest with verses.” End of quotation.

149 First, most of these words have never come from His Holiness the Most Exalted, may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him. They have fabricated many such statements and continue to do so; you are not acquainted with the details. Second, the verses of the mirrors are spoken of at the level of the mirrors, like the image of the sun in a mirror. That reflection in the mirror is seen and spoken of at the level of the mirror, but in relation to the sun in the sky it has never merited mention, nor will it ever do so. As can be observed, if infinitely many mirrors face the sun, its image appears in them all; when they turn away, all those images vanish, while the sun shines resplendent in its own station with all its radiance. The Primal Point, may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him, has made countless statements on this subject. Blessed, then, are those who behold, and then those who recognize. Anyone endowed with sight and understanding who reflects will perceive his rank and station from his verses.

150 Moreover, in one sense all things have always been and will ever be signs of God, if you would look upon the signs of God within created beings. “How many a sign in the heavens and the earth do they pass by, turning away from it!” The divine signs are visible and present in everything. Yet so long as these things are seen beneath the shadow of the Truth, they are spoken of as signs of the highest heaven; otherwise, they are reckoned as belonging to the nethermost prison.

151 By the Truth, I felt ashamed of your words, for you have spoken words that none of those veiled in the past have ever uttered. The Most Great Ocean is visible, and you have beheld it and continue to behold it; nevertheless, you have busied yourself with establishing the worth of water in a pool whose smell and taste cause distress. The sun is manifest and visible in its radiance and light, yet you have deprived yourself of its light and splendor and adduce proofs for a nonexistent thing that you have neither seen nor perceived. By God, you have not understood a single letter of the Bayán, nor set foot in the realms of exposition, where the divine breezes and the holy fragrances of the Eternal are made manifest. A word has kept you from the Possessor of the Word, and the veil of allusions has deprived you of the breezes of the Lord of Lords.

152 Glory be to God! What a mighty Sovereign, who, despite His complete manifestation and appearance, has prevented the idolaters from beholding His beauty, and despite His consummate loftiness and exaltation, has left the malicious without a share in perceiving the lights of His face. They use verses to establish a proof, yet turn away from the Sovereign of verses. Thus has He ever been powerful over all things, but the people do not see. Hear the call of this evanescent servant and enter the spiritual realm. The Primal Point Himself is manifest, and His lights and revelations have illumined the worlds of the unseen and the seen. You do not look toward Him, but use His words, of which you have never understood a single letter, to establish the legitimacy of His enemies. Bravo for your sight, which has taken the Friend for a stranger and reckoned the wicked among the good!

153 By God, this servant is bewildered—indeed, a whole world is bewildered and astonished. You continually write that the Primal Point said this and that, while at every moment you wound the Point Himself with the swords of hatred and the spears of malice and abuse. You strike and do not realize it. You are like those souls who awaited the Truth by night and day, and after His appearance used His former words to argue for His unbelief and corruption, until at last, in utter injustice, they shed His sacred blood. Truly, God’s curse rests upon those who committed injustice and commit it now, yet do not realize it. We had heard of deluded people, but, by God, we had neither heard nor seen anything like the state you have displayed. We ask God to purify us and you, to gather us upon the path of His love and good pleasure, and not to deprive us of the lights of His radiant, luminous face.

154 You also wrote: “He explicitly stated that his verses are from Me, and I am he and he is Me, and He called him His own fruit. Now judge fairly: was the Báb Himself better able to discern the verses, or are you and I? This is not imitation. And if you say it is imitation, judge fairly for yourself how many verses have appeared and continue to appear from Mírzá Yaḥyá from the beginning of the Cause until now.” End of quotation.

155 Even assuming this statement to be authentic, He has attributed all things to Himself and called Himself by every name, for in everything He has beheld a sign of His own revelation, while His essence remains sanctified above every attribute and His being exalted above every name. Thus, in one passage He says—His words, exalted be His mention: “Say: I have descended until I said, ‘I am indeed an atom, and less than an atom,’ just as I have said, ‘I am a Lord and the nurturer of every possessor of lordship,’” and so forth. It is astonishing that you have not grasped this clear matter. If you possess insight, you will become certain that from the beginning of His manifestation until the time of His ascension He beheld none but Himself, such that His blessed eye fell on nothing but His blessed countenance. This was one of the stations of that Countenance of Eternity, and no one has perceived it except He Himself, the All-Knowing, the All-Wise.

156 Above this station there have ever been limitless stations with God that should not be disclosed. The vessel of divine knowledge has always been, and will ever remain, sealed with the seal of protection. All things, insofar as they are attributed to the Truth, proclaim the call, “I am indeed God; there is no god but Me.” It is abundantly clear that this utterance and this rank do not arise from the thing itself, but are due to its having come to dwell beneath the shadow of the Truth and to be reckoned as belonging to Him. Once that relationship is severed, that same thing is spoken of in God’s sight as belonging to the people of the nethermost prison. Thus the Point of the Bayán, may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him, has said—His words, exalted be His mention: “This chamber, which is without doors and without customary boundaries, is today the loftiest of the chambers of Paradise, for the Tree of Truth is established within it. It is as though all its particles proclaim over every other chamber, ‘I am indeed God; there is no god but Me, the Lord of all things,’ even though those chambers be mirrored seats adorned with gold. But if the Tree of Truth should dwell in such chambers, then those particles of the mirrors would proclaim it, just as the particles of the mirrors of the loftiest seats of the Ṣadrí mansion, where He dwelt during His days in the land of Ṣád, proclaimed it and still proclaim it. No judgment applies to anything except insofar as it refers back to that Person.” End of quotation.

157 Reflect a little on this testimony that the Blessed Beauty Himself has given concerning the particles of dust, so that you may not remain veiled from the Truth Himself by words that stand a hundred thousand degrees below the description of dust. Yet, by the sun of the glory of sanctity, the veiled among this people are seen to be more deeply veiled than all others, to such a degree that they have nearly reached the point where a malicious report would veil them from a hundred thousand visible manifestations of divine power and divinely revealed verses—indeed, would cause them to rise up in rejection and opposition. This is among the promises made by the Author of the Bayán Himself, may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him. Likewise, this most unassailable, most holy Manifestation has foretold in most of His earlier Tablets what will appear hereafter.

158 Although He has affirmed concerning the dust, “There is no god but Him,” He says that no judgment applies to anything except insofar as it refers back to that Person, meaning the Truth Himself. Thus, today, all the attributes in the Bayán refer to the dominion of attributes, and likewise all the most beautiful names revealed in it refer to the kingdom of names. The purpose of all these references was the subsequent Manifestation in another cycle, for He was the beloved of His soul and the desire of His spirit. Anyone who examines the Bayán will see this stated explicitly, not merely implied, and will become certain that the purpose of the entire Bayán has been, and will remain, that Essence of exposition.

159 For example, He sent Tablets to certain people, at the beginning of which was revealed: “This is a Book from God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting, to Him whom God will make manifest. There is no god but Me, the Almighty, the Beloved.” Yet, outwardly, that Tablet was intended for another person. Likewise, in the letter to another was revealed: “This is a Book from God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting, to God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting.” This is why He says that, in its primary reality, what has been revealed in the Bayán is intended for the subsequent Manifestation; at a lesser level, it concerns the relationship people have had to the Tree of Truth. Likewise, He sent Tablets specifically to certain people, at the beginning of which was revealed, “He is you, and you are He,” among them the honored Mullá Muḥammad ʿAlí of Zanján. Furthermore, in speaking of the completion of the degrees of divine unity in the numbers of all things, He says: “There is no god but Him, and there is no god but Me, and there is no god but God, and there is no god but You, and there is no god but He who is, and there is no god but Me, the Lord of all the worlds, and there is no god but You, the Lord of all the worlds.”

160 We hope that through these utterances of the Sovereign of names and attributes you may become somewhat purified of the limitations of self and of the veils and allusions of rejected souls, so that, at the very least, if you do not aid the Truth on the Day of Manifestation, you will not draw a sword against Him or issue a decree for His death. In short, all names and all attributes have been, and are, applied to every thing that is attributed to God, whether at the highest height or the lowest depth—and this height and depth are spoken of with reference to creation—so long as it remains established beneath the shadow of the Truth. Otherwise, it is not even accounted as existing, much less as possessing exalted stations. We ask God to wash you with the heavenly water that has flowed from the finger of grace, to strip from your person the garment of allusions, and to invest you with the adornment of His name, the Most High, the Most Great.

161 As for your statement that He called him His own fruit, say to those who turn away: “You who are heedless of the meanings of the divine words and deprived of the fruit of the divine Tree! First understand what the teaching of the Bayán is, and what the Point of the Bayán—exalted be His grandeur—intends by His words and verses; then undertake your investigation. Do not be among those who have interpreted the divine words according to selfish desires and distorted their meanings.”

162 The teaching of the Point of the Bayán is as follows: He calls the source of the Manifestation the Tree, and then regards all the manifestations of that Revelation as branches, twigs, leaves, and fruits of the Tree of Revelation, as can be seen. For example, the Primal Point Himself—may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—was the Tree of divine Revelation, and all who profess His religion are reckoned as His leaves, branches, twigs, and fruits. Consider all Revelations in the same way, and describe them by whatever name and designation you wish: for example, the Point as the ocean and the others as waves, or as the sun and the others as mirrors. In this station, if He says of any one of the manifestations of that Revelation, “He is Me, and I am he,” this is true, and there is no doubt about it. Thus, concerning the Letters of the Living, some of whom are still alive, and likewise the limitless numbers that culminate in the Váḥid of the Bayán, He says that in all of them nothing is seen but the beauty of that Manifestation.

163 In one station, all things are attributed to the Truth, are from the Truth, subsist through the Truth, appear from the Truth, return to the Truth, look toward the Truth, and move through the Truth. By God, besides whom there is no god! Beyond what you observe, a station lies concealed in the tabernacle of divine meanings. Should the fingers of the power of the Eternal Lord stir the veil of the spiritual tabernacle, and a radiance shine forth from that countenance of hidden meanings, We would swoon, and everyone in the heavens and on earth would swoon. Yet the blind are shut out from the shining lights of the sun of meanings, and the deaf are deprived of the sound of the Pen of the everlasting Sovereign.

164 In short, even if one were to concede these virtues that have been conveyed to you unwittingly and that you have attributed to the tree of rejection, they have applied, and will apply, in one station to all things. Soar from the earth of limitation to the heaven of the glory of detachment, so that you may see with your own eyes what I am describing. Do not imagine that this mortal servant has wasted his life, consumed water and fodder, and, like you, contented himself with fancy and imitation. In any case, it is better to leave these matters aside, for discussion of this station has been, and will remain, beyond the understanding of the ignorant rabble.

165 Let Us return to the realm of your understanding and speak of the contentions of those people. The Primal Point—may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—has regarded the root of the Tree of the Cause, the Most Great Ocean, the Sun of Truth, and the Heaven of Eternity, in every Revelation, as the Manifestation Himself, and all besides Him as the creation of that Revelation, brought into being by a word through the movement of His Pen. Thus He says that if the Manifestation Himself should declare of a tree planted in this visible earth, “This is a proof for all things,” it would assuredly be a proof, just as the proofs He created before, and no difference would be seen between the two. This standing as a proof is due to the radiance within it of the sun of the Word, for it was created by His utterance. Thus a stone has become a place of circumambulation and a sanctuary of worship for His servants. Now consider: if it had not been that stone, and He had pronounced this concerning another stone, it too would have been invested with this same honor. This is the honor of names in the kingdom of names.

166 If only mature souls could be found, so that a drop from the surging ocean of names might be mentioned in this connection! It would then become clear that all things in that sacred court are seen on a single plane, and that distinctions among names are due to the consummate divine wisdom, which has remained hidden from the [corrupted original text] of creation, except those whom their Lord wills. It is better that this station too remain hidden from the veiled, as it has been and still is. Know only this much: these names hold true and this honor endures so long as a thing remains beneath the shadow of the Truth and clings to that name. Once it transgresses and passes beyond the limit, the robe of the name is taken away, and it is spoken of and seen in God’s sight as cast out and rejected. They have remained so far from the paradise of vision and unveiling that whenever they wish to express their fanciful notions, they seize upon and cite a tradition from among the traditions of earlier religious communities.

167 Say: “You who lack insight! Recognize and understand what came before and what comes after through this Cause, for this Cause has not been, and will not be, known through anything but itself. Everything in people’s possession has been, and will remain, unavailing imaginings. Whatever you may say, it is evident and clear to those with insight that such statements have been seized upon out of immaturity. The Truth is manifest and visible. Make Him the standard, do not transgress God’s standard, and weigh all matters by this divine standard. Accept whatever you find to conform to it, whether from before or after; and whatever is found to conflict with it, cast it behind you and stand firm with wondrous steadfastness.”

168 My friend in words alone! Regard the manifestations of the Revelation as the fruit of the Tree of Revelation. These fruits are reckoned as belonging to the Tree so long as they cling to it. Once severed from the Tree, the blowing winds of self and desire alter them so completely that they are deprived of all flavor, delicacy, and freshness. These descriptions have been given in accordance with people’s understanding. Otherwise, I swear by the exalted, all-glorious Beauty—may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—that the Manifestation Himself has never had, and will never have, any relationship, connection, resemblance, or likeness whatsoever to anyone besides Him. These relationships are spoken of in the worlds of names; otherwise, the court of the Manifestation is sanctified above all that has been or is being said.

169 Exalted is He above what you have imagined, recognized, known, said, and now say. No one but the All-Merciful Himself has known, or will ever know, the meanings of the words of the Bayán. If only you had sought shelter beneath the shadow of the Tree of Oneness and been nourished by its fruits, which have appeared in the form of “There is no god but Him”! Then it would have become clear what the Revealer of the Bayán intended by the Bayán. But judgment has been passed upon you through what your own hands have earned.

170 And as for what he has written: “Now judge fairly: did the Báb Himself discern the verses better, or do you and I? And this is not imitation,” and so forth. Grave indeed is the utterance of the one who prompted you, and great are his unbelief and his lack of true existence. They speak without awareness. I swear by the Point of Existence: as these words flow from the pens of those who turn away, all things are both bewildered by their reason and discernment and moved to laughter. For the Primal Point has appeared in His own Being, and the divine verses descend like pouring rain; nevertheless, they have turned away from Him and busied themselves with arguments to establish the claims of another.

171 Had they possessed the eye of insight, they would have seen that the Point of the Bayán is, by God, crying out, lamenting, and mourning because of the oppression of the manifestations of Satan and the sources of vain imaginings. For He has said that all the descriptions revealed in the Bayán refer to the later Revelation. And now it is seen that the Primal Countenance has appeared once more, yet with the words of His previous Revelation they have drawn a sword against His very Self, just as, in the past, souls like these invoked the words of His previous Revelation and shed His sacred blood. Say: You have done, and are now doing, what neither Nimrod, nor Shimr, nor Sinán, nor Ibn Muljam, nor the First, nor the Second ever did.

172 If only, deprived as they are of sight and heart, they at least possessed a sense of smell! By God, they have been deprived of that as well, for they have failed to distinguish a foul stench from the sweet fragrance of eternity, and for the odor of a dung beetle they have deprived themselves of the perfume of the flowers of the Paradise of everlasting glory. Do not regard these words as imaginary. They have become intimate with Siyyid Muḥammad-i-Iṣfahání—whose foul odor, by God, anyone possessing even less than an atom of a sense of smell would detect from many leagues away and shun—and have remained far removed from, and deprived of, the fragrance of the rose garden of divine holiness.

173 I am bewildered as to what I should say to such souls and what I should convey to them. Can one prove by arguments to a dung beetle that the fragrance of a rose garden is delightful? No, by the Self of the Beloved, if you are among those who know! Can one demonstrate to the deaf that the divine melodies are beautiful? No, by the Lord of names and attributes, if you are among those who perceive!

174 He says: “Do not let the Bayán and what has been revealed in it veil you from that Essence of Truth.” Yet, at the prompting of that idolatrous soul, you have written descriptions of the words of those who turn away and sent them to the most holy Seat, and, in objection to God, you have even invoked the words of the Primal Point as evidence. They reason about the Point Himself from His earlier words, without awareness. If they were aware, they would have to hold fast to whatever appears today from the Beauty of Oneness. Say: O sightless one, open your eyes! The Sun of Eternity is seated before your face, and His blessed heart is wounded by your oppression. Look upon Him and have mercy, and do not question Him about anyone besides Him. You have reached the ocean of meaning: leave off distorting words. You have attained the sun: detach yourself from the lamp.

175 The Point of the Bayán says that the entire Bayán today depends upon the confirmation of the Manifestation of Revelation. Nevertheless, they have neglected the decisive words of God and busied themselves with their own desires. By God, they are seen to be like those who ascribe the qualities of an ocean of sweet, refreshing water to a drop of bitter brine. It is astonishing that you have written, “Now judge fairly,” and, “This is not imitation,” when you are so tightly bound in the chains of imitation that, were the hosts of heaven and earth to gather together, they would be powerless to undo them.

176 He has also spoken of fairness. Now, O fair-minded one, judge fairly: has he heard what he has written and attributed to the Ancient Beauty from anyone but Siyyid Muḥammad? Under which religious law is the claimant’s own statement accepted and admitted as testimony? If you produce a ruling from any of the divine laws that have existed among people from a beginning with no beginning, showing that the claimant’s words are admissible, these servants will acknowledge your assertion. On the word of a soul who cannot distinguish right from left or light from darkness, and to whose wickedness, depravity, and malice every atom has borne and continues to bear witness, you have inflicted upon the True One what no oppressor has ever inflicted.

177 Nearly seventy souls were on this journey, and about the same number subsequently arrived at the seat of Revelation. If a single soul should say that anyone opposed these two who associate partners with God, or that any quarrel, dispute, or exchange of words took place between them, We will concede what they have written with the pen of falsehood. So judge fairly within yourself, O servant. Nevertheless, on Siyyid Muḥammad’s word, you have attributed oppression to the True One, although He is free to decree whatever He wishes, and whatever He does has been and will be the action of the True One. But there is no ear to listen and no heart to understand. However often the deaf are told that the True One does whatever He wills and ordains whatever He desires, what benefit can result?

178 By God, the leaves of the Tree of Blessedness have fallen through the oppression of those oppressors, the branches of the Tree beyond which there is no passing have been shattered, the radiant face of the Exalted, the Most High, has been altered by the blows of the insinuations of those embodiments of hatred, His smooth, radiant breast has been wounded, and the hem of His holiness has been soiled by the dust of the slanders of idolatrous souls. I ask the True One for a verse of purification, that He may cleanse the pure and blessed land of His recognition of wicked, unclean, filthy, idolatrous souls.

179 O fair-minded one! A Soul has appeared, manifest with all the signs of power and the revelations of everlasting glory, as everyone has witnessed and continues to witness. If you deny this, inquire among the various peoples. Moreover, He has appeared with the very same verses by which the Primal Point—may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—established His own truth, indeed with greater ones, and He says: “O people! I am indeed the very Self of ʿAlí among you, the Beauty of Muḥammad in your midst, and the Being of the Spirit between the heavens and the earths. O people! Fear God. I am a servant who has believed in God and His verses. I lay asleep upon My couch when the breezes of the All-Merciful passed over Me, and He awakened Me through the truth, taught Me the knowledge of all that has been and all that will be, arrayed Me in the adornment of His own Self, and caused Me to speak His praise, if you but knew.”

180 “O people! If you do not believe, do not assail Me, and do not do what you have been forbidden in all the Tablets. Fear God and do not associate partners with Him. I have believed in the scriptures and books of God that are in your possession, and I affirm what you have been commanded by God, the All-Powerful, the All-Protecting, the Almighty, the Self-Subsisting. Whoever possesses a sense of smell inhales from Me the fragrance of holiness, and whoever possesses a sense of taste, let him taste of the fruits of this Tree what Muḥammad tasted from the Tree beyond which there is no passing, and what ʿAlí tasted from the Tree of the Bayán, if you but knew.”

181 “O people! Fear God. I was as one of you and wished, of My own accord, to remain silent, but the Spirit stirred Me, moved Me through the truth, and caused Me to utter the verses of God and His clear proofs. This is not from Me, but from God, the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the Beloved. By God, if there were in your hearts a love of God smaller than could be reckoned, you would not shed the blood of this Wronged One. For even if He were not what you suppose, He nevertheless ascribes Himself to your Beloved; and, because of your love for this Name, you should not assail Him and do what casts the Spirit of the Most Great face down upon the dust, and with it the realities of all that has been created between the letters káf and nún.”

182 “O people! We have forbidden you what you were forbidden in all the Tablets and commanded you what you were commanded in the Tablets of God, the All-Protecting, the Almighty, the Self-Subsisting. Yet you did not heed My words and overstepped your bounds, until you violated the sanctity of God’s household and committed deeds that raised a cry between the heavens and the earth; but you do not hear. Likewise, We forbade you to violate other people’s maidservants and their wives, and likewise to shed blood. Because of this, you rose against Me in rejection and did what even those who associate partners with God have not done. Were I to be slain at every moment by every sword, I would never sanction your deeds. Thus have I been commanded by God. This is not from Me but from Him, and He is indeed the Truth, the Knower of all that is unseen.”

183 Now consider, O fair-minded one, who is unfair. Adulterous souls have acted treacherously within the very household of everlasting glory, and what other abominable deeds they have committed—by God, besides whom there is no other god, the pen is ashamed to mention them! Nevertheless, you have ascribed right to these souls and attributed oppression to the True One. Are We, then, unfair, and you fair? Read the book Mustayqiẓ, for in that book your guide, Mírzá Yaḥyá, has issued a decree for the blood of all holy souls. Indeed, the soul whom the Primal Point—may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—explicitly named the Third Letter of Him Whom God Shall Make Manifest was martyred by the decree of that oppressor. And read his other book, in which, in numerous places, he has specifically decreed the killing of all souls. Nevertheless, you have attributed it all to the True One.

184 That wicked man who associates partners with God himself submitted a letter to His Most Glorious Presence, which still exists in his own handwriting, and wrote: “Whatever we do is attributed to you, because you are the chief.” In the Great City he told one of the companions face to face: “The fabrications I have uttered so far have been nothing of consequence. From now on I will invent falsehoods and stir up disorders”—as indeed he has done. He specifically said that the Abhá Beauty had sent people to various regions to stir up disorder. You yourself know that everything he said was sheer falsehood, yet you have considered him truthful, even though day and night he taught you to lie, saying, “Say this and write that,” and you have acted, and still act, accordingly.

185 You are not attentive enough to reflect for a moment: “Adrianople is not beyond Mount Qáf. For God’s sake, let me go for a few days and see with my own eyes. The True One and those besides Him are present in that land.” You have not succeeded in doing this and have instead occupied yourself with fabrications. Today, when meeting Him was possible, you did not attain it. In any event, this unfailing bounty will not always be within reach, nor will this divine springtime always be manifest. Soon all will be deprived. This servant asks God not to deprive these servants, in this world or the next, of the shelter of the courtyard of His gate, and that we may always circle around the House, shine with His lights, be nourished by the fruits of His knowledge, and drink from the Kawthar of His good pleasure. We have left the world to those who seek it. From this world and the next we have sought, and will seek, none but the Friend. He is indeed the best of givers and the most gracious of responders.

186 Not only are you not among the fair-minded; you have never even passed through their quarter or set foot there. For had you taken a single step there, you would not have written that this was not blind imitation, when you have never had, and still have no, knowledge of the Bayán. Everything you have mentioned has been mere imitation of what you heard from that wicked, lying mischief-maker, Siyyid Muḥammad. Until now you have been deprived of the fragrances of fairness, but I ask God that you may turn from the left hand of oppression and injustice to the right hand of justice and fairness. This is very difficult, for the honeybee is one kind and the wasp another. Yet nothing has ever been, or is, impossible for divine power; that impossibility exists and is evident in the things themselves.

187 Reflect, so that you may understand. You had mentioned the Primal Point’s discerning judgment. If you looked to that discerning Judge, why did you not obey His statement, repeated throughout the Bayán, that at the time of the Manifestation all must detach themselves from everything and from all that they possess, and look to the Manifestation Himself? For whatever they previously clung to will not suffice on that Day unless they acknowledge Him. So emphatically does He speak that He says a single line of the verses of that Sun of Revelation is greater than everything previously revealed. Nevertheless, you have occupied yourself with establishing the validity of the words of one of His creatures, although you have never had, and still have no, knowledge of that person’s words. Whatever he possessed was a sprinkling from this Most Great Ocean, bestowed upon him and then taken away when he turned from the True One. Those who are informed of all these matters are now present. Do not regard all these servants as irreligious or reckon them faithless. For, by God, we have emigrated for God’s sake and journeyed toward God. It is clear, however, what that wicked mischief-maker has insinuated to you, attributing worldliness to all these servants. Reflect for yourself: where has this worldly gain been, and to whom has it come?

188 If these servants had heard even one manifestly truthful word from those people, it would still have been possible for us to interpret some of their deeds favorably. They are continually occupied with deceit, lies, and slander. When they saw that, by God’s grace, these servants remained firm and steadfast in the True One, they devised a scheme, hoping to discredit these servants by attributing worldliness and false matters to them, and to establish themselves as victims of oppression. By the Point of existence—may the spirit of all in the kingdom of the unseen and the seen be sacrificed for Him—they have committed an injustice that no one has committed from the beginning of creation until now. Indeed, God’s curse rests upon the liars, the slanderers, and the oppressors.

189 As for your mention of the verses of Mírzá Yaḥyá, the Primal Point—may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—says that, from the setting of the Sun of Truth, no one will again hear the verses of God until the next Manifestation. Now that Blessed Beauty Himself likewise says this in person. For the verses of anyone besides Him are attributed to the True One only so long as they point to the divine verses. Their existence is like that of mirrors, which have never had, and do not have, any independent reality. As long as a mirror faces the Sun of everlasting glory, its radiance appears within it; once it turns away, that radiance vanishes. This, too, follows from the statement in which He says that nothing is seen in the manifestations of Revelation but Revelation itself. Thus, in all the manifestations of Revelation, whether in the cycle of the Qur’an, the Gospel, the Psalms, or the Torah, nothing is seen but Revelation itself. Likewise, in the inhabitants of the Bayán nothing is seen but the revelation of the Point of the Bayán, and this is so only while they remain beneath the shelter of the Bayán. Should anyone leave it—God forbid—nothing is seen in him but Satan and his deeds and actions.

190 It is certain that anyone who hesitates in this Revelation is outside the Bayán and deprived of the stronghold of inner meanings, as the Point of the Bayán Himself—may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—has expressly stated. Thus the verses of each of the manifestations of Revelation may truly be called signs only so long as they remain established and abiding beneath the shelter of the Possessor of the Sign. Once they transgress that boundary, they are counted among the people of the fire, and that very transgression is their fire, if you are among those who see.

191 In short, do not mistake shadows and phantoms for the sun, and do not occupy yourselves with distorting the meanings of the divine words, as you have done before. Feel shame before the Beauty of the Point of the Bayán, who is now shining and visible. Do not turn away from Him because of His creatures. His words have been a mercy from Him to His servants. Do not make them your sword and strike His blessed person with them, as the people of the Qur’an, invoking the words of the Messenger of God as their proof, inflicted upon His being what they inflicted.

192 Apart from all these matters, say: “O heedless ones! Have you no hearing or sight? Read the verses yourselves. By God, besides whom there is no other god, all that you have heard cannot compare with a single tablet of wondrous verses. Have you not seen how kindly a fully mature adult treats a child, even praising his actions? Such praise and commendation arise from the utmost kindness. If the same action were performed by a person of understanding, it would certainly be, and remain, blameworthy. A child’s actions are praiseworthy at his own stage, but once he passes beyond that stage, they are blameworthy and unacceptable. Pay close attention to what is being said. Although no awareness has been discerned among the people and no understanding witnessed, if you and those like you fail to understand, servants will be created who will drink the Kawthar of inner meanings from the cup of the divine words and comprehend what is intended.”

193 Most people today are accounted nursing infants in God’s sight, for they drink from the breast of heedlessness, and their time of weaning has not yet come, much less that of maturity. If a mature person is found, he tastes and perceives, in the verses of everlasting glory manifested in this Revelation, the sweetness of the Tasním of divine knowledge and the Salsabíl of everlasting holiness. He sees all besides Him as a mirage on a plain. Say: “Fear God, and [corrupted original text] anyone besides Him in His presence, [corrupted original text] anything other than Him with His own Self, [corrupted original text] concerning Him what is unfitting and unseemly for you. Fear God, O people, and do not follow those who disbelieved in God in His days, disputed His proof, usurped His right, transgressed His justice, and committed oppression while claiming to be oppressed. These are the ones whom every atom curses, if you understand.”

194 You had also written: “The verses of Mírzá Yaḥyá are spread abroad, and he claims the station of truth for himself on the evidence of his own verses, quite apart from the explicit text of the Most Exalted Lord, which is the greatest proof.” Here ends your statement. This claim is fundamentally invalid, for if creatures independently lay claim to verses, they have been, and will be, false; that very claim is proof of their falsity. It is exactly as though the reflection in a mirror were to say, “I am the sun in the sky.” You yourself have written that he is a mirror. Even if we accept your word, there is no doubt that mirrors have existed, and will exist, to receive the imprint of the sun’s radiance. While occupying this station, a claim to the station above it has been, and will be, invalid. The contradiction lies here, not where you have alleged it, for while describing him as a mirror, you have sought to establish for him the station of the sun. If the radiance seen in a mirror claims, “I subsist by myself,” is it truthful? No, by the very Self of God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting!

195 It is also astonishing that you have taken the circulation of imaginary verses that you have not recognized as proof, and cited it as evidence of his truth, even though those verses belong to his own rank. Whatever any creature brings forth is within its own limits and station. Yet you have remained veiled from verses that hold sway over all who are in the heavens and on earth and shine like the sun from the All-Glorious horizon. If circulation is the criterion, these verses have circulated to a degree beyond which nothing can be imagined. Fear God, who created you, and do not mention anyone’s verses in the presence of His verses. If the sun appearing in the images of mirrors could rival the sun shining from the horizon of holiness, then his verses could rival the verses of God, the All-Protecting, the Almighty, the Self-Subsisting. And this is only if it is a true mirror; if it is a mirror like you, it has never had, and will never have, any standing before God. Open your ear, then, to hear from every letter of your Lord’s verses: “There is no God but Me. O all created things! You have been created by My own Self for My own Self. Beware lest you deny Me.” If you purify your sight, you will behold all the words of your Lord in the form of the Spirit, declaring: “There is no God but Me, the All-Powerful, the All-Protecting, the Almighty, the Beloved.” Say: “Ask forgiveness for what you have done, you who are heedless, for what you possess will never be accounted anything before God, and what you have recognized returns to you. Such has been the matter, but you are not among those who perceive.”

196 What affinity do the breezes of autumn have with the breezes of spring, and what resemblance do the breaths of the highest heaven bear to the breaths of the nethermost prison? By Him in whose hand is my soul! Had you possessed insight, a single letter of that Tablet which descended from the heaven of the divine will to complete His proof, and was sent expressly to you, would have sufficed you. Take that very Tablet as the balance against which to weigh what was revealed before, so that you may witness that this has descended from an eternal Revealer. If anything other than the words of God is praised or commended, this is to show favor and encourage souls, and it is spoken of within their limits and according to their station. If the creature could be joined to the Creator and the lamp could rival the sun, then the verses of anyone other than Him could be joined to His verses and rival them.

197 Fear God, and do not spread corruption on earth after it has been set right. Do not be among those who, through mention of the leaf, have become veiled from the tree, its boughs, its branches, its leaves, and its fruits—even if it is a true leaf. Because you have drunk from a turbid spring, you have remained deprived of the sweet, refreshing, flowing spring. Say, “In the name of God and by God,” then raise your hand, take a handful from it, and drink it. By God! Through it your heart will be illumined, your soul will find peace, your inner heart will be cooled, and you will be among those who are certain.

198 As for what you wrote, that he claims the station of truth for himself on the evidence of his own verses, know that this is an act of rebellion by you and by him against God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting. For this rank has belonged, and will belong, exclusively to the Manifestations of divine unity and this wondrous Revelation of the All-Merciful, which has appeared with the verses of God and His clear proofs and has appealed to nothing but revealed verses and manifestations of power, as is recorded throughout the pages of the Bayán. Open your eyes, then, so that you may recognize. This is the station of “O You who have pointed to Your own Essence through Your own Essence,” and likewise the station of “His proof is His verses, and His existence is His evidence,” and likewise “Know God through God.” For it is not fitting for Him to appeal to anything other than Himself as evidence of His truth. He has been, and will be, known through His own Self, while all besides Him are known through Him and glory in their relationship to Him. He is the divine Balance, the Path of unity, the Sun of eternity, the Most Great Ocean, the Most Perfect Word, the Hidden Mystery, the Frequented House, and the Inscribed Book. All are weighed in this Balance, while He is weighed by His own Essence. All are raised up by a breath from Him, while He is raised up by His own Self.

199 Say: “You who are far away! You speak without perceiving and make mention without understanding.” Had even a little awareness been discerned, some of the hidden meanings concealed within the tabernacle of the spiritual knowledge of this Revelation would have been mentioned, so that you might have gained some knowledge of the paradise that has no shade. But it would be a pity to place the pearls of the ocean of divine glory in the hands of thieves, or to unveil the secluded maidens of inner meanings in the presence of oppressors, for they have been, and remain, treacherous in eye and sight, in soul and heart. Thus they have stolen the words of the Most Glorious—may the souls of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—mingled them with their own fabricated words, and circulated them in their own name, heedless that the assayers of divine unity recognize a single pearl among a hundred thousand thousand stones and trace it to its source. For the Tasním of divine holiness does not flow from a limestone quarry, nor does the Salsabíl of divine wisdom stream from a spring of pitch. There are servants who find the hidden sanctuary of the All-Merciful through the fragrance of Riḍván, for that sweet fragrance guides those with the purest sense of smell to the Riḍván of the Most Exalted, the All-Glorious Beauty. Assuredly, keen eyes will appear in the world, and assayers of divine unity will arise. They will purchase the jewels of His words with their lives, renounce all existence and the contingent world in His path, and sacrifice their lives in His way. Through His name they will become detached from the ways of the world; through His remembrance they will have no need to mention the worlds; and through remembering Him they will be freed from remembering anything besides Him.

200 Say: “You who have no sight! If you look toward the Most Great Vision and wander through the Riḍván of the Bayán, you will see nothing but this statement: He has always been His own proof, and through His appearance the proof has been made complete and perfect for all the people of the world. If, out of His grace and mercy, He has brought forth verses, this is a token of His grace bestowed expressly upon His servants. He says: ‘Do not adduce anything as evidence of Him except what proceeds from Him, for evidence appears from Him, proof is established through Him, and demonstration is witnessed from Him, while all besides Him are nonexistent and absent in His holy presence.’”

201 Say: “You who are truly deaf! Have you not heard that He has said: ‘Do not let the Bayán and what was revealed in the Bayán veil you from that Countenance of the All-Merciful, and do not let the Letters of the Bayán cause you to hesitate in His Cause’? For all the Letters and Mirrors have been created by His word, and the whole Bayán has descended from the heaven of His command. It is as though you belong to the imaginary Jábulqá of the people of the Qur’an, and have only just emerged from the forest of delusion to enter another thicket of conjecture. You have never known, and do not now know, a single letter of the Bayán. You are unaware of the ways of those who possess true knowledge and are far indeed from the path of the lovers.”

202 Say: “You who are deluded by the abode of delusion! With such understanding, you have drawn the sword of oppression against the head of Him addressed as ‘But for You,’ imagining that you have attained true knowledge and reached God. Would that the road you travel ended in Turkestan, as it has been said: ‘This road you travel leads to Turkestan.’ But this path has led, and will lead, to the lowest depths of the fires of hell. It is astonishing that you have long been in the vicinity of God, for the Great City has not been so far from this good and blessed land as to make the journey burdensome for those who intend it. Yet not only did you fail to come yourself, but not a single sanctified soul came from you to the Most Holy Court, nor did any gracious message reach the most gracious, most pure abode. Thus you have deprived yourself of that for which you were created and have been among those kept far away.”

203 Finally, this point is mentioned: know that these verses are the very verses with which the Point of the Bayán was sent forth, through which Muḥammad, the Messenger of God, appeared, through which the Spirit spoke, and through which He who conversed with God was established. Now reflect a little on what those who have turned away from this Revelation have committed. In the eyes of those immersed in the ocean of inner meanings and those dwelling in the ark of divine wisdom, souls who turn away from these wondrous divine verses have turned, and will turn, away from all the Prophets and Messengers. They will never perceive the fragrance of the paradise that is the very Person of the Manifestation. They are among the greatest losers, but they do not know. We ask God to grant you the wing of certainty in this manifest Cause, so that you may soar with it to the heaven of glory and holiness and say: “Praise be to God, the Lord of the worlds.”

204 As for your having written, “Quite apart from the explicit text of the Most Exalted Lord,” listen to the call of this servant, and never speak of the explicit text of the Primal Point—may the souls of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him. For, by His bountiful Self and His all-merciful Beauty, you have no knowledge whatsoever of who was intended by the explicit text of the Point of the Bayán or what it means. Neither you nor anyone else knows, for this matter is hidden from the eyes of all, and no one knows its details except two souls. One of them was martyred in the path of God, and the other is still living. The idolaters have deemed him a liar so that they may spread their own ravings among the people.

205 Moreover, as for this explicit text that the one who has turned away from God lays claim to, and which has been mentioned to you, something greater than it was revealed concerning the Name of God, the Judge, Mírzá Asadu’lláh. Consider the utterances of the Point of the Bayán, in which He referred all the divine names to him. The details of this are found in the words that have shone forth from the dawning-place of the finger of the All-Merciful. Likewise, the servant present before the Throne has written certain Tablets at length on these matters. Examine them, so that you may take heed within yourself and rend the veils of imitation through the power of your Lord, the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the All-Praised.

206 And this same person, whom the Point of the Bayán called Dayyán, declaring this to be the greatest of the divine names, you now call base and name the Father of Evils.
