Your leader, Mírzá Yaḥyá, explicitly and without allusion, has issued a sentence of death against that wronged one, as well as other souls, in his own book of perdition, known as the Mustayqiẓ. Go and read it, so that you may learn what has befallen the Manifestations of the Truth. Likewise, the Primal Point wrote to him: “You are indeed [corrupted original text], O third Letter, the believer in Him whom God will make manifest.” This is a station that has taken precedence over all stations, and

207 He then made a statement concerning this station, from the allusions of whose divine words it is understood that he would attain the presence of God in the next Revelation, and that the Temple of Eternity would make Him known to him. Indeed, he came into His presence, and the Tongue of God spoke to him. A brief account of his story is this: when Mírzá Yaḥyá wrote the sentence of death against him, His Holiness the All-Glorious had departed from Iraq, and for nearly two years no one had any news of Him whatsoever. When the decree of death issued from your leader, a certain Mírzá Muḥammad went expressly to Azerbaijan to martyr that wronged one. God thwarted what he intended, so that the words recorded in the Tablet might be fulfilled. The aforementioned person came from Azerbaijan, and all the companions, Arab and Persian alike, rose up to kill him. This was at a time when His Holiness the All-Glorious had returned and was residing in Iraq. Matters became so grave that, one day, from the first light of dawn until nearly the afternoon, the All-Glorious Beauty summoned, one by one, those companions who were intent on harming that helpless man and emphatically forbade them.

208 Two days later, the aforementioned person came into His presence, repudiated the fabrications attributed to him, and swore, “Whatever they have attributed to this servant is sheer falsehood.” The All-Glorious Beauty treated him with the utmost kindness. He attained what had been recorded in the Tablet and returned to his lodging. A few days later, the person already mentioned deceived him, brought him from Káẓimayn, and martyred that helpless man near your leader’s house. By God, at his martyrdom a cloud of dust rose over Iraq, and darkness descended so thickly that day turned to night and lamps were lit in the markets. All the people of Iraq, fearful, distraught, and shaken, cried out, “O God!” Women and men alike called out, “It is because the blood of this wronged one has been shed that this calamity has descended upon Iraq.” Yet the leader did not perceive it and did not awaken from the sleep of heedlessness. By God, they martyred him with such cruelty that the heavens were filled with terror, lamentation, and mourning. Even so, they have not taken heed, and in their fiery tablets they speak contemptuously of the soul whom His Holiness the Most Exalted—may the soul of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—named the Peerless One of the One, and likewise addressed as the Manifestation of Oneness and the Name of God, the Judge. To this day none of the idolaters has recognized him, for the veil of the All-Merciful intervened. Say: O people, fear God and do not shed blood. Turn to Him in repentance, O company of the heedless, that He may expiate your offenses and forgive your misdeeds, over which the inhabitants of the earthly world and the heavenly kingdom, and then the company of the exalted ones, have lamented.

209 Your leader, seeking to belittle that martyr who had no helper, would mention that the Primal Point had written to him and requested that he obtain good paper and pens for Him to write with. Say: Granted that this statement is accepted, this very thing makes it clear that you were under his shadow, for such kindness is shown by fathers toward children. If only he had perceived even this much! No, by God, he will never perceive it within himself, even should God come with every sign, or with a company of the inhabitants of the heavens, or with the hosts of the Spirit, or with the scriptures of the former and latter generations. By God, because of the injustice of those idolatrous souls, the Temples of Oneness are lamenting and mourning, and the Primal Point is sighing and weeping. If only there were someone with ears, eyes, and a heart, that he might witness, hear, and understand! A flame of fire and a blaze of burning heat have enveloped their hearts and their inmost selves, preventing them from attending to their deeds and actions, to what has proceeded from their own selves, and to what comes from their mouths.

210 Say: O heedless and ignorant one, granted that your fabrications are accepted, the explicit statement of the Point of the Bayán throughout the Bayán is that, at the time of the Revelation, you must not allow the Bayán and all that was created within it to veil you from the Revelation itself. Praise be to God! That idolater and those like him have remained deprived of the explicit meaning of God’s firm and incontrovertible words, let alone the allusions contained in the intimations of the Lord of Names and Attributes. Say: Be ashamed before God. Do not soil the hem of God’s steadfast religion with the mire of suppositions and imaginings, and do not conceal the Sun shining from the heaven of Oneness behind the veils of self. Say: By God, this is indeed a Sun that no eclipse has overtaken, and a Moon that will never be eclipsed. Die, then, in your rage and in the fire of your envy, O company of the wicked! You have not yet understood even this much: when the Sun of the One to whom the proof points shines forth, the proof neither nourishes nor avails, for the purpose of the proof is to recognize the very One to whom it points. Open your eyes, then, that you may see it shining from the horizon of might, glory, and majesty.

211 His words—great is his heedlessness: “Then, if another should arise and claim a station after the Point of the Bayán, it cannot be otherwise: either he must claim that he is under the shadow of the Point of the Bayán, while the dispensation of the Point of the Bayán has not yet ended and this dispensation has not been completed. In that case he must show perfect servitude toward the Most Exalted Lord and the utmost obedience to His command, and never depart, even by a barleycorn, from the command of the Most Exalted Lord [corrupted original text], for the dispensation is His dispensation, and the claimant himself stands under the shadow.” End of quotation.

212 Now be a little fair, and open your eyes to see the position you occupy and the rank in which you stand. What difference do you perceive between yourself and the religious communities of the past? The reason those people have remained deprived of the clear, flowing water of the knowledge of divine glory and the heavenly stream of the favors of the everlasting Sovereign is that they have relied upon and clung to proofs they themselves regard as established. With those proofs they have raised objections against the Sovereign of all peoples and have been deprived of the holy waters of Oneness.

213 For example, the people of the Torah have reasoned that no one bringing a religious law will come after Moses, and that whoever does come must promote His law. They have likewise specified signs with which the Manifestation Himself must appear. Thus they were prevented from recognizing the Manifestations of Revelation, for the Manifestation Himself appears as God makes Him appear and as He has willed, not according to what people possess. Likewise, the people of the Gospel hold that Jesus Himself will descend from heaven after the sun has been darkened, the moon has grown dim, and the stars have fallen. Likewise, the people of the Qur’an have never accepted, and do not now accept, that a bearer of a religious law will appear. They say that a person was born a thousand years ago to Narjis Khátún, that he now exists in Jábulqá and Jábulsá, and that he awaits God’s permission to appear. He will come and promote the law of the Messenger of God—that is, this very law now in their possession. They have also mentioned signs in this connection, which would require a book to set out in detail.

214 They have established all these matters to their own satisfaction with such firm arguments, explicit texts, and verified proofs that they have regarded, and still regard, those who deny them as unbelievers, followers of falsehood, and idolaters. When the Suns of the glory of holiness shone forth, their appearance ran contrary to the opinions of these souls, and so all remained veiled and deprived. Nevertheless, you now begin to argue that this Person who has appeared must either be above Him or under His shadow. If He is under His shadow, He must act according to His practices; and if He claims a rank above Him, that is impossible, for the dispensation is still His dispensation and has not been completed.

215 Now be fair: what difference is there between you and this group? Indeed, the difference is beyond measure, for in those people’s books the next Revelation was mentioned through abstruse allusions and veiled, hidden verses, by implication rather than explicit statement. Thus they remained veiled, for they did not comprehend the meanings of the divine words. But you and those like you, even though the Point of the Bayán—may the soul of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—explicitly and without allusion says that you must not allow anything created between heaven and earth, whether words or allusions, to keep you from that Sovereign of existence and King of the visible world, adduce proofs that God may not manifest Himself.

216 A hundred thousand bravos for your understanding and accomplishments, and those of your guides! These grandiose, arrogant words they have instilled in you show that they consider themselves, not God, free to choose. Say: O deluded one, never in any Revelation has God appeared according to people’s imaginings. Rather, He has appeared contrary to what all people possessed, for His most inaccessible, most holy Essence has been sanctified above all conjectures, fancies, allusions, and indications. No one knows the Revelation itself or its manner except He Himself, the All-Knowing, the All-Informed. They have gone so far as to make the Jews and all the veiled peoples of the earth seem blameless by comparison. The Jews considered the hand of God fettered, but they did not go on to prescribe instructions for God, saying that if He makes such a claim it is inadmissible, and if He makes some other claim He must appear in specified, limited ways.

217 Grievous indeed are your words, O heedless one! You have not understood even this much: every former Revelation has been, and will be, brought to completion through the subsequent Revelation. Thus, in this wondrous, holy Revelation of the All-Merciful, in the year nine, holy, purified, stainless souls were brought to completion at that very moment, in the innermost secret. But you neither perceive, nor comprehend, nor recognize. For the sugar of the Egypt of divine knowledge has been the portion of the parrot of inner meanings, not of the raven of darkness. When the one who turns away from God halted and slipped from the path, at that very moment his form emerged from its human garment and appeared, plainly visible, in the skins of beasts. Glory, then, to Him who transforms existence as He pleases and ordains affairs as He wills. No one can repel His command or reverse His will. All things submit to His sovereignty, stand in awe of Him, and tremble before His might. That Lord deserves the acclamation of His unity by the people of the company on high, and the glorification offered by the inhabitants of the cities of eternity. But you appear to be one of those souls who have never intended, and do not intend, to understand any matter or comprehend anything. Their purpose is to establish a position of leadership for their guide, so that they themselves may become known and recognized under his shadow. By God, his shadow is a barren wilderness, and his water bitter salt, if only you understood.

218 Say: O inwardly deaf one, no one has encompassed God’s knowledge, nor will anyone ever encompass it. His knowledge is hidden, treasured, and protected from your scrutiny. If He so wills, He brings the creation of the first and the last to completion in a single instant. Yet you have still not understood completion. If only some capacity for understanding could be seen in you, and this servant, who is one of the attendants at the threshold, could find you receptive and impart words to you through which you might understand certain hidden matters. At least, even if you did not come to understand the completion of the Revelation, you would become aware of your own hatred. Be ashamed before God, and do not prescribe for Him the laws of your own self and desires. He appears as He wills, not as you will.

219 How audacious they have become, prescribing rules and laying down conditions for God, exalted be His remembrance! Now judge fairly: a God who appeared according to the instructions of those who associate partners with Him would be like those idolatrous souls themselves. Whatever you have known and apprehended is a creature like yourselves and is returned to you. By God, the lowliest of His creatures and servants would be ashamed to act according to the practices those idolatrous souls have instilled in you. For they worship the calf without perceiving it, follow Satan without recognizing it, pursue the idol without understanding, and drink the dregs of what the false god has drunk, without knowing.

220 They have not understood even this much: if, in every Revelation, God, exalted be His majesty, had appeared in accordance with the outward practices, conditions, and deeds prevailing among people, no one would ever have turned away or objected. All would have attained the Kawthar of the meanings of the Eternal One and been granted the encounter with God. Those who soar in the atmosphere of the spirit and regard all things with the keen sight of God see a hundred thousand souls like these in the depths of destruction, each clinging to a growing blade of grass. Yet that grass has never had, and will never have, any firmness or stability, and in the end each will drown, although even now, in the eyes of those seated in the all-glorious Ark, they are seen as sheer nothingness, drowned, perished, and nonexistent.

221 Clear your hearing of the cotton of imitation. Hear the word of God from the lowliest of His creatures, and know that God, exalted be His glory, is concealed by no veil and will be confined by no limits. He appears in whatever manner He wills, for He has always been, and will always be, all-powerful. His absolute power has never been, and will never be, taken from Him. Now, if, as you imagine, God were to appear according to the instructions of those who turn away, how would you interpret His power? For your words imply that He must necessarily appear in this manner and is unable to send to earth a Manifestation of His Cause in any way other than what you have written. Evil indeed is what you have imagined and continue to imagine! Your deeds have come to nothing because of what has issued from your mouths, yet you do not perceive this within yourselves and are surely among the heedless.

222 By God, foul, noxious odors from the implications of those idolatrous souls’ words have swept over the world and its inhabitants. We ask God to send a mighty wind of grace and dispel these winds. Those idolaters themselves argue that the fifty thousand years of the Day of Resurrection elapsed in a single hour. Say: O sightless ones, the same meaning applies here. You have nothing to say if fifty thousand years elapse in an hour, but if two thousand years, as you imagine them, elapse in a few years, you object. By God, all things give you the lie, and everything created between the heavens and the earths laughs at how little understanding and knowledge you possess.

223 Listen to this servant, tear through the veils of vain imaginings, and look to God Himself. For all besides Him are His creatures, made manifest by a word from Him. He has never been known through anyone but Himself; rather, all besides Him have been, and will be, known through Him. Keep your eyes on the Revelation itself and on whatever appears from Him, and thank the Lord for suddenly revealing the Manifestation of His own Self, as the Point of the Bayán says: “The noon has indeed drawn near, while you lie asleep.” The Sun shines and sheds its light at the zenith of noon. This is an occasion for gratitude, not complaint. Do not regard mercy as affliction or count blessing as wrath, although the appearance of the Manifestations of oneness has always been, and will always be, a blessing for the righteous and an affliction for the wicked. The wicked and evil have always taken blessing for affliction and regarded pure grace as wrath.

224 You had spoken of the completion of the dispensation. Listen to the words of the Revealer of the Bayán—may the soul of whoever dwells in the realities of existence be sacrificed for Him—who states in an explicit text, exalted be His majesty: “From the beginning of that Cause until before nine is completed, the essential realities of creation have not appeared. Indeed, all that you have seen is from the seed until We clothed it in flesh. Then wait until you witness the other creation. Then say: ‘Blessed be God, the best of creators!’ And bear witness that the difference between the Qá’im and the Qayyúm is the number nine. That is when the essential realities will be completed in their stations. That is the difference between every ‘most great’ and ‘great.’” And in another passage: “You shall surely watch for the difference between the Qá’im and the Qayyúm. Then, in the year nine, you will attain all good.” And in this connection it was revealed: “This is what We promised you before, at the time when We answered you: ‘Wait until nine has elapsed from the Bayán. Then say: Blessed be God, the best of originators!’”

225 O you who, in your own estimation, possess sight and fairness! Open your eyes and behold the other creation from the Revelation of the year nine until now, and proclaim: “Blessed be God, the best of creators!” and “Blessed be God, the best of originators!” Observe how explicitly, without allusion, He speaks of the completion of creation and the greater greatness of the subsequent Revelation in the difference between Qá’im and Qayyúm, and likewise between “most great” and “great.” When the period elapsed and God’s appointed time reached its end, the Beauty of the year Nine appeared with manifest authority. Then all who are in the heavens and on earth were seized with terror, except those who came to God with a sound heart. Fear God, O people, and do not leave the Qayyúm forsaken among you. Stand in awe of God and be among the God-fearing. Beware of distorting the words of God from their proper places, for that is a grievous error in the preserved Tablets of glory.

226 But the seed cannot comprehend the station of maturity, just as today no one has understood the divine utterances except those whom your Lord has willed. Yet they will interpret these words according to their own vain conjectures and distort them, as the Primal Point, exalted be His grandeur, has foretold.

227 Now, setting these matters aside, I will mention something, so that perhaps you may become aware of it and come to your senses. Suppose someone states that a tree will appear, sometime between now and the end of two thousand years, adorned with delicate, fragrant crimson blossoms, in the fullness of freshness and verdure, with perfume and pleasing fragrances, and expressly directs you to look to that tree itself and its appearance, and to nothing else. Then, one morning, you rise from sleep and suddenly see that this good and blessed tree has appeared with delicate, sublime crimson blossoms. What will you do now? Will you deny what you see with your eyes and perceive by smell, or will you become certain of what you have seen? No, by God! You and those like you are seen to be among those who would batter those visible, manifest blossoms with the stones of conjecture and desire, saying: “This has borne fruit too soon. These blossoms should appear two thousand years from now and be seen upon this tree.” Shame on you, O company of the deluded!

228 Yet, according to the explicit text of the Revealer of the Bayán, the subsequent Revelation has never been confined to any particular time, as He expressly states: “If He wishes to appear at this very moment, He is free to do so, for no one but God has ever known or now knows the time of His appearance.” And throughout the Bayán He states that it depends upon the will and wish of that Sovereign of Oneness Himself: “He manifests Himself however He wishes and desires. His verses alone are the proof, and His existence rests upon His own Self, since all others are known through Him, while He is not known through anything besides Him. Exalted is God above what you describe.” And He likewise states: “Since no one but God knows of the Revelation, whenever it occurs, all must acknowledge the Point of Truth and offer thanks to God.” End of quotation.

229 Now consider how explicitly He states that whenever He appears, they must acknowledge Him. Nevertheless, instead of offering thanks to God as He instructed, they have begun to complain rather than give thanks, and instead of acknowledging Him, they have assailed with arrows of malice and the sword of hatred the One around whom circle the spirits of the Prophets and Messengers. Say: O deprived one! Consider whether the known Sovereign has made that hidden, inevitable Revelation subject to any condition. Produce it! No, by Him who created all contingent things through a word from Himself! He has never made this Revelation dependent upon or subject to any matter or condition, for in that court conditions are utterly nonexistent, and no one but His own Self encompasses or knows Him.

230 He states that His verses are the proof of Him, and His appearance and existence establish His own Self. Nevertheless, they have confined the unassailable Truth within fanciful words and unworthy allusions. Woe to them and for what their hands have earned! Do not distort or interpret the words of God according to your own desires, for no one can attain their intended meaning. Thus, concerning outward ordinances, He instructed the leaders of the Bayán to ask Áqá Siyyid Ḥusayn, upon him be the glory of God, who was in the presence of the Throne. He states: “For you do not know what God intends.” Nevertheless, you have regarded one who turns away from God as the very Self of God.

231 Furthermore, “the Seal of the Prophets” is among the unequivocal verses of the Book of the Lord of the worlds. If anyone understood its meaning over the course of one thousand two hundred and seventy years, then now, too, someone other than God can comprehend the meanings of the revealed words of the Bayán. Reflect deeply on what is being stated and flows from the Pen by God’s permission, so that perhaps, through the scratching of this feeble one’s pen, you may attain the Ancient Beauty. He indeed guides whomever He wills to a straight path.

232 Moreover, it is not clear what you mean by completion. If you mean the completion of the divine verses, they are complete at the moment of revelation, and imperfection has never entered and will never enter that realm. Whatever has descended from Him was complete before and will be complete afterward and at this very moment. If you mean the perfecting of souls, souls have been seen to become increasingly imperfect the more time passed. Thus, at the end of the dispensation of the Qur’an, which according to your belief was the height of perfection, the utmost perfection of the leaders of that age consisted in rejecting all the Tablets of God and inflicting upon the divine Messengers what you have heard. Or does completion mean the perfecting of outward deeds? If so, those very deeds have been seen to become a veil for most people. Thus it was observed that when those who practiced the ordinances of the Qur’an saw that the appearance of the Most Great Luminary was contrary to their practices, they issued a decree for His death and shed His pure blood. Likewise did the people of the Gospel, and likewise the people of the Torah, and so on through the Revelations, back to the First Originator.

233 Know that the perfection of all things has depended upon the word and confirmation of the Point of Truth. Listen to what the Point of the Bayán—may the spirit of all besides Him be His sacrifice—says, so that perhaps, through the heavenly stream of the All-Merciful’s utterances, you may be cleansed of the dark dust of the allusions of the manifestations of Satan. Although it is certain that the words of God have had and will have no effect upon you, in most of His utterances God’s gaze has been directed toward the hearts of the sincere among His servants, for those sanctified and detached souls inhale the musk-laden fragrances of meaning from many leagues away, and through the words of God detach themselves from the words of all the worlds. His words, blessed be His mention, are: “For if He were to make all that is on earth a prophet, they would assuredly be [corrupted original text] in the sight of God.” From this utterance of the All-Merciful, comprehend the reality of perfection, so that perhaps you may be awakened, may not offer the potsherds of allusions as a gift to the ocean of the pearls of divine meanings, and may become certain that all things proceed from God, return to God, and are established and realized through His confirmation.

234 Reflect a little. Although the highest degree of perfection for human beings is the rank of prophethood, and most have considered it impossible for any soul to attain this lofty and exalted station, He nevertheless states that if that Most Great Revelation should will it, He would cause all who are on earth to attain this rank, which everyone regards as the most perfect of stations. By His life! For such a Lord we should sacrifice our selves and our souls, and then our very beings and realities. Yet this is little in the face of the manifestation of His gifts and the emergence of His bestowals, which have encompassed both humankind and jinn.

235 If a soul should live as long as the earthly dominion and the heavenly kingdom endure, and perform every prescribed deed, yet hesitate for less than an instant at the Revelation, all the deeds he has performed over that immeasurable time become dust. Indeed, his very existence and being become nothing, let alone his perfection. Likewise, if throughout this period he has—God forbid—neglected every deed, but at the moment of Revelation attains the declaration “Yes,” all deeds are recorded to his credit, and he has been and will be perfect in the sight of God.

236 No one but God knows when a dispensation is complete. Completion may appear in less than an instant, or it may not occur in a thousand years or more. It is abundantly clear that the completion of every thing depends upon God’s confirmation, not upon the length of time or the emergence of people’s imaginings. The subsequent Revelation is itself the proof of the completion of what appeared in the preceding Revelation. Look to God, not to the length of the period or the passage of time, for God is able to bring all ages to an end in a single instant, or to extend one instant over a hundred thousand years. His are creation and command; all act at His command.

237 At this point, a few couplets revealed in Iraq by the most wondrous, most exalted Tongue are cited for your awakening.

238 A questioner said to a mystic: “When did you come to know the divine mysteries? O you who are drunk with the wine of grace, do you remember anything of the day of ‘Am I not?’” He said: “I remember that voice and speech, which was [corrupted original text], and this is no wonder. His voice is ever in my ear, that spirit- and soul-nourishing call of His.” Another mystic, who had ascended higher and had pierced the pearl of the divine mysteries, said: “That day of God has not ended. We are in that day; it has not been cut short.”

239 These words were cited specifically to make it clear that comprehending these matters depends upon the insight of the mystics. Not every soul can comprehend or become aware of these degrees. It is strange that you consider yourself a mystic, yet do not even know the words of the mystics; otherwise, you would not have raised an objection here. For the mystics hold that the divine names are effective at every moment, since their suspension is inadmissible. Thus they have said that the name “the Giver of Death” returns all beings at every moment from relative existence to relative nonexistence, and likewise the names “the Giver of Life” and “the Raiser” bring all created things at every instant from relative nonexistence into relative existence. It is in this sense that they have interpreted the blessed verse: “Rather, they are in confusion concerning a new creation.”

240 In this case, the subsequent creation did not exist at the preceding moment, and likewise, at the preceding moment, the creation existing at the subsequent moment was not known. How, then, have they conceived of the perfection of any single thing? For the thing that exists at this moment does not exist at the next, since this thing undergoes renewal and origination. How can perfection be attained? If you say that it takes place at once, in that very moment of origination, this long duration was not and is not necessary. If people were capable of comprehending the meanings of the divine words, a detailed explanation would certainly be given here, so that they might detach themselves from all knowledge, past and future, and turn toward the waters of wondrous new knowledge that today flow from the right hand of the throne of the All-Merciful.

241 Listen to the words of this transient one, and do not confuse the matter for yourself. No one has comprehended or will comprehend the divine words, for the key to their gates has been in the hand of the Almighty, the All-Powerful. Until the hand of power breaks the seal on the vessel of divine knowledge, no one has gained or will gain access to it; indeed, they have not even caught, nor will they catch, its fragrance. Likewise, until the hand of power of the preceding Revelation broke the seal on the treasuries of the knowledge of the Qur’an, no one comprehended them. In short, perfection has been and will remain in the hand of God’s power.

242 The perfection of each cycle has come through the subsequent Revelation, and true perfection occurs at the moment of revelation. Yet the likes of those feeble souls have not had and do not have the capacity to comprehend it, just as that passage of fifty thousand years in a single hour has not been comprehended. If anyone has claimed to do so, it has been by imitation, except those whom God has taught from the gems of His knowledge. Much divine learning remains deposited in the heaven of God’s innate knowledge. It remains to be seen at what time and season holy Manifestations and exalted Daysprings will appear from the horizon of contingent existence, and receptivity to understanding will arise, and it will descend from the heaven of the divine will in the measure ordained. The command is in His hand. He does whatever He wills and is not questioned about what He has willed.

243 As for your statement that he must not deviate from the words of the Exalted Lord by so much as a barleycorn, it is unclear whether this is your decree that must be obeyed or the decree of the Point of the Bayán. The Point of the Bayán states that His action is the standard for all, and that whatever that blessed Being does, everyone must do. It seems you have never seen the Bayán at all and have no knowledge of it. After all, you speak at Satan’s instruction and move at his prompting.

244 O you who are known for spiritual knowledge and consider yourself a mystic! Show some sign of an independent existence, and do not make yourself like sheep, to be led by any shepherd in whatever direction he wishes. If only the shepherd were human! You have found a wolf for a shepherd, and at the very first meeting he swallowed all your spiritual knowledge and certitude and the fruits of your belief in divine unity. Now you appear as a dead body, a thing of nothing. Indeed, you are seen to be so dead and insubstantial that even a hundred thousand trumpet blasts would not stir you or bring you to move. By the one true God who is worshipped, my heart burns for you and those like you, for malice and hatred have never been and are not in these hearts. Whatever has been and is being said has been and will be solely for the sake of God.

245 Furthermore, the All-Glorious Countenance—may the soul of all else be sacrificed for Him—observes every prescribed deed, if you are among those who see clearly. It has been [corrupted original text] that this servant has been in His company night and day. By God, besides whom there is no god, nothing even slightly objectionable has been seen from this Blessed Beauty. All things have testified and continue to testify to the sanctity of His holy Being. Throughout the nights and days, nothing has been heard from that pure and holy house except the remembrance of God and the exaltation of His Word. You yourself, if you show a little fairness, will testify that He is sanctified beyond the imaginings of all others and exalted beyond the comprehension of all besides Him.

246 What deed is greater than offering one’s life in the path of the All-Merciful? Have you not heard what He has suffered for twenty years in the path of God? That wicked slanderer has indeed attributed his own deeds and those of his guide to the most pure, most holy Beauty, and you have accepted them from him without evidence or proof. But no, by God! The words of that man and his like cannot stain His pure robe.

247 As for your statement that the claimant has fallen under the shadow: although nothing but pure servitude has been heard from the most holy, most pure Beauty, this utterance has been and will remain utterly idle. By this reasoning, His Holiness the Exalted One—may the soul of all else be sacrificed for Him—was under the shadow of the religious scholars of His age, since He appeared in that age. If they deny that He was under the scholars’ shadow, then He was under the shadow of the Messenger of God. How low their station, how little their reason, how meager their understanding, and how great their claim! Yet I testify that before His Revelation He was under the shadow of a Prophet, and at the moment of His Revelation He was above every bearer of a divine message.

248 His words—may his shadow grow: “If such a person and his companions then claim, in speech and writing, that he has attained superiority over the Exalted Lord and is superior to Him, what a contradiction and explicit conflict with the original claim! For a great person, or indeed any person possessed of understanding, to make two contradictory claims is reprehensible. No rational person makes two explicitly contradictory claims, especially if he claims that ‘the Exalted Lord was created by a single word of mine.’ God save us! What claims a mere partial revelation of great personages to certain people as a test brings forth! And a number of ordinary people believe them and assent without reflection.” End of quotation.

249 Say to those who associate partners with God: “If you would listen to Me, then weep for yourselves and lament the paucity of your reason and understanding.” Descriptions of the likes of those who turn away have been revealed in the former and latter Books. His words, exalted is He: “Like a donkey carrying books.” Say: Your own existence is the contradiction. Remove it, so that you may behold nothing but pure unity and absolute oneness. If you wish to understand this claim and comprehend what He has said, so that you may perhaps reach the source of the ever-flowing waters of everlasting holiness, these are His words, exalted be His majesty: “O people! I am indeed the Beauty of ‘Alí among you, the very Self of Muhammad in your midst, and the Being of the Spirit between the heavens and the earth, if you only knew.” This is precisely the same truth that the Point of the Bayán expressed. That intention has been and will remain this very intention, and that will has been and will remain this very will; and this will and intention have been and will remain precisely that same will and intention. He has spoken of contradiction without understanding, for in truth he has established this contradiction with respect to all the Prophets. Every subsequent Prophet was under the shadow of the preceding Prophet; thus, with this relationship of being under the shadow established, His claim to superiority is—God forbid!—according to the words of those who associate partners with God, a contradiction and devoid of validity. Far be it, far be it!

250 Say: “O you who sleep on the bed of desires and suckle at the breast of heedlessness, ignorance, and passion!” Contradiction has never had and will never have access to the most holy court of the sacred Manifestations of God. This contradiction lies within creation itself, not in the Cause of God. Accept the words of this servant and free yourself from the burden of carrying these allusions and words, so that, unburdened, you may fly in the atmosphere of spiritual holiness and enter the abode sanctified from human limitations. Then you will find that court sanctified beyond any mention of contradiction, opposites, transformation, difference, conflict, conversation, disputation, allusion, or indication. For that most pure abode was not created from opposing constituents, nor did it come into being from the four differing elements. It has appeared from the element of spirit. Its air is fire itself, its fire is air itself, and its water is pure earth. All are united, without change or difference. Its moisture is dryness itself, its dryness is cold itself, and its cold is the very reality of heat. From this heat the motion of all that exists was created. Were it not for heat, there would be no motion. Heat is the agent, while motion is the action, but

251 The likes of those souls who have been created from the clay of greed, the fire of pride, the air of self, and the water of heedlessness certainly cannot draw near to that most holy, most pure abode, which has been and will remain sanctified beyond the knowledge of all who dwell in the realm of creation. Should the thought of drawing near enter their minds, they would burn up at once. Whatever was said in the former Manifestations is stated in this most wondrous, most exalted Revelation, identically, word for word and letter for letter. Contradiction and opposites revert to those of their kind and their like, while unity and harmony remain for the loved ones of God, in the beginning and at the return. O fair-minded one! Do not assume, on your own assertion, a relationship of being under the shadow, and do not regard what appears from the All-Glorious Beauty as a contradiction.

252 They had also written: “Especially if he claims that the Most Exalted Lord was created from a single letter of mine.” Fabricated, idolatrous words of this kind, which that idolatrous soul has instilled in you, have issued from wicked, idolatrous souls in every age when the Manifestations of God have appeared. They have never been peculiar to this wondrous Revelation. Thus, at the appearance of the Point of the Bayān—may the souls of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—they accused that goodly divine Tree of falsehood, fabrication, idolatry, and unbelief, as everyone has heard and knows. It is therefore no wonder if such words issue from the likes of those who turn away, for this has always been, and will remain, the way of that rejected one and his like. By God, words such as these have increased, and will increase, the certainty of everyone with insight and their acknowledgment of the True One, for such things have always been, and will remain, among the established ways of the Cause, as recorded in all the Books, both earlier and later: “Every nation plotted against its messenger to seize him, and they disputed with falsehood to refute the truth thereby. So I seized them, and how was My punishment!” And elsewhere: “No messenger comes to them without their mocking him.” Thus, whatever objections of this kind are voiced, the truth of the divine words recorded in His Books has become, and continues to become, apparent to those who are certain and discerning.

253 Say: O you who lack insight! Whatever wondrous and exalted utterances have been sent down, the True One has sent them down. Have you not heard what has descended from the heaven of oneness? His words—glorious is His majesty and mighty His exaltation—are: “O people! I was asleep in the house and silent in remembrance when the breezes of God blew over Me and quickened Me through the truth. He caused Me to speak in praise of Himself and made Me a guidance and a reminder to the worlds. Whenever I wish to remain silent, the Holy Spirit causes Me to speak the truth, the Most Great Spirit stirs Me, and the Spirit of Eternity moves the Pen of Bahāʾ, if you are among those who understand.”

254 O people! Fear God, and be ashamed before His Beauty. Do not speak words for which every atom curses you, and beyond them the Tongue of God, the King, the Truthful, the Trustworthy. This is not from Me but from Him, if only you perceived. By God, if the matter were in My hands, I would have concealed Myself from your eyes and would not have cast My heart beneath the claws of the wolves of the earth. God is witness to what I say.

255 Beware, O people, lest self and desire prevent you from ascending to the highest abode. Look with the eye of fairness upon the proofs of the Prophets and Messengers. By God, this is indeed the Youth of the All-Merciful, whom God has manifested among the concourse of created beings, chosen from among His creatures, and revealed among the worlds adorned with His own Self. At every moment He addresses the company of the Jews, saying: “O company of obstinacy! By God, the Promised One has come to you, and this is indeed the Spirit. If you wish to crucify Him, do whatever you please, and do not delay.” Then He addresses the people of the Gospel and says: “If you wish to dispute with Muḥammad, the Messenger of God, this is indeed Muḥammad among you. Do whatever you desire, for He has offered up His soul in the path of God, the All-Protecting, the Almighty, the All-Powerful.”

256 Then He addresses the people of the Qurʾān and says: “O company of tyranny! If you wish to suspend in the air the Manifestation of My Self who was named ʿAlī, by God, this is indeed ʿAlī standing before your faces, O company of wolves, O band of swine! Do to Him whatever you please, and do not delay for even a moment, for He has taken neither helper nor supporter for Himself. God has truly taught Him what has befallen Him and what will befall Him, O armies of devils!”

257 Then He addresses the people of the Bayān and says: “O company of tyranny and unbelief! The Promised One, who was promised to you in all the Tablets, has come to you. By His Beauty, He has appeared in truth and has not safeguarded Himself in the path of God, the King, the Most Exalted, the Almighty, the All-Praised. He has been in the presence of His enemies every night and day and has aided the Cause of His Lord through His own Self, the Most Exalted, the Almighty, the All-Beautiful. What has befallen Him cannot be expressed in words, and no one knows it except God, who raised Him up in truth and sent Him to the worlds. If you wish to kill Him as you killed Him before, do whatever you please, for He awaits what He was promised in all the Tablets, if you are among those who understand.”

258 Then ʿAlī, from the right hand of the Throne, addresses the people of the Bayān and says: “O servants! Did We not give you the glad tidings of this Revelation in all the Tablets? Did I not take from you the covenant of His very Self in the primordial realm of the Bayān? Be ashamed, then, before the Beauty of the All-Merciful, and do not act in a way that causes the lamentation of the people of the Concourse on high to rise. Fear God, and do not be among the wrongdoers.”

259 By God, I sacrificed Myself out of longing to meet Him and a desire for union with Him. I am with Him at all times and weep over what He has suffered at your hands, O company of wrongdoers! Alas, alas! Despite My counsels, you have done what no one has done to another. At this the Manifestations of the Prophets and Messengers have wept and continue to weep. By God, O people, I placed all matters in His grasp. Throughout My days I moved only to remember and praise Him, spoke only His name, and commanded the servants only to enter His mighty, impregnable shelter. If only you had acted as the Jews acted toward the Spirit, done what the people of the Gospel did to Muḥammad, the Messenger of God, and committed what the people of the Qurʾān committed when I appeared in truth with manifest authority!

260 No, by My own Self! You have not been content with what those people did, and you do what the very realities of all religions seek refuge from, if only you perceived. Will you kill the One in whose path My blood was shed? By God, after it was shed upon the earth, it was inscribed with His name, the Most Exalted, the Almighty, the Wondrous. O people! Be ashamed before God. Do not refute the truth with what you possess, and do not commit what will deprive you of the breezes of God, the Almighty, the All-Wise. Listen to My words, then return to Him; repent and seek forgiveness, so that He may forgive you through the wonders of His mercy and cleanse you of the stain of disobedience and the defilement of your own selves, O company of the heedless!

261 By God, O people, I revealed in the Tablets what would befall Him, and what was inscribed by the Most Exalted Pen upon the Tablets of securely guarded glory will soon appear. Then the tribes of the cities on high will lament, while you rejoice within yourselves and trifle with the verses of God, after We gave you glad tidings through them in the pages of impregnable glory. O people! He is My spirit, and I am His spirit, and My will has always been His will. Do not distinguish between Me and Him. Whatever proceeds from Him is beloved of My heart and inmost being, if you are among those who affirm the divine unity.

262 This servant has cited these wondrous divine words, which have descended from the cloud of the outpouring of the All-Merciful’s mercy, so that the heedless servants may perhaps become aware and not judge by the measure of their own selves. May they at least realize that those who turn away from these wondrous divine verses have opposed, and will continue to oppose and wage war against, the True One, for it is the True One who sends down the verses. Now, if they are at war with the Lord of the worlds, that is their own affair. Today there is no escape for any soul, nor any evident refuge: either they must acknowledge what has appeared, or they must disbelieve in all the scriptures, books, and sacred writings. This is the word of truth, and beyond the truth there is nothing but plain and manifest error.

263 Those who turn away are like the souls who, at the appearance of the Primal Point, turned away from God and objected to Him. Some said, “This Siyyid has claimed spiritual authority”; others, that He had claimed prophethood; and others, that He had claimed lordship. Each of those malicious people voiced a different word, yet they did not understand even this much: that one must determine who sends down the verses, and that the One who sends them down can be known only through the verse itself. Lost indeed are those who have disbelieved, associated partners with God, and rebelled against God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting.

264 You had also mentioned revelation through divine radiance. By God, neither that soul who instilled these ideas nor his guide has understood either the radiance or the One who sheds it, for had they understood, they would not have objected to the True One. It would have been fitting for you to direct these words at the one who turns away from God, for, clothed in a single one of the names, he has exalted himself against the True One and disbelieved in the very One in whom he professes faith. He reads His verses and strikes with his sword at His being. He acts without perceiving and speaks without knowing. He and his like are on an equal footing before God, except that he is more wicked and more unjust. May God protect us and His loved ones from his evil and theirs. He is indeed the Preserver, the Ruler, the All-Knowing, the All-Informed.

265 You had also spoken of the common people, saying that they assent without forming a conception. Now judge fairly: for six years this servant has been present, day and night, in His service, and has associated, and continues to associate, with each of them. I speak of what I have seen with my own eyes, while you rely solely on the word of a wicked, impious troublemaker—the sky has never cast its shadow over a troublemaker like him. Now, is it these servants who have affirmed the True One without forming a conception, or you? By God, even donkeys laugh at the intelligence and discernment of that idolatrous soul who has instilled these words in you, let alone human beings. Nevertheless, you have regarded these servants as common people and stated that we have affirmed the True One without forming a conception.

266 Surely you consider yourself learned. First, how did you come to know this, since you have not attained the presence of the All-Glorious Beauty? No fair-minded person has ever committed such an act: without seeing Him or attaining His presence, merely on the word of the most wicked and corrupt of people, heedlessly taking up the pen, writing whatever flows from it, and sending it to the cities. If only you had acted even according to what the students in the seminaries say! For among them assent depends on three conceptions: first, the subject; second, the predicate; and third, the relation of judgment. Only then is assent established and brought into effect. What was the subject you understood, and what was the predicate? By God, you have committed what none of the ignorant has committed.

267 Yes, these servants have affirmed the Manifestations of God in the past, and through God’s grace and favor they will do so in the future. God willing, through His care and generosity, may no veil conceal Him from us and no hindrance hold us back. By God, whatever you have heard from that wicked unbeliever has been sheer falsehood and pure fabrication, and you do not know with whose breath you have become familiar. By God, the True One, hell flees from his breath, and the fire of punishment seeks refuge in God from his face and his presence. By His truth, these servants have not heard a single truthful word from these two souls, and you do not know what they have done. They have perpetrated every injustice and then, with the aim of casting doubts into people’s hearts and concealing their own vile and reprehensible deeds, have attributed to the True One more fabrications than can be counted. I hope that they see no good in this world or the next, and that they suffer punishment in this world before the punishment of the next.

268 In any case, a word is spoken for God’s sake, so that perhaps you may be cleansed and sanctified from your conceptions and assents, your self-evident truths and theoretical judgments, and reach the water’s edge of assent, which is the very essence and reality of conception. The Primal Point—exalted be His grandeur—says: “Before He makes Himself known to you, enter the fire of love.” He says that before that Ancient Beauty makes Himself known, you should enter the fire of His love. For many years these servants heard all people speak of Him and praise Him, until we reached the water’s edge of nearness and at every moment witnessed what we had neither seen nor heard from anyone else. Apart from all these things, the verses of God descend at every moment like spring rain. How, then, can hesitation be permissible, when the Point of the Bayán explicitly states throughout the Bayán that you must exert your utmost effort not to hesitate even for the time it takes to say “Yes,” for during that very interval you will be in the fire, and all your deeds will come to nothing?

269 This servant was deeply ashamed by these words of yours. You yourself would have affirmed this servant, but a veil intervened. We ask God to lift it through His power, so that you may witness the sun of your Lord’s countenance rising, radiant and luminous, among the worlds. By God, it is bewilderment upon bewilderment: what has held you back, so that you have set yourself to violating the sanctity of the True One and have risen with all your might to dispute and wage war? By God, the sword of your rejection is sharper than a sword of iron, for the latter wounds the outward body, while your sword has cut through the very hearts of existence, both unseen and seen. Yet you rejoice within yourself, as though you were without sin. We ask God either to return to you what you have done, performed, and perpetrated, or to atone for your sins, guide you to Himself, and grant you His mighty, unassailable presence.

270 As for your calling these servants common people, I swear by the Educator of all existence that this very statement of yours is proof of the validity of this Cause. For at the beginning of the Revelation of the year sixty, they voiced precisely the same statement as an objection to the True One. Previously, too, in the days of the Manifestations of Revelation, the tongues of the idolaters uttered these words, as revealed in the manifest Book—exalted are His words: “The chiefs among His people who disbelieved said, ‘We see you only as a human being like ourselves, and we see that none but the lowliest among us have followed you, without reflection. Nor do we see that you have any advantage over us; rather, we think you are liars.’” Thus they counted all who believed in the True One as lowly and common people. By God, even livestock are ashamed of the words of that unbeliever who instilled these ideas. Say: Know yourself, and speak within your limits. Your worth has become manifest and known from what you have put forward. Through God’s power and might, we associate no one with our Lord and turn to no one besides Him.

271 At this point, since it was observed that those who turn away are drinking from the chalice of conceit and are drunk from the cup of pride—for they have called those who know God common people—it became necessary for this servant to quote some of the verses of the Primal Point—may the spirit of all else be sacrificed for Him—so that at least the learned may be distinguished from the ignorant. He says—exalted be His remembrance: “Say: God has created nothing more precious in His sight than knowledge. Through it all are created; through it all are sustained; through it all are put to death; through it all are brought to life. This is your knowledge of God, then of the Manifestation of His Self in every Revelation, and then of what appears from the Manifestation of His Self. Apart from this, nothing is knowledge in God’s sight, if you but knew. At the appearance of the next Revelation, all knowledge consists in your knowledge of Him and of what appears from Him, not of what appeared before, nor in your knowledge of what was ordained before. For at that time you will not be rewarded for it, but rather questioned about it. In every Revelation, its guides should comprehend what has been sent down in that Revelation, not according to the divisions you make.” He continues—exalted be His remembrance—until He says: “Rather, the command is that you abandon all that and count it as belonging to yourselves and your knowledge, if you believe in God and His verses.”

272 Now some reflection on these divine words is needed, so that perhaps they may comprehend the knowledge that has always been beloved and praiseworthy in God’s sight, and you may not regard those who turn away from God as learned. By Him who strengthened the Spirit through the spirit, today, even if all who are in the heavens and on earth were to appear with all earthly learning, and you were to hesitate before this wondrous Cause, you would be counted among the ignorant in God’s sight. Supporting this statement is the explicit utterance of the Point of the Bayán—may the spirit of all else be sacrificed for Him. He says—glorified and exalted be He—addressing the Letter of the Living: “If you believe in Him, whether you know anything or do not know, nothing whatsoever will diminish your merit. But if you are veiled from Him, even if you possess all knowledge, it will not benefit you.” According to the explicit words of that Essence of existence, today the designation ‘learned’ will apply to no one except those souls adorned with the robe of faith in this wondrous Cause.

273 It is astonishing that, despite these clear verses, you have nevertheless called those who believe in God common people. How quickly the breath of the All-Merciful has been cut off from those hearts, and they have become veiled from the sanctified One! For if less than a mote of the fragrance of His knowledge and wisdom had passed over these wayward souls, they would never have occupied themselves with these words, which the lowliest followers of the Qur’an have used as arguments for years. Alas for them, for what they have perpetrated against God! They are among the losers.

274 Listen to another melody of that Sovereign of Oneness. Addressing the Letter of the Living, He says—exalted be His utterance: “He Himself makes Himself known to all things through Himself. Who, then, can make Him known through anything other than Him? Yet, if you attain the day of His Revelation, and if you recognize someone as the most learned of the learned of the Bayán, but see him hesitating before His Cause and then apply to him the designation ‘human,’ you have not negated the letters of negation in order to affirm the Manifestation of Oneness.” End of quotation.

275 How precious are His words, how firm His allusions, how manifest His proof, and how exalted His utterance! Yet you, O people of the Bayán, have wronged the Self of God and His very being. He has left nothing lacking in His exposition: He has laid out every path for you, made clear to you all that was concealed from you, and explained everything to you in detail, as a mercy to you from Him and as guidance and a reminder to the worlds. Yet you have confused the Cause of God for yourselves, just as the religious communities of the past confused it for themselves. Thus their efforts came to nothing in this world and the next, and they were an evil people, the greatest losers.

276 And yet that All-Merciful Beauty says that if, on the day of the revelation, rising, and radiance of the Most Great Luminary, you see the most learned of the learned of the Bayán hesitating and apply to him the designation ‘human,’ you will assuredly be among those souls who have not negated the letters of negation on that day in order to affirm the Manifestation of Oneness.

277 Today, anyone who applies the designation “human” to any of those who turn away, whether the highest or the lowest among them, is deprived of all the outpourings of the All-Merciful—how much more so if he wishes to establish a rank or station for those souls.

278 Consider that the person addressed in the revealed verse is one of the Letters of the Living, who has preceded, and will precede, all the people of the Bayán, according to the explicit statement of the Point of the Bayán Himself: “They are the first creation of the Bayán.” He also says: “Through the existence of these Letters, all attained the law of Divine Oneness and the divine Revelation.” Yet He issued such a warning to those souls, as has been mentioned. Reflect, then, O people of insight! Reflect, O people of discernment! Reflect, O people of goodness! Reflect, O people of righteousness! Reflect, O people of knowledge, wisdom, and utterance!

279 Say: By God, all that was revealed in the Bayán has returned to a single point, and it is now held in the grasp of power and might. We intend to unfold it once again, despite you, O company of those who associate partners with God, just as We have unfolded it time after time. This is a time that has struck terror into the inhabitants of the heavens and the earth, and then the inhabitants of the cities of names, if you are among those who understand. Say: He was My own Self, and now His Self has appeared in truth and spoken between the heavens and the earths. He says in truth: “All verses have returned to My verses, have been raised to life in My words, have appeared by My permission, and have descended from the heaven of My will, if you are among those who are certain. All revelations have culminated in My Revelation, all daysprings have shone through My radiance, and all dawning places have grown resplendent through My rising and My appearance. Where are the eyes that see? Where are the hearts that understand? Where are the sanctified souls? Where are the pure selves? Where are the illumined breasts, that they may recognize, comprehend, behold, and witness what has appeared and shone from the horizon of God, the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the Most Great?” Say: Die in your rage! He has appeared in truth and fears no one. He awaits what has been ordained for Him by His Beloved, the All-Powerful, the All-Knowing, the All-Wise.

280 At every moment He says, addressing your swords, your spears, and your arrows: “Come, draw near, for I yearn for you!” My longing and yearning, My ardor and My melodies bear witness to this, if you are among those who perceive. He who has appeared in truth is My Beloved, and I am His beloved; He is My Self, and I am His Self. What proceeds from My tongue is His wondrous, most gracious, most holy, and unassailable utterance. Anyone who possesses awareness finer than a hair and sight smaller than a speck of dust will surely witness the Revelation of God and His grandeur, perceive the fragrances of His holiness in the breaths of these melodies, and be among those who stand firm in this most great and manifest Cause.

281 Say: I live through His life and am moved by His love, just as He lived through My life and breathed only in remembrance and praise of Me among the worlds. May My soul be sacrificed for Him, and His soul for Me; may My whole being be sacrificed for Him, and His whole being for Me. Whenever I say that He is a sacrifice for Me, it is His very statement that He is a sacrifice for Him. Beware of seeing any distinction, O company of the discerning! Thus has God now sent down verses of His grandeur upon the Sinai of the heart. We have made them manifest to you so that you may thank God, your Maker, O people of the Bayán, and not be among those who are veiled. If

282 O you who are immersed in the sea of allusions! Know that your rejection and objections are directed not at Me, but at God, My Lord and your Lord and the Lord of our forefathers. For I am a servant who has believed in Him, His Messengers, and His chosen ones. I find no existence of My own, for He has raised Me up in truth and sent Me to the worlds. If you wish to object, go to Him and say: “Why did You raise up One through whom my being, and then the realities of those who associate partners with You, have been struck with terror?” If you find Him and are able, speak a word on My behalf in His presence, that He may deliver Me from you and the company of the wicked and make Me one of those who are detached. Say: “O Lord, You know the people of the Bayán. Why did You send this servant of Yours? Behold Him, then, beneath their swords, their claws, and their talons. O You in whose hand is the dominion of the kingdom of the heavens and the earths! Have mercy on Him, O King of kings, and aid Him in truth. You are indeed the All-Powerful, the Most Exalted, the All-Conquering, the Almighty, He who wills.”

283 By Him who has sent Me in truth, I am bewildered by His dealings with Me, for He has raised Me up in days the like of which have never been stirred by the breaths of God, the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the All-Knowing. Through this I have been afflicted as none of His chosen ones has been afflicted. His own Self, the All-Knowing, the All-Informed, bears witness to this. Do you imagine that I speak of My own accord and exist through an existence of My own? No, by the Lord of the worlds! Rather, the winds of His will move Me as He wishes and desires. Anyone possessed of understanding will perceive, in the stirring of My being, the stirring of God, the King, the All-Powerful, the All-Conquering, the Omnipotent. Can you keep yourself still when a shivering fever seizes you and makes every limb tremble? No, by Him who created you in truth, if you are among those who perceive! Just as it moves you and you cannot keep still, so the winds of the will of God move Me. If you wish to object, object to Him. I am only a servant who turns to Him, and I find for Myself neither stillness, repose, nor movement except at His command. None denies this but every treacherous, transgressing sinner.

284 In every Revelation, the first to turn away have been the religious scholars of the age. For had the scholars not denied God, the Truth, no one would have turned away, and whatever corruption has arisen on earth has resulted from the scholars’ denial. Thus He explicitly states—exalted be His mention: “The first to be veiled from the Point of the Bayán are those who regard themselves as the guides of learning. Yet, in God’s sight, they perceive nothing and do not understand.” Thus, in God’s sight, such souls are not counted among the learned, while those who have been enabled to believe are established upon the seat of knowledge, even if they know not a single letter of outward learning.

285 Today, knowledge and ignorance, high and low, nearness and remoteness, the truthful and the false, the living and the dead, the mature and the suckling, the discerning and the heedless are known and distinguished by their acknowledgment of the Revelation. Whoever believes in Him is among the highest of the learned, the most honored of people, the most perceptive, the most God-fearing, the most discerning, the most exalted, the nearest, the most truthful, the most exact in understanding, and the most comprehending. Thus has it descended in truth from the dominion of great glory. Hence the Point of the Bayán says in this connection—exalted be His mention: “Guide, O God, all who love Me to Your religion, for the matter rests equally with You: if the lowest of creation believes in Your Proof and Your verses, he will become its highest, and the most remote of creation will become its nearest.”

286 The purpose of these statements is that all may know that, in God’s sight, the learned are those souls who have come to know Him and have not hesitated to utter the word “Yes.” These souls are the possessors of knowledge and its guides, the possessors of purity, the mine of wisdom, the source of utterance, and the dawning place of remembrance. All good names refer to these souls so long as they remain beneath the shadow of God. Outward learning has never carried weight in this station, nor will it ever do so. Thus that Point of Existence and Countenance of the Desired One has given countless counsels in His revealed words addressed to the learned. He has counseled them all not to become conceited about their own knowledge, wisdom, and utterance at the time of the Revelation, so that they may attain the knowledge of God and what is with Him. Hence the Point of the Bayán says—exalted be His mention: “Perhaps, on the day of the Revelation of the True One, someone may attain the honor of learning by becoming a student before that Source of Knowledge. How exceedingly great is this matter, for His knowledge is none other than His own Self!” He also says—exalted be His mention: “No greater delight has been created in the realm of possibility than for someone to hear His verses, understand their meaning, ask no ‘why’ or ‘wherefore’ concerning His words, and not compare them with the words of anyone else. Just as His being is the manifestation of Divinity and Lordship over all things, so His words are the manifestation of Divinity and Lordship over all words. Were His words a human being endowed with speech, they would assuredly say: ‘I am God; there is no god but Me, and all besides Me is My creation. O all you letters, fear Me!’” End of quotation.

287 O people of the Bayán! Although you have appeared in such a state of limitation that there is no prospect of anyone attaining sanctity and purity, and it seems that even if a hundred thousand doors of divine knowledge were opened, no one would listen, much less understand, heed the divine counsel. At all times, reflect upon the previous Revelation and the events that took place in its days, so that those occurrences may not fade from view and may become a cause of steadfastness.

288 At the beginning of the Revelation, all the religious scholars rose in denial, according to the explicit statement of the Point of the Bayán Himself—may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him. That is, to each of the mujtahids throughout the regions, that Countenance of Oneness revealed a tablet bearing his name and designation, and in that tablet described his rejection in detail. Copies of those tablets are now extant. He wrote to the scholars of Najaf and Karbalá, one after another, and likewise to the scholars of Fárs, name after name, and likewise to the scholars of the Land of Ṣád, the Land of Qáf, and other lands. The doors of fairness were so firmly closed that not a single one of those people renowned for learning and knowledge came to believe in that Tree of divine knowledge, that Source of everlasting wisdom, that Dawning Place of the All-Merciful’s utterance, or attained His presence.

289 The soul who, before all others, attained the greatest honor and became the gate of mercy, the gate of knowledge, the gate of guidance, and the gate of grace for all beings was the honored Báb—may the glory of God rest upon him. Surely everyone has heard the account of that holy person: he was not widely known among the people, nor did he occupy a pulpit or prayer niche. He was one of the students of the honored Ḥájí Siyyid Káẓim—may God’s mercy and blessings rest upon him—and most of the outwardly learned declared that holy soul an unbeliever. Among the students of the departed Siyyid, too, there were some who were better known than he. Thus, one day, when the honored Mullá Mahdí Khú’í, one of the Siyyid’s companions, was asked about the learning of the honored Báb, he named several individuals and ranked them all above that Most Great Gate and Most Noble Word in outward learning and accomplishments, and

290 None of the prominent, well-known figures came to believe; instead, they rose in rejection and opposition. Among them were Mullá Ḥasan Gawhar, Mírzá Muḥíṭ, and Mírzá Karím Khán, as well as others who were renowned in Azerbaijan and the surrounding regions. In short, all the renowned scholars, whether of the Shaykhí school or of the outwardly learned, hesitated concerning the Cause of God; indeed, they turned away and denied it. Because of their rejection, He denied those heedless souls the designation of knowledge.

291 But the honored Most Great Gate—that is, the first to believe—and the seventeen souls who were with him, having attained the honor of faith, are all accounted in God’s sight as guides to knowledge. Thus He says: “These souls, in all the most beautiful names, are gates of mercy to all people.” This is mentioned and recorded in detail in the Persian Bayán; let them consult it. He exerted the utmost effort so that, on the day of the Revelation, you would not remain veiled by anything, nor fix your gaze on anything that has been or will be created. For if, on that day, any soul looks for less than an instant toward anything other than the True One, to that same extent he will be far from the waters of nearness.

292 Keep your heart pure of all things, and turn your gaze away from the signs and allusions, the nearness and remoteness, that you have apprehended through your own reason. For no one has ever known, or now knows, God’s hidden will and His concealed purpose. At every time He has appeared, and will appear, in a wondrous manner. He may well appear in a manner that no one has ever previously conceived. Seek refuge in God and beseech Him night and day to appear in a way that His creatures can readily comprehend, so that perhaps they may not be deprived of the outpourings of that Most Great Ocean and the radiance of the lights of the Sun of inner meanings.

293 The previous Manifestation—may my spirit and my self be sacrificed for Him—gave abundant counsel on this matter and spoke of it in countless utterances, so that no matter could occur to anyone’s mind without having been recorded by His Most Exalted Pen. For example, if someone looks to the Bayán, He says: “Beware lest you be veiled by it from the One who revealed it.” If someone clings to the Letters, He says: “Beware lest you be veiled by the Letters from their Originator and Creator.” If someone prides himself on knowledge, He says: “The designation of knowledge does not truly apply to anyone unless he knows the Manifestation Himself.” If a soul is confined by signs, He says: “Beware lest you be veiled by them,” for no one knows how that Eternal Essence will appear: “He manifests Himself however He wills.” If someone looks to the attainment of perfection, He says that the validation of all things has always depended, and will always depend, on the validation of that Source of truth. If He says that all are perfect, “No one may say ‘why’ or ‘wherefore.’” If He says that all are imperfect, “No one may object to Him.” If a soul is confined by a fixed time, as everyone is veiled by it in these days, He says that whenever He appears, all must acknowledge the Tree of Truth and give thanks to God. If a soul looks to the leaders and mirrors of the age, He addresses them, saying: “You have become proofs through My word. Beware lest you be veiled from My own Self in the other Revelation.”

294 To that Lord belong my glorification and the glorification of all things, my sanctification and the sanctification of all things, my remembrance and the remembrance of all things, for He neglected nothing in counseling His servants concerning this most great and mighty Announcement. He answered beforehand all the objections within His servants’ souls, so that when He appeared once more, nothing would befall Him that would grieve His soul or trouble His heart. Now consider the Cause of God and consider the condition of His servants—how heedless they are! Those souls who were brought into being by His word, instead of thanking Him, praising Him, extolling Him, remembering Him, and showing gratitude for His bounty, have all appeared with swords of malice. We ask God to protect this Wronged One from these malicious people and those who associate partners with God, to aid His Cause, to make His proof manifest, and to exalt His testimony throughout the heavens and the earths. May my spirit be sacrificed for Him, and may my self be sacrificed for Him, for no counsel remained that He had not given His servants.

295 In particular, since He had witnessed conceit and arrogance among the scholars in the previous Revelation, He counseled this group at length, reminding them through gracious admonitions and healing utterances not to follow the ways of the past or become heedless of the True One at the time of the Manifestation’s appearance. For when those souls turn away, all people turn away, except those whom your Lord wills. Thus He says—His words, exalted be His mention: “It is thus that, at every Revelation, the people of that Revelation are veiled by its scholars, while they remain heedless of what lies beyond the matter: by a single ‘No’ from Him, all these become untrue. Watch for the Revelation of the True One, lest on the day of the Revelation you acknowledge Him on the strength of the acknowledgment of the people of the Bayán. For in His presence all their affirmations become pure affirmation through a single ‘Yes,’ and through a single ‘No,’ the opposite.” End of quotation.

296 Likewise, in this connection He addresses the scholars of the Bayán, saying—His words, glorious be His majesty: “O scholars of the Bayán! Have mercy on yourselves, and show mercy by not allowing the concerns of your religion to veil you from the One who appears with the truth. For your entire religion is, in His presence, as it was on the day God created that religion. Look to the Point of the Beginning, for you are being tested like the people of all Revelations. In a night you lie asleep, while in God’s sight you are believers; and in your daytime you are like the scholars before you. God will pass no judgment in your favor. Watch over yourselves, then remain watchful.”

297 O people of the Bayán! Have mercy on yourselves and on the Cause of God, and do not become a cause of discord. Do not commit any act that would divide the united body gathered around one word. Reflect on what has occurred solely for the sake of God. By God, besides whom there is no other god, whenever and in whatever age the True One has appeared, He has appeared in such a manner that those endowed with insight and fairness had no room for hesitation, misgiving, or doubt, except for souls who have deliberately confused themselves. Fear God, O people of the Bayán! Hold fast to God, O people of the Bayán! Have mercy on yourselves, O people of the Bayán! Do not oppose the One in whom you believed in His previous Revelation, through whom the faith of every believer, the certitude of every assured soul, the affirmation of divine unity by everyone who professes it, the knowledge of every learned person, and the remembrance of everyone who remembers God have been established. This is the best counsel from this servant for you, and it is incumbent upon you, if you are fair-minded.

298 You also wrote: “If he claims, ‘I myself am that Point of the Bayán,’ it must mean one of two things. Either he means, ‘I am that very same individual in person,’ which is clearly false, since the repetition of divine manifestations is inadmissible and self-evidently false; or he means the renewal of like beings, whose falsity is even clearer. Moreover, even in this case he must command precisely the same former laws, without any difference. How, then, can change be permissible?” End of quotation.

299 From what appears, you have not yet understood even the meaning of effulgence, for you have not recognized its proper place. What is at issue here is the Source of effulgence, not effulgence itself. If you look with the eye of insight, you will recognize the Origin of infinite effulgences. This answer has already been given: He says precisely what the Primal Point said.
