Set aside discussion of effulgence and its non-recurrence, recognize the Source of effulgence, and detach yourself from all else.

300 What do you say about the Primal Point—may the souls of all else be sacrificed for Him—who says that the Point of the Bayán is precisely that same Point of the Qur’an of former times? Likewise, throughout the Bayán He says, “He who has appeared is the very same as He who will appear, and He who will appear is the very same as He who has appeared.” He also says, “I am indeed He, and He is indeed I.”

301 Beyond this, the Manifestations of divine unity are united in one respect and appear in different revelations in another. Insofar as all shine from the dawning-place of the Cause, have spoken from God, and have been commissioned to convey the message to humanity, they are all one. Thus He says, “We make no distinction between any of them.” In another station, that of laws, names, and earthly conditions, each has appeared with a name, a law, a human form, and an outward appearance. Yet if you look with the eye of insight—by God, besides whom there is no other god—you will behold them all as one reality, one revelation, one movement, one deed, one action, one law, one name, and one practice. It is astonishing that you have not grasped this explanation. The children of this Revelation have come to understand it, for all these degrees have been described in detail in the Persian Tablets that descended from the heaven of the glory of divine unity during the years of residence in Iraq. Blessed, then, are those who have attained and those who understand.

302 You have not yet understood the nature of the Revelation. You have supposed divine power to be like your own power and made His Cause dependent on your own affairs. How evil is what you have imagined! He appears however He wills, just as He has appeared in truth, despite those who associate partners with God.

303 This Revelation speaks in precisely the same manner as the Primal Point—may the souls of all else be sacrificed for Him—has spoken. Reflect, so that you may understand. This is that very promised Revelation which all were promised in the Bayán. Today the deeds of everyone who turns away from this Revelation are rejected before God and receive no mention. He now says, “I am indeed God; there is no god but Me,” just as the Point said before, and precisely the same will be said by whoever comes afterward. Have you not seen that He says, “If the sun rises a hundred thousand times, it has been and will be one sun”? And He says, “Regard the Manifestations of divine unity in this same way.”

304 Know that effulgence is considered from two perspectives: from one it recurs, and from the other it does not. These two perspectives pertain to outward, earthly effulgences, while effulgence itself, in its own station, has been and will remain sanctified above both perspectives. Understanding this station, however, is somewhat difficult. Anyone who wishes to understand it as it truly is must come into the presence of the Countenance, so that he may understand what he did not understand before and become acquainted with what is concealed.

305 Furthermore, you have held the renewal of like beings to be impossible. If the renewal of beings like the divine Manifestations is impossible, the renewal of satanic counterparts is nonetheless a visible reality that leaves no room for denial. As can be seen, in every city an exact likeness of these embodiments of darkness exists and is visible. Take heed, then, O people of understanding!

306 In short, effulgence has proceeded and will continue to proceed without interruption from its Source. Differences appear in the forms of the Manifestations, if you are among those who understand. If you rise a little higher and cleanse your heart of the words of those who associate partners with God, you will yourself understand what you have now denied without consideration. The issue is not the recurrence of effulgence, such that it might be admissible or inadmissible.

307 Listen to the words of this servant, and with a pure heart and clear sight consider the words of God and His Revelation. Do not take pride in two fabricated words that Satan has suggested. Consider the peoples of the past and their scholars, who composed a hundred thousand books. Their purpose in all those books was to establish the truth of the True One, yet after His appearance they were all deprived of Him by those very books. Nevertheless, those who turn away have still not taken heed and have now begun writing treatises. Woe to their fingers, their sight, and their hands! At the time of Revelation, no word is established in its proper place except by permission of the Manifestation of Revelation.

308 The Point of the Bayán—glorious and exalted is He—has said, “On the day of Revelation, do not use His words as arguments against Him.” At Satan’s prompting, you have used, and continue to use, the fabrications of souls who turn away as arguments against Him. He foretold these very arguments of yours and of others like you. God, the Most High, the Most Great, has spoken the truth. This is the explicit statement of the Revealer of the Bayán, who says—exalted be His mention—“He has exerted His utmost effort so that, on the day of the appearance of the True One, His words should not be used as arguments against Him, for the whole Bayán is the utterance of His previous Revelation, and He knows what He has revealed better than all creation, since the spirit of all is in His grasp. With all others there is only a shadow, if they are established in the truth; otherwise, it is not worthy of mention. Thus, even if someone were the Plato of his age in every science, would his knowledge benefit him if he did not acknowledge the True One? Far be it from God! Rather, for him knowledge is that which is knowledge of God, His Messenger, the Manifestations of His Cause, and the Manifestations of His command. Those endowed with understanding do not apply the term knowledge to anything else.” End of quotation.

309 Say: Open your eyes, O heedless one, and then listen to what the Dove sings upon the branches of the tree of utterance concerning God, the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the All-Bountiful. Consider how lofty and exalted the Cause is, that that Temple of divine unity and self-subsisting Essence has barred His own words from that most holy, most pure, most manifest, most luminous court, expressly saying that at the time of Revelation you must not use the words of the Bayán as arguments against that Temple of inner meanings and Manifestation of self-subsisting glory. Nevertheless, the people of the Bayán have felt no shame. If only they had used His words as arguments! Instead, they have used, and continue to use, rejected, fabricated words issuing from malicious souls as arguments against the Gems of divine unity and the Essence of eternity. Alas for these souls, who have been deprived of the shore of the ocean of divine knowledge by two fabricated expressions, and have been kept from the holy breezes of divine unity by a foul odor! If only they had been content with argument. No, by Him who has set the tongue of the Spirit speaking in His own praise, they have not been and will not be content with argument. Rather, with swords of malice and spears of hatred, they have advanced and continue to advance toward the Most High, the Most Exalted.

310 He also says—His utterance, exalted be His utterance—“No one is acquainted with the Bayán, nor has anyone encompassed or will anyone encompass its knowledge, except that divine Temple, the mine and wellspring of divine knowledge.” Nevertheless, those who have risen to wage war have claimed and continue to claim knowledge, although—by God, besides whom there is no other god—in the school of true knowledge they have never equaled and will never equal a child learning the alphabet. Indeed, if they entered that presence, they would find themselves incapable. He says that the spirit of all has been and will remain in the grasp of the power of that most unapproachable, most holy Revelation, and that with all others there is only a shadow of it—and even this only if they remain steadfast in the truth. Otherwise, He says, they are not worthy of mention.

311 Now consider to what a station these souls have fallen. By God, they are in the lowest depths of the abyss, if only you perceived it. Today all the sciences of the earth are as nothing before God and receive no mention. Rather, the divine knowledge that has been intended and has been praised in all the divine Books and everlasting Scriptures is knowledge of the Manifestation of His own Self in every Revelation, at the time of His appearance. Today, whoever attains this, even if he does not know a single letter, is accounted before God the most learned of all people. But if—God forbid—he remains deprived of this supreme bounty, even if he knows all the books by heart, he is regarded before God as more ignorant than anyone else.

312 Listen to another utterance of the Revealer of the Bayán—may the spirit of all else be sacrificed for Him. His words, exalted be His majesty: “And likewise consider that, at the appearance of that Ancient Beauty, even if all the people of the Bayán should become like Him in the essence of knowledge, this yields no fruit except through affirming Him. Take heed, then, O people of knowledge, and fear Him.” End of quotation. By God, this servant is now ashamed to speak: here is this utterance of the Primal Point—may the spirit of all else be sacrificed for Him—and there are those words of yours that have darkened the world with gloom and dust. Raise your head from sleep, then behold the lights of your Lord’s revelation, so that you may become aware of what you have neglected and be among those who turn in repentance to God, who created me and you and made us among those who attained the days of meeting Him and were among the victorious. I ask God that a breeze of loving care may blow, that perhaps it may draw that dead one into the realm of life, and that, through His grace and loving care, He may give him a drop of the water of life and of the heavenly stream flowing from the right hand of the throne of your Lord, the All-Merciful. He is powerful and mighty over all things.

313 Leaving these matters aside, what do you say about John son of Zechariah and Jesus son of Mary? John son of Zechariah was sent before Jesus and also claimed prophethood, as all the sects of Islam acknowledge and affirm his prophethood. He also appeared with laws and ordinances. Thus, whenever anyone entered his presence and acknowledged his mission, he baptized him with water according to the rites sent down at that time from the heaven of the divine will, and instructed him in the divine words. He also gave the people glad tidings of the appearance of the Most Great Word, that is, the Spirit of God.

314 After the Spirit appeared and the divine Word rose from behind the concealing veils, shining and resplendent with the radiance of divine oneness, the people raised objections, because He outwardly associated and consorted with sinners. It is recorded and written in the Gospel itself that He associated with tax collectors. Thus, on numerous occasions they objected to that Sovereign of Oneness. In particular, one day some of the heedless entered His presence and objected: “O Spirit of God, the companions of John son of Zechariah were very ascetic and devout, so much so that they fasted by day and stood in remembrance of God by night. But from what we observe of you and your companions, you do not engage in worship and remembrance; rather, you do not refrain from taking people’s property.” The Spirit reflected within Himself, then raised His head and said, “If John was as you say, why did you kill him, O company of those who associate partners with God?” He says: “According to you, I am a sinner. But John son of Zechariah had done no wrong, and you all acknowledge the asceticism and piety of that manifest Beauty. Why, then, did you kill him?” The one who disbelieved was confounded and went back disappointed and defeated.

315 They also objected: “You have changed the law of John, even though his Cause has not yet been established among the people and his dispensation has not run its course, for only a few days have passed between that revelation and this one. He baptized people with water, while you say, ‘I baptize with the Holy Spirit,’ as your companions have done. They fasted, while your companions eat. He appeared in perfect asceticism, never taking so much as a mustard seed’s worth of people’s property, whereas whenever any of your companions pass through the fields, they do not refrain from taking and eating, nor do they observe any other practice.” He said: “These are days of joy and delight. A time will come when they will observe all the practices.” His meaning was that, since these are the days of meeting God, this is in truth the station of the paradise of oneness, and in that station, should there be negligence in certain outward matters, God—exalted be His glory—will forgive it. After the Spirit’s ascent to the Most Exalted Companion, they will certainly carry out what they have been commanded.

316 By God, the revelation of the Primal Point and this most wondrous, most glorious Revelation are exactly like the revelations of John son of Zechariah and the Spirit of God, and everything has occurred in correspondence with them. John was a prophet and messenger from God, and also the herald of the revelation to follow, as he said: “O my people, I bring you glad tidings of the Kingdom of God, and it has drawn near,” and elsewhere, “And the Kingdom of God has drawn near.” He also brought laws and a religious code, and the Spirit appeared during the days of his revelation. Likewise, the Primal Point—may the spirit of all else be sacrificed for Him—after taking a covenant from everyone and giving glad tidings of the revelation to follow, says: “The decline has indeed drawn near, while you lie asleep.” This conveys exactly the same meaning that John son of Zechariah expressed and announced as glad tidings.

317 In this Revelation too, the people of the Bayán have raised the same objections that were raised against Jesus son of Mary. Thus you write that the dispensation has not run its course, and you also write that the laws must not be changed, wishing to imply that He has changed the laws. This very claim, which is an outright lie, has been instilled in you, for He has expressly instructed them to act according to the Persian Bayán, and

318 Furthermore, changing the laws has no bearing on rank, such that every revelation claiming superiority to the preceding revelation must change the laws and abrogate the previous commandments. The Point of the Bayán Himself has said: “Were it not for the objections of the people of the Qur’an, I would not have abrogated the law of the Qur’an.” Moreover, according to your own belief and that of all your associates, David is one of the greatest prophets and the bearer of a Book; nevertheless, he commanded the people to follow the law of the Torah. Likewise, among the words of Jesus son of Mary is this saying: “The Son of Man did not come to judge the world, but came to give life to the world.” From this utterance it appears that, at the beginning of His revelation, He did not intend to change the previous laws, just as John son of Zechariah made no wholesale change, except in certain matters. What is certain is that the Spirit changed the law of the Sabbath and appointed Sunday, and likewise abolished the law of the sword and also prohibited divorce.

319 Yet consider this Revelation: although at the outset nothing appeared but pure servitude, the wolves of the earth among the people of the Bayán nevertheless rose in rejection and opposition, emerging from the thickets of hypocrisy to assail the Luminary of the horizons. This was despite the firm command of the Bayán, recorded in all the Tablets of God, that if anyone should appear with verses, you must not oppose him, dispute with him, or argue against him. He has emphasized this command to such a degree that, by God, besides whom there is no other god, no command firmer or more established than this has been revealed in the Bayán. It is as though the entire Bayán had been revealed concerning this most wondrous, most unassailable Cause. Nevertheless, the first to turn away and others like him have risen in rejection and inflicted what no oppressor has ever perpetrated. You were not in this land to know of it. By the Primal Point and all the prophets and messengers, I speak in truth and sincerity. When he saw the divine verses descending like pouring rain, the most great divine Ocean surging, the Tree of everlasting glory adorned with wondrous, lofty fruits, the winds of grace blowing from the right hand of the divine throne, and the lofty heaven of eternity embroidered with the stars of new sciences and knowledge, he rose in hypocrisy and sought to cut down the divine Tree. When he proved incapable of this, the deceit within his breast became manifest. All the emigrants were thrown into turmoil, the members of the holy household were occupied with lamentation and mourning, and the Beauty of Oneness withdrew from among the people. Then they resorted to slander and lies, intending to conceal that heinous deed with fabricated calumnies and foul, lying suppositions. Afterward, they did what they did, as you yourself know what that mischief-making slanderer who came to that land has said and done. No one has assailed or spoken against that source of the abyss. Nevertheless, he has called himself wronged and instilled in others like himself what Satan has instilled and continues to instill in his allies. Woe to them and to those who have followed them! By God, they are indeed in grievous loss.

320 Another statement: “He must be more perfect and more complete, just as the revelation of every messenger and prophet has been higher than the preceding revelation, and the Most Exalted Lord has made the same claim in relation to His predecessors. This is why each successor has abrogated what came before.” End of quotation.

321 First, if the eye of fairness is open, it sees that nothing like this Revelation has appeared in creation. What has descended from the dominion of God, the All-Compelling, the Almighty, the All-Powerful, bears witness to this. Open your eyes, then, to witness that the Beauty of Revelation is now established upon the throne of grandeur and independence. At His right is the Point of the Bayán, with the sovereignty of might and majesty; at His left is Muhammad, the Messenger of God, with the lights of God, the Mighty, the Most Exalted. Before His face the Spirit stands with a company of the heavenly concourse and has descended in truth, if you understand. Behind Him are ranks of the angels of heaven, with pitchers from the Kawthar of everlasting life and cups of Tasnim, if you know. They all lament, weep, and cry out over what has befallen the Beauty of God, the All-Compelling, the Almighty, the Self-Subsisting.

322 If you had sight and turned toward the Most Great Vision, you would behold the divine lights directly, face to face, in the Manifestation of the glory of the everlasting Sovereign. But the inflammation of vain imaginings has so afflicted their eyes that, even if they entered the most holy abode, I do not suppose they would attain His presence. Just as a dung beetle, even if it dwells near Ridvan, has always been and will remain deprived of the fragrance of its roses, so a blind person, even if he stands before the sun, has always been and will remain deprived of its light. They have no share in this most great Ocean.

323 As for your statement that every successor must take precedence over and be higher than his predecessor: if you had not appeared troubled and shaken, I would have mentioned here a word I heard from the True One. This is a matter in which anyone other than God who speaks is reckoned to be meddling. God knows His intention in what He has revealed, for none but the Manifestation Himself has ever known or will ever know the pearls of meaning concealed within the shells of the oceans of divine words. Knowledge of all this is with my Lord, and no one has become acquainted with it except His own Self, the All-Knowing, the Omniscient. Since the people of the Bayán appear more veiled than those who clung to vain imaginings before them, it is better that we show forbearance and conceal what we have understood, lest another outcry arise. God’s all-encompassing power and all-embracing mercy have never been and will never be confined within any limits. He does what He wills and decrees what He desires.

324 Concerning the abrogation of previous laws, the Point of the Bayán—may the souls of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—says that everything depends upon the will of the Manifestation Himself. If He wishes, He confirms the former commandments and prohibitions, and if He wishes, He abrogates them. Authority is in His hand. No one has the right to object to Him or to say, “Why?” or “For what reason?” concerning anything He does or commands. Whoever says, “Why?” or “For what reason?” has disbelieved in God, fought against His own Self, disputed His sovereignty, denied His Cause, and become one of those who associate partners with God.

325 Another statement: “If it is by way of reflection, as with the holy and pure Imams in relation to Muhammad, who claimed likeness to Him while remaining beneath His shadow, then the matter is clear, for it does not befit such a person to claim superiority under such circumstances.” End of quotation.

326 This matter has been addressed in the preceding explanation. These further words of that prompter who associates partners with God, which are more contemptible than the words of children, have been and will remain unanswered. O man, what reflection? What reflector? This court is sanctified above mention and description and exalted beyond the understanding of all who dwell in the contingent world.

327 At this point a story came to mind. In the days when the Primal Point, glorified and exalted be He, was imprisoned in the mountain of Maku—as He is now imprisoned in this land, though do not compare this imprisonment with that one, for now He is imprisoned both by the government and by the people of the Bayán, whereas the imprisonment of the former Manifestation was solely at the hands of the government; and if you consider carefully, all governments also share in this imprisonment.

328 In those days, then, the honored Vahid, that is, Aqa Siyyid Yahya—upon him be the glory of God—had raised the banner in support of the Cause, as you have heard. In every land the religious scholars had risen in rejection and denial, and from every pulpit they were occupied—God forbid—with reviling and cursing the Primal Point and His companions. The Imam-Jumih of Tehran, although in truth he bore no hatred and was fairer than all the others, one day ascended the pulpit and said, “Among other things, I have heard that the Báb has declared air to be a purifier. Now tell me, O man, in the book of which jurist or scholar has He seen such a statement, that air is a purifier?” He had not realized even this much: that this was not the station of independent legal reasoning, but a station beyond such discussions and words, and that He Himself was the renewer of religious laws. Instead, he had understood Him to be one of the jurists who had stated this point of religious law and deemed it permissible in opposition to the scholars.

329 And now you too do not know what is happening or what Cause has appeared from the horizon of the divine will. Nevertheless, you take pride in these words, which have always befitted those who prompted them and which you too have written at their prompting. Surely, at Satan’s instigation, you have also sent them far and wide, as He has specifically informed us. He is indeed the Truthful, the All-Knowing. Soon God will nullify what they possess through His true word and expose the scheming of their souls to His servants who are near to Him.

330 What befitted you was first to ask the Manifestation Himself what His claim was and what His proof was, so that you might become acquainted with it. If He said that this was the very Revelation whose advent the Point of the Bayán had announced and for whom He had taken a covenant from everyone, then all these objections were and would remain fruitless. You would not have put yourself to so much trouble to piece together the fabricated words of those who prompted you. Fear God, do not say what you do not know, and do not follow everyone who cries out. If you wish to become acquainted with the truth, ask God, your Lord and the Lord of the worlds.

331 Another statement—how great the heedlessness of the one who prompted it: “At most, He is one of His guides and mirrors. The mirrors have ranks, and the one who fully reflects will be from the lineage of one. The succession of the perfect mirror will be one after another. The others will not fully reflect, and the Most Exalted Lord has confined this too to Mirza Yahya.” End of quotation.

332 The one who prompted you has erred, rebelled in what he has perpetrated, disbelieved in God who created him from dust, and slandered the One to whom he returns in his final destination and abode. He is among those who have divided the Book into fragments, for he has now introduced the notion of a perfect and an imperfect mirror. By God, the Pen is bewildered as to what it should say in answer to such heedless people. The Primal Point Himself says in prayer, “My God, send in every year a mirror, that it may point to You and reflect You.” Then He says, “Send one in every city.” Even if we were to accept your statement, it contradicts the words of the Point of the Bayán. The highest rank of the mirrors is this rank of which He speaks—His words, exalted be His majesty: “Say: O suns of the mirrors, you look toward the Sun of Truth, for your existence depends upon it, if you perceive. You are all like fish moving through the water in the sea, yet you are veiled from the water and ask about that by which you subsist.”

333 Say: O heedless one, consider how He tells the suns of the mirrors, which is their highest rank, to look toward the Sun of Truth. Then He says that you are like fish moving in the sea, veiled from the water, and questioned about that by which you subsist.

334 By God, if the people of the Bayán were fair, this utterance of the Revealer of the Bayán alone would suffice them all. They would detach themselves from the words of those who associate partners with God and would witness today the meaning of these divine words. Thus this soul who has turned away, who considers himself a mirror and whom some, without understanding, have assumed to be a mirror—even granting this assumption—has remained deprived of the Sun of Truth. As can be seen, he looks to his own self, clings to a vain imagining, and remains distant from and denied access to the Sun of Truth. He moves in the water, inasmuch as he shelters beneath the radiance of the divine lights, yet he is estranged, veiled, and deprived of Him. Likewise, today all the mirrors are questioned, for all stand at the place of questioning. Whichever departs from the water’s edge of the recognition of God is reckoned as stone—indeed, is unworthy of mention—and whichever recognizes Him and takes refuge beneath the shadow of His loving care is numbered among the foremost mirrors in the sight of God. Thus has the matter been set forth in detail, so that people may understand.

335 Now reflect a little on this utterance, so that you may perhaps attain the Kawthar of oneness flowing from the wellspring of the divine words. Say: O uninformed oppressor, where in the Bayán are these restrictions recorded, which you have put forward, slandering God by claiming that the mirror that fully reflects will come from the descendants of one person, and that the Most Exalted Lord has further confined this to a certain individual? Say: You have fabricated a lie against the Lord of the throne and the exalted seat. These words reveal the true measure of the understanding of the one who prompted you and to whom inspiration was given. Well done! How thoroughly you have fettered the outstretched hand of God! They speak like the Jews and do not perceive it. Dust upon the mouths of such speakers, and clay upon the heads of the immature, who have sullied the transcendence of the Cause and the sanctity of the divine words with the dust of ignorance and restriction. The Cause of God has always been sanctified above these limitations.

336 By God, besides whom there is no other god, they are seen to be more veiled than the lowliest followers of earlier religions. Yet the master has been greatly pleased by this statement, and in consideration of this service he has certainly appointed you, or will appoint you, as one of the witnesses of the fire. For he has always intended to confine the Cause of God to himself and what proceeds from his loins, and you have endorsed this even before he intended it. A disciple—that is you! A hundred thousand congratulations to you for forsaking the Creator for the sake of a vile creature whose deeds, by God, besides whom there is no other god, Satan has shunned and continues to shun. Shame on him and on whoever follows him! By God, at his fabrication the pillars of the throne shook and the supports of the exalted seat trembled.

337 Hear the call of this servant, and wash clean this book of self and desire that, at Satan’s prompting, you have inscribed with conjectural, lying, slanderous words. Leave this narrow wasteland and enter a vast, lofty expanse. Do not speak without seeing, and do not write without understanding. Soon you and he and we will all return to dust. O fair-minded one, why have you set yourself so fiercely to undermining the Cause of God, and attributed all these fabrications to God to please a worthless, insignificant creature? The blasts of wrath have seized you. What wrath could be greater than this: you worship the cow and do not know it, then implore God to bring forth a calf for you from his loins, so that you may worship it and be among its worshippers; then, from the descendants of this calf, another calf. Should the line of descent be cut off, you would console yourselves, lament, and weep as you weep at the loss of your fathers and mothers—indeed, even more, if you were among those who perceive.

338 Consider how heedless these souls are: the Sun of meanings has risen and shone from the horizon of reality in the utmost sublimity of grandeur and glory, yet they have not esteemed Him even as much as what comes forth from the loins of the cow. Say: A curse upon you and upon your sense of shame! The Temple of Oneness has torn the robe of grandeur because of your oppression, O company of the obstinate.

339 What is astonishing is that you have assumed Siyyid Muḥammad of Isfahan to be truthful, though he had no knowledge whatsoever of this matter and was not with Him, while you have deemed all the souls who were informed to be liars. At his instigation you have drawn the sword of hatred against the Beauty of the Lord of the worlds. Cast away these selfish promptings and subhuman deceits. By the Sun of meanings, those who circle around Him are ashamed to mention these false mirrors of yours. You have not understood even this much: the existence of the mirrors has not been of itself and for itself. Rather, when they face the sun, a radiance from it appears in them; after they turn away, it has vanished and will vanish. A hundred thousand mirrors have been and are created by a word. So long as they remain beneath the shadow of the Tree of Affirmation and the Sun of Truth, they are reckoned as mirrors; otherwise, they are reckoned among the stones of Sijjin and the people of the fire.

340 Another statement: “Let us concede that he is superior to the Most Exalted Lord, that he is His Resurrection, and that His dispensation has been superseded—in the sense that ‘I am that very One whom God will make manifest, promised by the Most Exalted Lord Himself, through whom the dispensation of the Bayán will be superseded, and who will abrogate it.’ The falsity of this claim is clearer than the sun. First, the Báb Himself has stated this in a manner that no one can dispute. One of those statements, in brief, is this: ‘If the creation of that Revelation is not completed, God will not manifest the other Manifestation.’ He has also explained the meaning of completion: since whatever He has said and commanded is from God, it must not fail to be fulfilled. A creation must be created beneath His shadow, and all His pronouncements, whether concerning exalted stations or subsidiary religious ordinances, must be realized. Otherwise, the appearance of such a person would be futile, and all the tidings that come from God would be false.” End of quotation.

341 Say: O asses! Whatever God says is true and is not invalidated by the words of those who associate partners with Him. Rather, what is and will be clearer than the sun today is the falsity of those very souls who turn away. Say: Rein yourself in, O deceiver, O deluded one, whose like the eye of time has never seen! I do not know to whom I can compare those souls, for in turning away they have no likeness, equal, or counterpart.

342 Say to that prompter who associates partners with God: O heedless one, do not deem God false, and do not reckon His words false. By God, O denier, the fragrance of the divine words is so distinct from that of all others that, were He in the east of creation and to speak a single word, one in the west would perceive the fragrance of holiness from it, provided he were purified from the winds of those who associate partners with God. Whatever proceeds from God—glorious is His glory and great His majesty—in deed or word has been and will be obeyed by those endowed with hearts. No one is permitted to turn away or object. To this every person of reason, understanding, and perception bears witness. Yet, since they were created from ignorance, they will not perceive and will remain among the heedless. One must first recognize God, what He says, and what His proof is. Once this is established and clear evidence and proof have been presented, all these words, and those who utter and speak them, have returned and will return to the fire. The fire is indeed their abode, and wretched is the abode of those who associate partners with God.

343 As for his having written that the Most Exalted One said, “If the creation of that Revelation is not completed,” and so forth: the matter of completion lies within the grasp of divine power. You and those like you have not understood it and will not understand it. People may practice a religious law for a thousand years, yet the term “completion” may not apply to it; or all may be brought to completion in a single day. No one but God knows whether completion has occurred or not, for the completion of every matter depends on His will. As mentioned before, if He wishes, He causes all people to return to a single soul, and from that same soul causes them to return to His own Self, to be gathered and made manifest. Likewise, the resurrection of all souls and their completion: upon a soul’s acknowledgment of the Revealer, at that very moment the completion of the preceding Revelation is decreed in that soul. All this is an ordainment from One who is all-powerful and mighty. Yet those who are veiled and deluded have not attained this station or come to understand it.

344 It may well be that the Sun of Truth appears and shines while all people remain veiled, and all the manifestations of affirmation appear, are established, and abide in the person of the Revelation Himself, while the decree of negation applies to and holds true of all others. This is a hidden mystery that no one has known except one whose sight God has opened and illumined with the light of certitude. Thus the Point of the Bayán—may the soul of all else be sacrificed for Him—says, exalted is His majesty: “Do not let the multitude of these people and their rigid adherence to their own cause veil you. For if it be supposed that in a Revelation the Tree of Truth were to speak and all were to hesitate concerning Him, all would have been and would be negated within ‘there is no god.’ Though they remain on the face of the earth, their continued existence has been and is an existence in the fire, and the whole of affirmation would be that inaccessible, exalted Soul.” End of quotation.

345 Now, what do you say about this utterance of the Point of the Bayán? How do you conceive of the completion of the cycle of that Revelation, when no one has attained recognition of Him? Understand, then, that the completion of the cycle has been and will be through the person of the Revelation Himself. Yet no one understands this except one who has drunk the Kawthar of meanings from the Most Great Ocean, which has appeared to the right of the throne with manifest, unmistakable waves.

346 Likewise, the substance of the utterance of the Point of the Bayán—may the soul of all else be sacrificed for Him—is that He says: The interval between two Revelations is prolonged because people lack readiness. After setting in the All-Glorious horizon, the Sun of Truth watches over His servants. Whenever He observes that a soul has appeared who can hear the Word of God, He makes Himself known to that soul at that very moment, and does not wait even for less than a ninth of a ninth of a tenth of a tenth of a minute. In this connection He has given the Point of the Qur’an—exalted is His grandeur—as an example, saying that after the setting of the Muhammadan Sun, He continually watched over His creation. Had He observed that a soul had come into existence on earth who could bear the Trust of God, He would never have delayed His Revelation. The interval extended to the number of Gharís for this reason. Thus, when the first believer became ready and accepted the Most Great Word, there was not the slightest delay in making the Cause manifest, and at that very moment the Word of God was imparted to him. Had he attained maturity and entered His presence one instant earlier, its imparting would not have been delayed even for that one instant.

347 You who are veiled, reflect on the words and utterances of God, that you may perhaps attain a sprinkling from the surging ocean of meanings. If you have eyes to see, you will recognize the very person of this wondrous Revelation as the means of completing the Bayán, for at the very moment of His Revelation the cycle of the Bayán was completed. Beyond this, He has enjoined everyone to observe the laws of the Bayán and has Himself acted in accordance with them. Had all the veils been torn apart, what would you have done? Know that, at the time of the Revelation, in every soul who entered His presence, all the names, attributes, religious laws, ordinances, and commandments that had existed before were, and are, brought to completion through that soul’s turning toward the Truth. Reflect, then, that you may be among those who recognize.

348 As for your statement that all His pronouncements, whether concerning exalted stations or subsidiary religious matters, must be fulfilled, otherwise the appearance of such a person would be futile and all God’s tidings false—know that everything in the Bayán has come into the realm of manifestation and has been gathered into the presence of God. At that time, however, such souls were dead and as nothing, and therefore remained unaware. After the Revelation, these assertions have availed no one and never will, for the completion of all things has depended on His confirmation, the glory of all on His command, and the deeds of all on His acceptance.

349 For example, should He wish to take back everything He bestowed in the preceding Revelation, no one has the right to object to Him, for He has been and will remain powerful. An utterance of the Point of the Bayán—may the soul of all else be sacrificed for Him—is quoted, that you may perhaps become aware within yourself and be among those who perceive.

350 His words—exalted is His majesty: “The whole Bayán is like a jewel entrusted to someone. On the day of the appearance of the Manifestation of Truth, if He wishes to take back from everyone what He has given them, they must not hesitate in the slightest—not that one should be veiled by a question concerning its subsidiary matters, another by its principles, another by the aspects of authority, and another by the aspects of glory. Rather, everything comes from Him and returns to Him. Recognize Him by His verses and exercise no caution in recognizing Him, for to that same extent you will remain veiled in the fire. If, in the privacy between yourselves and God, you turn toward Him, it is His image that is in your hearts. Do not let it veil you from Him. Recognize the One for whose recognition you have been created.”

351 End of quotation. Now consider how far you have remained from the shore of the ocean of recognition, and how greatly the veil of self and desire has deprived you of the Originator and Establisher of the names. By God, you have acted contrary to everything the Point of the Bayán desired. You have distorted and explained away all His decisive verses through false conjectures. You have not drunk a drop from the ocean of holiness or received a breath of the winds of the glory of sanctification. Yet concerning the Kawthar of utterance that has flowed from the wellspring of the pen of the Sovereign of existence, you speak sometimes of subsidiary matters and sometimes of principles. By God, you have had, and still have, no aim but to seize a cord among the cords of self and use it to object to the Sovereign of Oneness.

352 Now consider how the words of God have been fulfilled. He expressly stated that on that day no one should remain veiled by reference to subsidiary matters, principles, authority, or the aspects of glory, for that Essence of Eternity is Himself the One to be obeyed, and whatever He pronounces at the time of His Revelation is the decree of God and His command among His servants. Nevertheless, you and those like you have argued against the Truth by invoking principles and subsidiary matters, and the spiritual guide by invoking the aspects of glory and authority. Today you are all seen in the fire, except those who have held fast to God, the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the Unconstrained.

353 You who are unaware! So emphatically has He spoken that He says: On that day, exercise no caution in recognizing Him, for to the extent of that caution you will be in the fire. Nevertheless, you have inflicted what none of the manifestations of negation and Sijjín has inflicted. Another utterance of the Revealer of the Bayán is quoted for the sake of God, that you may perhaps cast the tattered garments of hatred from your bodies and attain the robe of divine glory. His words—exalted is His majesty: “But do not become complacent through love for the Point and the Letters of the Living, for that day is the day of testing. If someone loves that Point and those Letters and is guided by their guidance, this is proof that he has loved this Point and these Letters.” End of quotation.

354 Consider how firmly and conclusively He has set forth this most wondrous, most unassailable, most holy Cause in the Book, even saying: Do not become complacent through love for Me, and do not remain veiled from that Ancient Beauty. Nevertheless, at every moment you have advanced, and continue to advance, countless arguments. If you say that this Revelation is not that Revelation, this is the very assertion made by all previous religious communities at the times when the Manifestations of Oneness appeared. For example, when the Spirit of God and His Word appeared, everyone invoked the former scriptures to argue that He was false, and all were opponents and deniers, except those whom your Lord willed, who were but a few. Likewise, at the Revelation of the Seal of the Prophets, who shone from the dawning-place of Bathá, all denied Him, saying, ‘You are not the promised Revelation.’ They put forward fabricated proofs and wrote treatises refuting the Truth. Likewise, in the Revelation of the Point of the Bayán, you have seen, and still see, what they have done and are doing. What difference is there between this community and that group?

355 A person of insight must today look to the Revelation Himself and the essence of the Cause. If he sees the same clear evidence and proof by which he established the Primal Point and the Countenance of Oneness, hesitation for less than an instant is not permissible, for to the extent of that hesitation he will be in the fire. Indeed, for those who behold the Most Great Vision, the Revelation Himself is His own proof. This servant is bewildered as to what to say, for it is evident that you have remained deprived of the explicit statements of God’s words, let alone their allusions and intimations. It seems that you have not even intended to understand, nor do you now. Thus this servant’s words and melodies are like playing a lute for souls deprived of hearing. This verse is fitting here.

356 Saná’í’s subtle, enigmatic words among the ignorant are like playing a lute before the deaf or holding a mirror before the blind. God has laid deafness upon your ears and a covering of fire upon your hearts.

357 O heedless one! He says—exalted is His remembrance: “Do not veil yourselves with the veils of knowledge and wisdom, for this will surely bar you from the path of God.” Yet now you send learned allusions as gifts to the Sovereign of all that is known, and dispatch a dried leaf as an offering to the Ridván of the glory of Oneness. If only you possessed knowledge and wisdom! No, by God, you are seen as sheer nothingness and dried skins containing small stones, from which a sound emerges when they move, yet which are bereft and devoid of true existence and movement: “A calf, a body that lows.”

358 Today no proofs can bar the One they attest from His Cause, and nothing created between the heavens and the earth has been or will be sufficient except entry beneath the shadow of His Cause. Were the whole earth filled with books, they would not equal a single letter appearing today from the tongue of the Essence of Eternity. Cast away these investigations that neither nourish nor enrich, and cleanse your hearts of the misgivings arising from the expressions of those versed in allusions, that perhaps in this divine springtime you may attain the wondrous Paradise. Sever yourselves from all but Him and cling to Him. Strive, that today you may not be veiled by words, allusions, names, and their manifestations from the One who brings them into being and gives them reality. This is why the Point of the Bayán—exalted be His glory—says, in His exalted words: “All is for this purpose: that on the Day of Manifestation you may not remain veiled by names, but rather look to that through which the names of all things subsist, even the designation ‘Prophet,’ for that name is created through Him by what God sends down.” End of quotation.

359 If you are fair-minded, you will perceive from this utterance of the Beauty of the All-Merciful that from which you are veiled today. Through one of the names, whose identity and nature you do not know, you have been deprived of its Creator. A few of you have gathered together and fashioned a figure with the hands of fancy and conjecture, and then devoted yourselves to it. With the hands of ignorance you fashioned the figure of the calf, then took it as a lord for yourselves instead of God. Woe to you, people of idolatry and hypocrisy!

360 We set aside all the verses and words. Today, once again, establish a criterion for the recognition of God and weigh both sides. It is God who should test and weigh His servants, not His servants Him, for God Himself and whatever appears from Him have always been and will always remain sanctified above the standards of humanity. Yet We have consented to this so that truth may be distinguished from falsehood, since you do not regard the divine testimony as testimony and have rejected His proof. Therefore, to make God’s testimony against you complete, it is declared that they should adopt as their standard whatever criterion and proof that group regards as the measure for recognizing God, so that truth may be distinguished from falsehood. This statement is made in accordance with your beliefs, so that no excuse may remain and the Truth may become as manifest, evident, and plain as the sun to every soul. Otherwise, God has always been and will always remain sanctified above the standards of His servants and their proofs, testimonies, and arguments, for God’s standard today is His own Self, and His proof and testimony are whatever has appeared from Him, if you understand.

361 As for what he has suggested in the language of idolatry and rejection—that the appearance of such a person would be futile and all God’s pronouncements false—may God cut out the tongue of the one who prompted you, and may God break his hand, his fingers, and his pen, for he has overstepped his bounds, fabricated lies against God, the All-Compelling, the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the Self-Subsisting, and committed what has violated the sanctity of the Cause among His servants. Even the inhabitants of the nethermost prison will disown him, how much more God’s servants who are near to Him!

362 Say: First, you have not understood those pronouncements and are wholly unaware of their meaning, just as the peoples of the past, through failing to comprehend the meanings of the divine words, held back from the shore of the ocean of oneness at the appearance of the Manifestations of Lordship and were deprived. For example, concerning the signs of the next Manifestation, the Gospel says: “The sun will be darkened on that day, the moon will be deprived of light, and the stars will fall upon the earth.” Because of these very expressions, the people of the Gospel have not, to this day, turned toward the religion of Muhammad. Now, what do you say? Were these pronouncements false, or did those people fail to comprehend their meanings?

363 Likewise, there is the mention of the “Seal of the Prophets” in the Qur’an, and, among the signs of the next Manifestation and the signs of the Resurrection, the splitting of heaven, the cleaving of the earth, the scattering of the mountains, the rising of the dead from outward graves, and similar statements describing the signs of the Resurrection. Today, all are barred by these very expressions from the Sovereign of names and attributes. Now what will you do—affirm or deny them? For, in their outward sense, what was written has not appeared. You must either say that all is false or say that they have not comprehended its meanings. And I do not know what you will say about the explicit designation “Seal of the Prophets,” for the Primal Point has expressly stated that prophets have come from a beginning that has no beginning and will come until an end that has no end, although people have deemed it impossible for anyone to come after the Messenger and claim prophethood.

364 Say: O heedless one! Know that no one has ever been or will ever be acquainted with the true meanings of the divine words except the Manifestation of divine knowledge and wisdom. The Point of the Bayán expressly states that no one has attained or will attain knowledge of the gems of divine meaning concealed in the treasuries of the words of the Bayán except the next Manifestation Himself. Had all peoples comprehended the meanings of the words, they would never have been deprived of the Dawning-Places of revelation and inspiration. Today, likewise, those among the people of the Bayán who remain veiled have been deprived of the Beauty of the All-Merciful through failing to comprehend what was revealed in the Bayán. By God, if they attained even a little awareness, they would renounce their knowledge—indeed, their very selves.

365 O you who are heedless of the heavenly river of meanings! Do not regard the divine words as false or count them as futile. Rather, recognize your own inability to comprehend them, and concerning any that you find yourself unable to understand, ask the Source and Wellspring of innate knowledge, so that you may not remain thirsty for the outpourings of the ocean of meanings or drown and perish in the seas of satanic misgivings. Not a single letter of what has been revealed has been or will ever be futile. Rather, the pearls of the meanings of all things lie concealed in every letter of the divinely revealed words, and none can draw them forth except one possessed of mighty power. And if, God forbid, you wish to discern futility and flaws in the revealed words and manifestations of power, the souls that remain veiled are more deserving of such scrutiny. Say: Set your mirror before your face, that you may behold your skin and know what is in it, so that you may be enabled to return and become repentant, turning back, acknowledging, confessing, and admitting your transgressions and misdeeds. He is indeed the Ever-Forgiving, the Merciful.

366 His words—great is his malice: “As yet, none of His functions or commands have appeared. As for the signs of the appearance of the Qá’im affirmed by the people of the Qur’an, although the signs accompanying His appearance have appeared and people have not understood them, the signs following His appearance have not appeared. If the people of the Qur’an object and ask what the signs following His appearance are, what will the answer be? The failure to understand the signs accompanying His appearance, and the failure of the signs following His appearance to appear, will cause people to be veiled from God, and this is far removed from God. Consider for yourself: when a seed is planted, how can there be another seed, which is its fruit and resurrection, without its roots, leaves, and branches appearing?” End of quotation.

367 It has reached the point where you continually write refutations of yourselves without realizing it. For you have written that the signs of the appearance of the Qá’im appeared at the time of His appearance, but people did not understand them. Now consider why this failure to understand occurred. Undoubtedly it was because the things people imagined did not appear: they imagined meanings for the expressions describing the signs of His appearance, and when they did not observe anything in the material world corresponding to their imaginings, they failed to attain recognition of the Point of the Bayán. You yourself and others like you likewise did not understand until the Revealer of the Bayán interpreted the earlier words. Even after the explanation of the Temple of the All-Merciful, only a few affirmed, while most denied. This is precisely what you and others like you among the people of the Bayán are doing today, for you have not comprehended the meaning of the divine words and unthinkingly cry out that this Manifestation must not appear, since the creation of the Bayán has not yet been completed. Had you understood, you would certainly not have been veiled by these words. You have regarded God as powerless like yourselves. By God, if a soul were certain of God’s power, it would never hesitate over such words, for He is able, through a single word of His, to bring all the Books back and likewise to originate them. He is indeed powerful over whatever He wills, and He is indeed the All-Knowing, the All-Wise.

368 All the signs following the Manifestation have appeared, but you have not understood them, just as, by your own account, the people of the Qur’an did not recognize the signs accompanying His appearance. Concerning every sign that you have deemed necessary after the Manifestation but have not recognized, as you have written, ask the Source of divine knowledge, so that you may become certain that all have appeared, but that such souls have remained veiled from comprehending them all.

369 The principal sign of the Manifestation after the Qāʾim was the appearance of Ḥusayn. By your own account, you acknowledge the first and deny the second. If you reflect on this very statement, you will discover boundless mysteries. Concerning the appearance of Ḥusayn after the Qāʾim, it is written in your own books of traditions that all the Prophets and Messengers will come beneath His banner. It is likewise written that the Qāʾim will return and will also join Him. If you reflect a little on these passages, you will understand that what the books say accords with what has been revealed in the Bayān. Know at least this: all the signs following the Manifestation have appeared; some you have not understood, and others you have denied. Thus have your desires commanded you, if you only knew.

370 Now what do you say concerning the Point of the Bayān—may the souls of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—who mentioned no sign whatsoever and expressly stated that whenever He appears, all must affirm Him, give thanks to God, and look to the Manifestation Himself and to whatever appears from Him? He explicitly says that He is free to appear in whatever manner He wills, and that no one has the right to dispute with Him. He gave the people of the Bayān countless counsels, lest they use the Bayān and what was revealed in it to dispute with the very Person of the All-Merciful. Yet you have advanced your own vain imaginings as arguments and spoken of the completion of the dispensation. God Himself has appeared, uttering the verses of the Point, and says, “Through My appearance the dispensation of the Bayān has been and is being completed.” Yet you are so veiled that you have not considered God Himself to be equal even to His creation, and you say that it must specifically be completed among the creatures. But the Cause of God has not been and will not be completed without Him; rather, it has been and is being completed through His holy Person, if you would only perceive. Everything you have said is an act of rebellion on your part against God, the All-Powerful, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting.

371 I do not know what you would have done or what you would have inflicted had the Point of the Bayān mentioned signs. Has a single word been found in the Bayān saying that if someone appears with divine verses and manifestations of divine glory, you should rise up to kill Him or stain the robe of the glory of divine oneness with your self-inspired fabrications? Rather, throughout the Bayān He says that if someone appears and says, “I am your Beloved,” even without producing any proof, no one has the right to object to Him.

372 And now the Sun of Meanings is shining from the horizon of the grace of the All-Merciful, uttering divine verses in such abundance that no one can count them. Yet some of you say that the previous dispensation has not been completed, and others say that He has appeared too soon.

373 Say: You who are unjust! The All-Glorious Beauty Himself speaks, and the meaning of His words in Persian is this: “People! I was one of you, resting asleep on My bed. The breezes of the All-Merciful blew from the right hand of the glory of divine oneness, awakened Me, and caused Me to voice His praise between the heavens and the earths. People! I swear by God that I have not vaunted Myself over anyone, nor have I denied or do I now deny the verses of God. I acknowledge what you possess of the Bayān, and I am certain of the Lord who created all. Have mercy on Me and do not assail Me. If you will not turn toward this river of divine glory, do not object. People! Had the choice been Mine, I would certainly have concealed Myself from your sight and would not have accepted for Myself what no contingent being has ever borne. For not a day has passed without My suffering abuse, taunts, and ridicule. But the Holy Spirit spoke within My breast, and the Most Great Spirit spoke through My tongue. This is not from Me, but from One whose power encompasses and surpasses all things.”

374 “People! Whether the Manifestation comes sooner or later has never been and is not in either My hands or yours. Rather, it has been and will be in the hand of divine power, held within the grasp of His might. He has brought it forth however He wished. Fear God and do not be among the wrongdoers. People! Drink the Kawthar of meanings from the cup of the words of God. If you inhale from it the holy fragrances of detachment, then be fair, and do not inflict that which would cause the inhabitants of the chambers of Paradise to cry out in anguish. Do not be among the heedless.”

375 “People! I speak truthfully what I have been commanded by your Lord, and the breezes of truth are readily and clearly distinguished from falsehood. Fear God and do not be among those who remain far away. People! I have never assailed anyone, and I have associated with the people of the Bayān with the utmost mercy, kindness, humility, and lowliness. In the days when every heart was troubled by the onslaught and every soul trembled beneath the scourges of wrath, I Myself stood among the enemies. At every moment their arrows struck My breast, and no one but the knowing Lord of the world could count My afflictions. How many nights everyone was at ease while this servant was in chains and fetters! How many days you dwelt in your homes while this servant suffered in the hands of the enemies! You are all witnesses to what has been related, if you speak honestly and are among the truthful.”

376 “People! Are you powerful, or is the Lord of the worlds? If He is powerful, He has brought forth the Manifestation of His own Self in whatever manner He willed. If this is the servant’s sin, it did not originate with Him, but with the Creator of earth and heaven. People! Hear My call, drink in these days the Tasnīm of life from the cup of the All-Merciful, and do not be among the deprived.” End of quotation.

377 This is the meaning of the divine words, which this servant has related according to his understanding, so that you may perhaps recognize that the matter has never been and is not in anyone’s hands, and that no one has known or will know the manner of the Manifestation’s appearance. All are held captive in the grasp of His power and might, and He is the Ruler: He brings forth whatever He wishes. All are commanded, at the Manifestation, to look to the Manifestation Himself, not to words and allusions.

378 By God! When divine verses were descending without interruption from the heaven of the everlasting Will, the one who turned away from God and Siyyid Muḥammad joined forces and devised a plan, hoping to deprive people of this Salsabīl through certain fabrications. Thus one rushed to Istanbul, while the other busied himself continually in this land with fabricated letters. By God, besides whom there is no other god, He has treated those two souls and everyone else with the utmost kindness and compassion. It is astonishing that, although you yourself have heard of the qualities of the Blessed Beauty and most of the people of the region have seen them with their own eyes, you have nevertheless erased them all from your heart because of a few fabricated words from the tree of corruption, and have risen up with such vehemence in rejection and opposition. We ask God to enable you to recognize His own Self and to detach you from allusions, so that you may discover what you have been veiled from and be among those who attain.

379 If you learned of the circumstances of these servants and what has happened during these years of residence in Adrianople, and were fair-minded, you would weep and lament. The one God is witness that in every hour of those years an upheaval has arisen, for at every moment that person whom, through Satan’s seduction, you regard as God instead of God has committed some abominable deed or shameful act. I adjure you by the God who created all things through His will: can you imagine that He would withhold bread from someone associated with Him? But what use is it? You were not here and are not here. Solely to discredit the Cause, they rushed to the Great City for a morsel of bread, and how many complaints they have made in this land! By God, I do not lie, and I have no ulterior purpose. But my heart burns because you have regarded such souls as the Lord and have deprived yourselves and the people of the unapproachable True One. These are the last days of these servants. Although you have imagined that we came to this land for worldly ends, God is witness to how we came and what our purpose was. You yourselves know that this servant was not in need of bread in his own dwelling, that he should run from city to city for bread. Rather, the breezes of the All-Merciful have drawn these servants onward, if you are among those who understand.

380 In short, they have ruined their own reputation, that of these servants, and that of the Cause—all of them. Yet you counsel this servant and write admonitions asking why he has not come to believe in such souls and is not certain of them. Their baseness has reached the point that they have submitted a fabricated list stating how many of the emigrants have left and how many still reside in this land. They have cut off what used to reach some of the poor and have entered it in their own names. By God, it would be better to eat Zaqqūm than such bread! Indeed, they have sent to this land the details of the list of persons submitted there, and no one knew of those details except the tree of corruption and those with him.

381 If only they had entered this remainder in their own names as well! By God, we servants would have been grateful. Know at least this much: by the truth of the True One, most of the emigrants have no clothes. Yet they have attributed worldly motives to these souls, who set out for God’s sake and turned toward this region with the Beauty of Divine Unity, weeping and lamenting, while claiming detachment for themselves. If only you knew them, what they possess, and what they have committed in this vain life to preserve their positions of leadership, and were among those who are informed!

382 As for what you wrote about planting the seed and your explanation of it, all these words suggest that you have not understood a single letter of divine knowledge and have not known what the purpose of revelation has been or will be. Now, passing over the most exalted analogy, you have spoken through the lowliest analogy in protest against God. Let us concede that it is just as you have written and stated. Has this seed needed watering, or does it not? He says: “I am that blowing of the winds of divine unity that comes from the right hand of the Paradise of ‘Know God through God’; the nurture of all things has depended, and will depend, upon this glorious, merciful breeze and this holy, spiritual breath.” And He says: “I am that life-giving Kawthar through which the seeds of understanding and inner meanings, and the ears of divine knowledge and wisdom, sprout.” Without the blowing of these winds and this sweet, refreshing water, no seed of knowledge has sprouted, or will sprout, in the paradise of human hearts. Yet you speak and write, and do not perceive what you are saying.

383 Set aside this [corrupted original text] that associates partners with God, and look with the eye of divine unity, so that you may see all the Manifestations as one soul and the religious laws of all as one law. All these differences have arisen, and will arise, according to the requirements of time and circumstance and the capacities of the manifestations of existence. God has had good, blessed, holy soils in which, when the seed is sown, the appearance of further ears of grain is witnessed in less than the blink of an eye. This station is the realm of command, which creation is powerless to comprehend. As He says: “His command, when He desires a thing, is only to say to it, ‘Be,’ and it is.” It is of these soils that He says: “Its vegetation comes forth by the permission of its Lord.” But the soils of souls that associate partners with God never bring forth anything except what the True One—exalted be His mention—has announced in His exalted words: “It brings forth only scantily.” Even if, as you would have it, the revelation were delayed a hundred thousand years, good ears of grain have never grown, and will never grow, from the barren soils of those souls.

384 Of course you will say that the cycle has not ended or reached completion. Woe, then, to those who recognize God’s favor and then deny it! You see with your own eyes that ears of hidden meanings have sprung from the seeds of divine words and stand verdant and flourishing in the Paradise of Divine Unity. Yet you have denied, and continue to deny. Perhaps, however, you have neither seen nor heard and are unaware. God willing, this is so, and you are not among those who have seen with their own eyes and denied, and heard with their own ears and turned away.

385 Apart from all this, His most holy Essence has always been, and will remain, sanctified above analogies, for likenesses and comparisons have never had, and do not have, access to that seat. Analogies are mentioned only with regard to human understanding, so that through these limited comparisons people may perhaps comprehend some divine matters according to their capacity. If the unapproachable True One so wills, in a single instant, with one breath of the winds of grace, He can adorn all withered trees with the raiment of spring; with one word He brings forth all the seeds of existence as ears of wisdom and knowledge. It is as though you have placed a mirror before yourself and have attributed, and continue to attribute, to the True One everything you observe in yourself: impotence, ignorance, contrary dispositions, and signs of foolishness. Repent to God, you who are asleep! If only you could be counted among the sleeping, for a sleeping person is himself at rest, and people are at rest from him. As has been said, no deed has been or is better for a tyrant than sleep, since while he sleeps both he and the people are at rest. Reflect on your injustice, you tyrant whose injustice has caused all things to lament and the souls of the sanctified to tremble. Fear God and do not follow your desires. Cast away what you possess, then take hold of what is with God through a power from Him, so that the gaze of providence may return to you and He may make you one of the fair-minded.

386 The words of him whose speech caused the tribes of the worlds to lament: “Another submission is this: are the praises of the Báb directed toward the particular person of Bahá, toward a specifically designated individual, or toward the name Bahá? If they are directed toward a specifically designated individual and a particular person, this is the very point at issue and requires proof. Explain where in the Bayán this appears and in what words. If they are directed toward the name Bahá, this name is one of the names of God. Praise of the names of God is not confined to the name Bahá. He also praised the names Jalál and Jamál, and likewise all the names of God. Thus every Mírzá Kamál and Mírzá Jamál should lay claim to this Cause. Moreover, Dayyán, Mírzá Ghawghá, Shaykh Ismáʿíl, and Ḥájí Mullá Háshim also laid claim to this Cause, and their claims have been shown to be false. A mere claim, therefore, will not suffice; otherwise, these gentlemen must be that very Promised One.” End of quotation.

387 Say: Restrain your pen, you who have been deprived of the robe of courtesy and fairness! Through what has flowed from your pen, you have violated the sanctity of God among His servants. Now your pen disowns you, as does the tablet on which you inscribed what you inscribed. By God, had your ear been attentive, you would have heard, while you were occupied with these statements, the lament of the company on high, then the outcry of the chosen ones, then the mourning of the holy ones, then the cry of all things created by the word of God, the King, the Transcendent, the Most High, the Most Great. Hold your tongue, enemy of God, restrain your pen, and leave your tablet! Be ashamed before God, who created you by a command from Him for this Revelation, which has appeared in truth and through which the heavens of your certitude have been torn apart, you heedless company! Do you imagine that anything escapes His knowledge? No, by the Lord of the worlds!

388 When what flowed from your pen came forth, the eye of ʿAlí wept in the heaven of decree. He gazed upon you and addressed you: “Wretched one! God has blackened your face, for you have committed what no one among the former generations committed and no soul among the later generations will commit. Did We not counsel you in all the Tablets not to say, at the time of the Revelation, ‘Why?’ and ‘By what means?’ Yet you, deceitful unbeliever, have made a mockery of the Cause of God. You have struck, and continue at every moment to strike, My body with the swords of malice and hypocrisy, without perceiving it, while imagining yourself among those who are steadfast in My Cause. With your arguments you seek to establish what appeared from Me, yet at every moment you slay My beloved Self, and you feel no shame before God, to whom the affairs of the worlds return. Through your deeds I have been put to shame before the company of those brought near, then before God’s sanctified chosen ones, then before His elect Prophets.”

389 “By God, because you have associated yourself with the Bayán, its letters, words, realities, and meanings have wept, while you rejoice, laugh, play, and join the mockers. Soon God will seize you with His overpowering might and His crushing force. He is indeed the most severe of avengers. Have you not reflected within yourself on the testimony through which you believed in Me, and the proof through which you associated yourself with Me? Thus everyone in the heavens and on earth has disowned, and will disown, you, and My own Self is witness to what I say. Has not the One raised up among you appeared with My verses, My revelation, My sovereignty, My majesty, My testimony, My proof, and My evidence? Why have you disbelieved in Him while acknowledging Me? No, by My own all-knowing, all-informed Self! You have not believed in Me, nor in My verses, nor in what descended in the eternity of eternities from the dominion of God, the King, the All-Powerful, the Most High, the All-Knowing.”

390 Be fair, then, heedless one, and for one hour devote yourself sincerely to God, your Lord and the Lord of the worlds. If you do not believe in the One who recites God’s verses to you at every moment, do not assail Him. Fear God! He has dispensed with your belief and the belief of those like you, if only you could perceive it. All things have been put to shame before God because of the relationship between you and them, for all ultimately rest beneath the shadow of His name, the Maker. Such is the matter, if you are among those who perceive. Thus all things will surely disown your association with them and seek refuge in God from you, you whose deeds have set ablaze the hearts of the Prophets and Messengers.

391 Your purpose in what flowed from your pen was nothing but mockery and derision of God. Yet you mock yourself and your leaders without perceiving it, just as servants before you mocked God’s emissaries and His trusted ones. You have followed in their wake with a deed that has shaken the foundations of assurance and the pillars of certitude, convulsed stillness itself, and caused the supports of a mighty throne to tremble.

392 Listen to My words and do not overstep your bounds. Know yourself, you who associate partners with God and His verses, dispute with His own Self, and wage war against His Being. Enough of what you have committed! By God, your deed has unleashed the tempests of vengeance upon the worlds. Soon they will seize you and your allies. There is no God but Him. His are power and might; His are majesty and the freedom to choose. He sends down whatever He wills by a command from Himself. He is indeed the All-Powerful, the All-Subduing.

393 All things marvel at what could have prompted you, at the instigation of that rejected soul, to rise up with such audacity and insolence to wage war against the Beauty of Divine Unity. Of that most unapproachable, most holy, most great and most ancient Beauty, the Primal Point of the Bayán—may the souls of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—says: “My allusion cannot reach Him, nor can any allusion made by anyone refer to Him, so exalted is His station, so great His glory, and so lofty His majesty.” Yet in mockery of Him you have written what no soul has ever dared to commit or write. It is astonishing that you feel no shame and still make mention of the Point of the Bayán. On what evidence and proof, after all, have you deemed such insolence permissible?

394 This servant’s bewilderment is of little consequence: all the inhabitants of the Concourse on high are bewildered by the likes of you. How quickly you have disbelieved in the very One in whom you professed faith! Granted that you have not understood this Cause, surely, in the face of all these manifestations of divine glory and divinely revealed verses, you should at least withhold judgment. Indeed, if you look to the Bayán, you have never had, nor do you now have, any warrant whatsoever to object to such a Being, whatever He may say.

395 Shame on those who have breathed into you and filled your breast with malice and hatred toward God! Alas, alas! A lament that no pen can record, a lament that no utterance can fully express, for what you have done to please one from whom Sijjín in its very being, hell in its reality, and fire in its blazing and flaming flee.

396 Throughout the Bayán, He has forbidden the people of the Bayán to assail or dispute with one another. He says that if you wish to debate with one another using evidence and proofs, you should state and write down your evidence and proofs with the utmost courtesy and modesty, lest that debate bring sorrow upon that Essence of Glory and Sun of Meanings in the days of His manifestation and radiance. Thus He says—exalted be His majesty: “And when you wish to debate using evidence and proof, write down your evidence with the utmost modesty, and then speak with the greatest courtesy.” Further on He says—exalted be His remembrance: “Lest you encounter God, your Lord, and commit a deed that grieves God, your Lord, by grieving the Manifestation of His own Self, while you neither take heed nor remember.” Here ends the quotation.

397 If only you had observed even this much of the counsel of that Beloved of all creation! No, by God, you have not acted upon a single letter of the Bayán except what you have found to accord with your own desires. We seek refuge in God from this sin, for His words are sanctified above your selves, your desires, your knowledge, and your understanding. If only you had confined yourself to disputing with the Manifestation of the Lord of Lords! You showed neither modesty nor regard for courtesy. You have committed a deed such that, from this day until the day that has no end, every person possessed of courtesy and modesty who looks at what you have written will be ashamed and abashed.
