Yet today everyone endowed with sight must sanctify the heart from all allusions, indications, and names, and look to the Revelation itself and whatever proceeds from him. All have been commanded to do this in the Bayān, for names and invocations have all been, and will remain, his creation. Today most of the people of the Bayān have halted at the obstacle of names and are seen and counted before God among their worshippers. In God’s sight, the people of the Most Great Vista are those souls who, in this springtime of the glory of singleness, have gathered with their own hands the fruits of the divine Tree and eaten them. Those who cling to anything other than this Tree are rejected before God and numbered among the people of negation and fire.

488 At this moment the Spirit called out, “Write to the servants: No injustice greater than this can be seen—that someone should ask the Manifestation of the Truth for evidence and proof other than what has appeared from him and descended from his presence. Look to the Truth. If that proof by which the authority of the Primal Point was established is seen and has appeared from God, there is no longer any room for hesitation. In that case, no excuse from anyone has been or will be accepted, even if he clings to the words of the first and the last and adduces them as evidence. Thus does God explain his Cause, if you understand.”

489 As for your statement that Dayyān, Mīrzā Ghawghā, Shaykh Ismāʿīl, and Ḥājī Mullā Hāshim made claims and proved false, and so forth: the idolaters raised objections like these at the beginning of the Revelation of the Bayān. They used to say that a few people had made this claim before the Sayyid, the Bāb, and had been false; therefore—God forbid—this assertion too was like the assertions of those people. With such words they turned away from the Truth. At every Revelation, the idolaters of that age have always occupied themselves with objections of this kind. Yet such objections have never kept, and will never keep, an insightful knower or a discerning, detached soul from the path of God. Rather, they will strengthen the firmness and steadfastness of upright souls. It has been abundantly clear that the greater the Revelation and the more manifest the Truth, the fiercer has been, and will be, the rejection of the letters of negation, as can be seen. But as for these souls you have mentioned: first, how has their falsity become clear to you?

490 As for Dayyān, who was named in the Book of God by all his most beautiful names, he never made any claim that was contrary. In the first year of his cause, several prayers of his were sent to the Most Holy Court, and in those tablets nothing was seen but expressions of humility, reverence, and faith in God and the Manifestations of his Cause. Yet when the well-known individual saw that someone had appeared and written prayers, the fire of his envy blazed so fiercely that he issued a ruling for the killing of him and his loved ones. Thus, through his ruling, they martyred one who, according to the explicit text of the Point of the Bayān, was a manifestation of oneness. Afterward, in his own book, he attributed fabrications to him that had no truth whatsoever, although statements explicitly concerning him, surpassing what he had claimed, had been revealed by the Revealer of the Bayān. Look, then, into the Bayān, so that you may be among those who know.

491 There have been a number of these souls whom the well-known individual rejected, pronouncing them all unbelievers. Before God, those souls have not been, and will not be, rejected. Indeed, a single hair of theirs is better than him, if you are among those who see clearly. Among them is the honored Āqā Sayyid Ibrāhīm—upon him be the glory of God, the All-Glorious—concerning whom verses were revealed. Among those verses is the passage cited here, so that perhaps you may understand and realize that no one was looking to the Primal Point and what descended from him. Rather, out of love for his leadership and to preserve his standing, he committed what no one had committed from the beginning of creation until now. His words—exalted be his glory—are: “Bear witness, O Ibrāhīm, that you were, on a day, the throne of the revelation of your Lord, and we were indeed manifest before and afterward. Behold, we have created you, sustained you, caused you to die, and brought you to life up to this moment, while those who were given the scriptures remain veiled to this moment. When you came before God, your Lord, the Lord of what is seen and what is unseen, the Lord of the worlds, you heard the voice of those who follow your cause, while they suppose that they rise to exalted heights in their love for you. Say: No, indeed, no! I and those who followed me have been gathered before God, my Lord, on the day when, as Moses, I was the throne of God’s revelation, among the believers. These people do not follow; had they followed me, they would have believed in Moses before Jesus, then in Muḥammad after Jesus, then in the Point of the Bayān on the Day of Resurrection.”

492 Now consider how he has called him a throne of revelation, saying, “Behold, we have created you, sustained you, caused you to die, and brought you to life at this time. Those souls who had believed in you in the scriptures remain veiled until now. We heard the call of the souls who followed your cause and supposed that through their love for you they had attained exalted stations. Say: No, indeed, no! I was gathered before God, together with the souls who followed me, when the throne of revelation appeared in the name of Moses. Those veiled souls did not follow me. Had they followed me, they would assuredly have attained the subsequent Manifestations and would have acknowledged and believed in those holy souls.”

493 Say: O deaf one of this age! Pay close attention and give heed to the words of God, which explicitly, without any allusion, have called him the throne of revelation. Beyond this firm and exalted station, all names circle around him. Whether he addresses him as “You are God,” “He is God,” “from God,” “to God,” “the Essence of God,” or “the Being of God,” all these stations are established and confirmed in his rank. Yet today the people of the Bayān call this very soul Abū al-Davāhī, at the decree of the well-known individual. They have assailed him so severely, and the verdict declaring him an unbeliever has been so widely circulated, that he has now withdrawn from everyone for a long time. Indeed, God’s curse rests upon the oppressors.

494 Now consider how far they have exceeded all bounds in the Cause of God, and what deeds they have committed in rebellion against God. He then says, addressing that glorious name—these are his words, exalted be his glory: “O my friend in the Scriptures! There has been no limit to the thrones of God’s revelation, either before or afterward, but the people are veiled from the mystery of the Cause. And O my remembrance in the Books after the Scriptures! There has been nothing in the thrones but what points to God, their Lord. Say: All are from God and return to God. O my name in the Bayān! Consider how you will watch for my guides in every revelation. Until that time, I had not opened the gate of the name in any previous revelation. This is a grace from God to those in the Bayān, but the people do not know.”

495 The very soul to whom he explicitly says, “You are my friend in the Scriptures, my remembrance in all the heavenly Books, and my name in the Bayān,” they call Abū al-Davāhī, and they are unaware. Say: God has taken away your hearing, your sight, and your hearts, and you neither hear, nor see, nor understand. Read the book Mostayqeẓ, among the writings of the one who has turned away from God and whom he has taken as his lord, and see what accusations he has made against these holy temples. Matters became so difficult that, when this name once came to Iraq, they all sought to kill him as well. The All-Glorious Beauty restrained the people day and night until he returned from that land to his own home safe and sound. Such was the matter, and God is witness to what I say. Praise be to God that everyone has his book Mostayqeẓ; otherwise, by the One who created all beings out of nothing, he would have denied it, just as he has denied most of his deeds.

496 Would that he were mentioned before God as being of the rank of even one of these souls whom he has counted as rejected! You have made delusion a lord for yourself instead of God, and you do not realize what you are saying. But God witnesses what you have committed in this vain life, and he will soon seize you with a might from himself. He is indeed the most severe of avengers. Yet you have regarded such neglectful souls as followers of the Bayān, while counting as one who has abandoned it the very soul through whose revelation the Bayān appeared. By God, you have spoken only out of desire, and you walk only in the wilderness of delusion and conceit, while imagining yourself to be among the rightly guided.

497 Moreover, these souls did not appear with divine verses. If you say that they possessed verses and were counted as rejected, this is rebellion on your part against God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting. For if one who possesses verses is false, no faith will remain for you or anyone on earth. The faith of all is established through the Manifestations of God’s Cause, and those Manifestations of the Cause appeared with verses. If, God forbid, one who possesses verses is false, by what proof will you establish the truth of the Point of the Bayān? Grave is the word that comes from your mouths, O company of those who turn away! It is clear that you have mentioned these souls solely to disparage the Cause of God, and have also compared this revelation to those souls. Heedless one! Know that those souls have not been, and will not be, rejected before God. Rather, the rejected one is he who denied them, assailed them, and decreed their death.

498 Among the souls he has mentioned is the honored Ḥājī Mollā Hāshem. The petitions he sent to the seat of the throne still exist, and nothing but the utmost humility has appeared from him. O claimant to spiritual knowledge! You have disparaged all the mystics. It has been heard that one of the philosophers was once in the land of Ṣād, and the religious scholars of that land pronounced that poor, helpless man an unbeliever and ordered him to leave. A group attacked that poor man; among them was the well-known Mollā ʿAlī Ḥakīm of Nūr. At that moment the wronged man said nothing to any of the religious scholars, but turned to the philosopher just mentioned and said a word that it is not pleasing to repeat. His meaning was: You consider yourself a philosopher; why do you assail a philosopher? Now, with all these objections, you also claim wisdom and spiritual knowledge! Turn in repentance to God, who created and fashioned you, and do not assail those who have believed in God and his verses, and who have suffered in his path what no one in all the worlds can reckon. Affirming or denying souls on the basis of imitation has never sufficed, nor will it ever suffice. Many a curse returns upon the one who utters it, and many a rejection returns upon the one who rejects, while he remains unaware of it within himself and is among the heedless.

499 Say: Die in your rage! The idol of delusion and desire has been shattered. By God, besides whom there is no god, he has clung to a delusion without parallel in creation. I do not know by what evidence or proof you have preferred the person who rejected those souls. Hear the counsel of this servant and never reject anyone, for the Point of the Bayān specifically enjoined everyone not to assail anyone. Thus, although the well-known individual—by God, besides whom there is no god—sought with the utmost cunning and calculation to cut down the divine Lote Tree, and what lay [corrupted original text] hidden in his heart was disclosed, he nevertheless did not assail him until some papers from him were brought into his presence. They were all in rejection of God and his verses, and they still exist in his own handwriting. Thus divine verses descended from the dominion of the Cause in rejection of the one who rejected God. This was not from himself, but from God, the All-Powerful, the All-Protecting, the Almighty, the Self-Subsisting.

500 Verses belong exclusively to the bearer of a revelation, as the Point of the Bayān has explicitly stated: after the setting of the Sun of Truth, no one will hear the verses of God until the next revelation. This same well-known individual would stand in his presence through the nights and at dawn, and divine verses were imparted to him. Afterward, on the strength of merely two words, he became so conceited that he committed what none of the manifestations of negation had committed. Anyone with even a little understanding knows that he was always in the service of the All-Glorious One, and if he understood anything at all, it was a sprinkling bestowed upon him from the depths of this surging ocean. Yet you have written what no rational person has written. By God, if a hundred thousand like him could speak in his presence! These servants have witnessed it with their own eyes, whereas you speak from sheer delusion. In any case, may God curse that satan who has led both him and you astray, and who has attributed so many fabrications to the True One for the sake of outward leadership. God willing, we hope that he will not be granted the wheat of Ray.

501 Furthermore, I do not know what Mīrzā Ghawghā has done that such hatred of him has taken root in the hearts of people like him, to the point that they write treatises against him. Many souls utter words when overcome by spiritual delight and longing; he too has said something. They should not seek so fiercely to dishonor God’s servants. Fear God and do not wound the hearts of his servants. Nothing has appeared from people like you from which even a trace of the fragrance of the True One can be perceived. We ask God to free your hearts from rancor toward his loved ones. He is indeed powerful over all things.

502 The words of the instigator—great is his aversion and immense his hypocrisy: “If the claimant to this station has a proof other than verses, please state it. If it is verses, Mīrzā Yaḥyā, in addition to an explicit text and decisive proofs, stands opposed with a higher, greater, and better claim, and has many volumes in hand.” End of quotation.

503 By God, he has spoken words that one is ashamed to read. How quickly you have changed God’s standard and violated his sanctity, and how quickly you have set about distorting his words! By God, everyone with discernment has perceived, and continues to perceive, your standing and that of your instigator from what you have written. Woe to him and to those who have followed him! This servant is bewildered as to which of these absurdities to hear and answer. In the end, during these few brief days of life, what has driven you to occupy yourself with these fanciful, self-centered ravings at Satan’s prompting, and to remain deprived of the shore of the ocean of divine unity? Suppose that all souls took him as a lord instead of God: what benefit would come to you and him? None, by the One in whose hand is my soul! Rather, it would be loss in this world and the next.

504 You have written that if the bearer of this revelation has anything other than verses, he should produce it. This explicitly contradicts what the Point of the Bayān—may the spirit of all else be sacrificed for him—has revealed throughout the Bayān. Whoever has spoken or speaks these words has been cursed and is cursed by every atom and everyone in the kingdoms of command and creation. Yes, by God, you have taken people for fools, for if people were not fools, you and that soul who has turned away would not have been able to speak openly in terms other than those God has spoken. The Point of the Bayān has explicitly stated throughout the Bayān that the proof of the next revelation has been and will be nothing other than verses, yet you have explicitly written that if he has a proof other than verses, he should produce it. You say what neither of you understands.

505 The station you have sought to establish for the one who has turned away from God depended on the words of the Primal Point, and the Primal Point was established by divine verses. Now reflect on how far you have strayed from the path of the True One and fallen into the wilderness of error. Reflect on what you have done and said, so that you may become aware of your deed and be among those who seek forgiveness from God, who created all things by his command, brought forth the Manifestation of his own Self through his sovereignty, and kept the hem of his robe sanctified from you, your suppositions, and your imaginings, and from the imaginings of the idols you have taken as a lord instead of God. Woe to them for the punishment of a barren day!

506 By God, you feel secure in the fools of the Bayān, and you have reason to. Now we will set verses aside: whatever that well-known soul may produce, if something surpassing it does not appear from the source of divine power, we will submit to your fabrications and those of that soul who associates partners with God. Yet, by God, you do not stand firm in this matter either, nor will you, for we know that well-known soul: in claims and pretensions, greater than anyone great, and in manifestation and action, smaller than anyone small. O destitute pauper, if only you had at least taken someone like yourself as a lord! But what use is it? You appear utterly heedless. Otherwise, I would have mentioned certain matters so that you might fully comprehend this illusion and become aware of his deceit.

507 As for your statement, “If it is verses, Mīrzā Yaḥyā stands opposed with a higher, greater, and better claim, and has many volumes in hand”—how low his standing, and how grand his words! This claim from that source of pride, self, and desire is like an ant in a barren valley claiming lordship; indeed, to everyone with illumined sight it is even greater, for his very being was created through a single letter of these verses of eternal glory, and whatever words have issued from him have been at his own level. Open your eyes, then, so that you may recognize this. What he possesses will never be mentioned before God unless he returns to him, repents, and becomes one of those who repent and seek forgiveness. By God, he has committed what no one before him has committed.

508 Now judge fairly for yourself: what prompted these words you have written, that he stands opposed with a higher, greater, and better claim? Any ignorant person would be ashamed of this statement, let alone a person of understanding. O sightless one, the very fact that you have written that he stands opposed is itself a clear proof of his falsity. Those with a sanctified sense of smell perceive in these very words the odors of pride, arrogance, and turning away from the True One. It seems you have never even read the Qurʾān, which declares—exalted are his words: “Who is more unjust than one who invents a lie against God, or says, ‘It has been revealed to me,’ when nothing has been revealed to him, or one who says, ‘I will send down the like of what God has sent down’?” End of quotation. Reflect a little on this revealed verse, so that you may become certain that whenever anyone says, “I will send down the like of what the True One has sent down,” this very statement has been and will be what invalidates his claim and proves him false.

509 O truly deaf one, hear the song of the divine Dove, who has sung upon the highest branches of the Tree of the Bayān, saying: “If you were to appear at this moment, I have been and will be the first to bow down and the first to believe in you.” Yet you have felt no shame or embarrassment before this utterance of the Tongue of the All-Merciful, and have explicitly written that he stands opposed with a higher, greater, and better claim. By God, these very words are a manifest proof of the aversion, unbelief, and hypocrisy of such souls. He also says—exalted be his mention: “If all the people of the Bayān were to become like him in the essence of knowledge, it would bear no fruit except through acknowledgment of him. And it is perfectly clear that no one can be like him in the essence of knowledge except the Point himself.” That divine Sun speaks thus of himself, while you have occupied yourself with such unworthy statements on behalf of a soul whose cause you wish falsely to establish through the words of that Sun of Truth. Alas for you, for him, and for those who have followed you both! By God, they are in nothing but manifest error.

510 Besides this, you write that he has many volumes in hand. This statement is exactly like the statements of those who turn away at every revelation, who have taken pride in their own writings and compositions and objected to the Sovereign of names and attributes. If books could make a soul self-sufficient, then surely the books on the face of the earth at the time of the revelation of the Point of the Bayān—may the spirit of all else be sacrificed for him—would have made people self-sufficient and free of need. No, by God! All are poor at the gate of his wealth, all are ignorant at the gate of his knowledge, and all are powerless before the manifestation of his power. He, in his own Self, is indeed independent of all else, and he is indeed the All-Powerful over all worlds.

511 I ask you yourself to judge fairly: if one person possesses a single ruby and another a hundred thousand donkey-loads of pebbles, which do you consider rich and free of need? Even if a hundred thousand books were to appear, they have borne and will bear no fruit except through acknowledgment of the revelation, for words are like a niche, and the love and remembrance of God are like a lamp. If that niche remains without the lamp, it will be a useless object. Words are good when the fragrances of divine love can be perceived in them; when they lack this quality, they are regarded as the lowliest words of the people of the earth. Fear God, set none of his servants against him, and mention no one other than him in the court of his holiness. Tear through the veils, then speak the praise of your Lord among all peoples.

512 Here listen to the words of the Revealer of the Bayān, who gave this counsel to one of the Letters of the Living. He says—exalted be his mention: “If you recite or write down a single verse from the verses of that Essence of Eternity, it will be more precious in the sight of God than if you were to write down the whole Bayān, for on that day that single verse will save you, while the whole Bayān will not. Similarly, if someone in the time of the Messenger of God had written down all the heavenly books and everything written under their shadow, he would have been judged as nothing; but if he had written down a single verse of the Qurʾān, he would have been judged a believer. This is the essence of knowledge and action.” End of quotation.

513 Now consider how lofty and immense is the greatness of the Cause, and how low the station and standing of you, your words, and the words of those who associate partners with God. The shining Sun will not remain concealed by these veils, nor will the Manifestation himself be defiled by the stain of these words. He has ever been sanctified from you and your words, and he himself is God’s proof among his creatures. Those on earth should follow what appears from him, not what they themselves possess. This is indeed the truth, and beyond the truth there is nothing but error, if you are among those who understand.

514 Many a donkey has carried numerous volumes without itself understanding a single letter of them, as this likeness has been mentioned in earlier books and explicitly revealed in the perspicuous Book. By God, besides whom there is no god, the volumes you speak of are fit only to be washed away with water, or for someone to return them to their source, for in his own estimation it ought to be a grave for his words. These servants have seen many of those volumes. If only you would shatter the idol of illusion and look with the eye of insight at his fabricated words! By God, every discerning person of understanding laughs at him and at what has issued from him.

515 Another statement had been put forward by that source of arrogance and pride—his words, abased be his standing: “That is easy. He claims, ‘I will write in a single day all the verses the claimant has written during his lifetime, better, finer, and more eloquent, so that any child can tell the difference.’” End of quotation.

516 All your virtues and his have become evident from this one statement. This servant is bewildered as to what to say here, for he has uttered words such that, by God, even Pharaoh never spoke their like before Moses. By the one, incomparable God, if only he could speak before a single one of those who circle around, let alone before the source, manifestation, and fountainhead of divine outpourings! Now put this very assertion to the test. That opponent is present, and the servants of the House are also present. If he can confront the servants in any respect, we will acknowledge you in everything you have said. Yet speaking at length has never been, and will never be, a proof of truth. Since you have adduced these imaginary proofs in objection to God, this servant speaks these words in reply, so that no excuse, testimony, demonstration, or proof may remain to you. Otherwise, the most holy, most pure court has always been, and will always remain, sanctified above all these proofs, references, words, allusions, and the meaning to which you have clung.

517 O unaware one! Thus far, the equivalent of what has descended from the heaven of utterance has been revealed, yet all that is in the heavens and on earth cannot equal a single verse. If you are looking to such matters, then come into my presence, so that you may see that, when the verses descend, no one can manage to write them down. Put it to the test, you who would test, although it is not fitting for anyone to test God. Rather, He tests His servants and tries them, just as He tried you and returned you to the lowest depths of hell.

518 To every mature, rational person, these proofs of yours have always been, and will always be, like the words of children. You have made the Cause of God a diversion, a game, a mockery, and an object of ridicule. God will therefore seize you with a subduing power from Himself. He is indeed the All-Subduing, the All-Powerful, the Omnipotent. The odors of your proof and argument have withered the fragrant flowers of the paradise of divine proof and demonstration. If you yourself were to consider your words fairly for less than a moment, you would recognize their ugliness in less than a moment. Have you ever heard, from the beginning of creation until now, of anyone opposing the True One with anything like what you have used to oppose Him in this book of Sijjín of yours? No, by the Lord of the worlds! The eye of creation has never witnessed anyone more veiled than you, nor the eye of origination anyone more heedless. Yet you rejoice in what you have written and are among the exultant. We ask God to change your joy into the greatest sorrow, unless you repent, return to Him, and become one of the repentant.

519 Again, at the prompting of that insinuating idolater, he has written: “Stranger still, he himself claims that his expressions are verses, and his messengers say that even his seven-year-old children utter verses. How, then, will the verses of Him Whom God will make manifest establish His authority, when His creatures also utter verses and his little son also utters verses? If they say that verses can appear both from the station of the creatures and the letters, and from the original Point and Tree of Truth, then the verse itself will not prove the truth of the original Tree. Something else is needed; let it be explained what that is.” End of quotation.

520 Yes, if He wishes to cause every soul to speak His praise, He is able to do so. He is indeed the All-Powerful, the Omnipotent. If He wishes to bring forth a soul endowed with all the verses, He is able to do so through His sovereignty, and no one has the right to object to Him. God is witness to what I say. He has caused a child among the children to speak, that he might be a sign pointing to Him and a proof of His own Self. This is a matter for which you should thank and praise God, yet you have opened your mouths in objection to this Remembrance, who has appeared under the name of the All-Glorious between the heavens and the earths.

521 Now consider: has any ignorant person ever made, or does any make, such an objection? No, by God, except you and those like you, who have been created from the air of selfish desire, the water of rejection, the fire of envy, and the dust of hatred. Then die in your rage! He has appeared in truth, and there is no turning Him back. Your Lord, the All-Merciful, has taken it upon Himself to bring forth throughout existence guides who point to Him. They will assuredly arise between the heavens and the earth and speak His praise, and the banners of victory will be raised among all creatures. These are servants whom God has made pure of all besides Him, detached from what you possess, and steadfast in holding to the firm, mighty cord of God. Neither the allusions of those on earth nor the arguments of the idolaters can hold them back.

522 Say: O ignorant one! You have failed to distinguish affliction from blessing, have counted pure good as evil, and have regarded the essence of mercy and justice as injustice itself. Yes, a child among the children of the House spoke the verses of God at the age of six, and that child is now present. By God, that very tree of corruption himself repeatedly said that what had come from him had never appeared from anyone, from the beginning of creation until now, and he spent most nights and days praising him. Now matters have reached such a point that, not content with turning away from the exalted True One, he has not left even the children alone, nor will he do so. They will not desist until they have dyed the earth with this pure blood. This is what has been revealed in the Tablets, if you are among those who look. He has repeatedly declared: “We have caused him to speak verses in childhood, that he might be a proof of this merciful Revelation and this holy, eternal Dawning.” Yet you have counted this supreme mercy as an affliction and, instead of thanking the exalted True One, have begun to complain.

523 As for his statement that “he himself claims that his expressions are verses,” he has used the word “expressions,” imagining that he is mocking God. Restrain yourself, O ignorant one! This is a station beyond the reach of your hands and the hands of the idolaters. The Point of the Bayán declares that everything existing on earth has always been, and will always be, His signs, let alone what has descended from Him and appeared from His presence. The True One says: Know that this is indeed the One who appeared before and will appear hereafter. Whoever makes a distinction between Him and anyone who has appeared or will appear has disbelieved in God and His signs. If he possessed keen sight, he would behold all Revelations in this Revelation, which has appeared in truth with manifest verses.

524 Know that all the testimonies and proofs manifested by all the Prophets and Messengers from the beginning that has no beginning have been proofs of this Revelation. Whatever constitutes a proof in this Revelation has always been, and will always be, the proof of the proofs of the past and the future. Yet We specifically caused this child to speak verses so that certain souls who have received a sprinkling of the boundless outpourings of this Most Great Ocean would not exalt themselves arrogantly above the Revealer of verses. The person to whom you cling was continually in Our presence, yet remained deprived of a sprinkling from the Ocean of Oceans and returned to his abode in the fire. It is indeed the abode of the wicked, and wretched is the abode of these evildoers, if you are among those who are certain.

525 Know that an immeasurable difference is evident between the sun and its radiance upon walls and stones. Even weak sight perceives it, let alone keen sight. Moreover, the image of the sun is seen in an outward mirror. Can it be said that this is the very sun in heaven? Rather, a difference as great as that between earth and heaven is evident, and none denies this except one who is treacherous and detested. Likewise, today, if you refine your hearing and cleanse and purify it of what you have heard from the idolaters, you will find the words of the one who turns away from God to be like the words of children—indeed, more contemptible—beside the words sent down from the heaven of the All-Merciful’s utterance. I swear by God that no comparison can be made; rather, what is other than Him is not worthy of mention. They are worthy of mention only if they are assured of divine favor; after denial, they have been and will remain sheer nothingness.

526 Know that divine verses have always been the fundamental proof. Yet from this Most Great Revelation there has appeared, apart from verses, that which all have been and will remain powerless to match. Fear God and do not follow what your desire commands you. Follow the True One, who has come to you from the dawning-place of the Spirit with the tidings of God, the All-Powerful, the All-Protecting, the Almighty, the Self-Subsisting. He will indeed suffice you in place of all things, while nothing besides Him will suffice you, even if you cling to the creatures of the heavens and the earths, if you are certain. This is God’s counsel to you and to His servants. Whoever heeds his Lord’s counsel does so for his own benefit. As for whoever turns away, God is independent of all that has been and all that will be.

527 Another insinuation from that insinuating opponent: “If they say that those people have acknowledged him, Mírzá Yaḥyá’s statement now is precisely this: even supposing that you too utter verses, or that a thousand people do so—as the Most Exalted Lord has said, ‘If I wish, I can raise up, to the number of all things, those who speak in the language of verses’—they must still acknowledge me and remain beneath my shadow, because I am the first mirror and all are reflected from me, since the innate nature of God is manifest through me, as attested by the Point of the Bayán.” End of quotation.

528 Even supposing this claim is accepted, there have always been and will always be mirrors of the True One, and no one has reckoned their beginning. This has not been exclusive to one soul rather than another. All things have been and will be divine mirrors. Yet the former Manifestation complained of all the mirrors, saying—exalted and glorified is He: “I will surely complain to you, O Mirror of My bounty, of all the mirrors: each looks upon Me through its own colors.” Addressing that Name, He says: “You are that Primal Mirror which has always reflected the True One and will always reflect Him.” Elsewhere He called the First to Believe the Primal Mirror. These designations are mentioned at the earthly level; otherwise, the mirrors have neither a first nor a last. All these mirrors have been and will be created by a word. As long as they remain beneath the shadow of the Tree of Oneness, the lights of the True One are manifest in them, so that nothing is seen in them but God. After turning aside, however, they have been and will be satanic manifestations. By Him in whose hand is my soul, this complaint He made against all the mirrors is a mighty proof, if you are among those who understand.

529 Such is the station of the mirrors, as stated in the explicit text of the Point of the Bayán. Yet you speak of mirrors before the Sun, when the very suns of the mirrors have been and will be utter nothingness at His revelation. If the Manifestation Himself designates all things as mirrors of the first rank, it is the truth, beyond all doubt. Hear the call of this servant, rescue yourself from these barren lands, and enter the expanses of holiness. By God, these references only increase vain imaginings and veils. The more you occupy yourself with these words, the further you remain from the peerless, pure, flowing water of the Eternal One, and the more deprived you become. Cast aside these comparisons and look to the True One and what appears from Him, for all things culminate in Him and proceed from Him. Such words are suitable for presenting arguments to people like yourself in those outward arts of ignorance which you imagine to be knowledge. Do not send a bitter, briny drop to the ocean of sweet, refreshing water. Know that whatever the word “existence” applies to has been and will be created.

530 You have mentioned the innate nature of God. All things have been created according to the innate nature of God. You have not understood innate nature. Beyond this, hear the call of innate nature, raised up in a bodily form and speaking these words before the Most Great Gate. His words—exalted be His glory: “Among those who associate partners with God are some who say, ‘These verses were not revealed according to innate nature.’ By God, the True One, innate nature has now appeared in the form of a servant and has stood at the Gate in humility and repentance, crying aloud and saying, ‘Woe to you, company of the malicious! By God, I was created by a command from Him.’ Thus it testifies on its own behalf, but these wicked ones do not understand. By God, it indeed takes pride in its relationship to Our own Self, the True One, while We have always been independent of it. We created it and all things by a command from Us. None denies this but every ungrateful denier.”

531 These are verses previously revealed from the Abhá heaven concerning the original divine innate nature. This is among the subjects addressed by the people of the Qur’an, as revealed in the divine Book: “Stand upright in devotion to religion—the innate nature of God according to which He fashioned humanity.” Thus it is stated in the prophetic tradition: “Every child is born according to innate nature,” and so forth. This innate nature is that same innate nature of God, and this station holds true as long as one remains beneath the shadow of faith. After a change, one is counted among those who associate partners with God, as the remainder of the tradition says: “It is only his parents who make him a Jew, a Magian, or a Christian.”

532 Say: O heedless, confused one! Today you see with your own eyes and hear with your own ears that the ocean of verses is surging and that they descend like pouring rain from the heaven of the divine will. Yet you write that Mírzá Yaḥyá says, “The revealer of verses is a reflection of me.” O deprived one! This station is sanctified above both the one who casts a reflection and what is reflected. Had mature souls to the number of Váḥid been found in the Bayán, a land of spiritual knowledge would surely have been spread out, such that all would acknowledge that they had not understood a single letter of the Bayán. Yet none have been seen thus far, and hereafter the matter is in His hand.

533 His words—great is his heedlessness: “Setting all other statements aside, Mírzá Yaḥyá now claims, on the evidence of verses, a station above which none can be conceived. Whoever makes a claim must produce their like or something greater.” End of quotation. These words have issued from nothing but the wellspring of arrogance, the mine of falsehood, and the dawning-place of obscenity. Even if his status as a mirror is acknowledged, the Primal Point—may the souls of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—says that if the mirror claims, “I am the sun,” it is clear to the sun that it is an image of itself that is speaking. By God, this statement of the Revealer of the Bayán is an explicit declaration that after Him they would raise objections against the True One, and that souls claiming to be mirrors would claim to be suns before the Sun of Truth. Woe to him and to you! You invent falsehoods about yourselves and then about God, yet you do not perceive it.

534 Furthermore, this statement he has written is utterly shameless: “Today anyone making a claim must produce verses like those of Mírzá Yaḥyá, or greater.” O blind one! He has indeed been powerless before what has descended from the realm of utterance, from God, the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the Bestower. By God, all things are bewildered by these assertions you have made. His very existence and being are unworthy of mention today, let alone his words. Every discerning person knows that if even a word of the words of the True One is found with him, he has taken it from this most holy, most pure Seat. How unaware you are! I ask God to favor your sight with the eye-salve of spiritual knowledge, so that it may perhaps be freed from the inflammation of vain imaginings and look [corrupted original text] of the all-knowing King, that you may see all words as utter nothingness beside a single letter of the words of God. Thus has the decree descended from the dominion of judgment, as a grace from Him to all the worlds.

535 His words—little is his understanding: “Now, my brother, by God, you have become confused about the matter. If you truly wish to understand that no ulterior motive is involved, you yourself are my representative. If it becomes clear to you, write to me, but be fair. It is very easy for you. Civic governance has no bearing on the truth of a claim. This is practical philosophy, whose branches are called the refinement of character, household management, and civic governance: the knowledge of how to deal with people and with those attached to one, whether servants or disciples, treating each with affection and good character in a manner agreeable to his disposition. You have mistaken this practical philosophy for the essence of truth, and these things have become a veil for you. If you wish to test this, appoint a day and undertake an examination. Read a page of the Qur’an with the utmost speed before the person claiming to be the true one, and let him write the substance of that page in a particular rhymed prose without hesitation, then a second time in a different rhyme and wording, and so on. Whoever writes it is truthful in his claim; otherwise he is a liar, in contrast to Mírzá Yaḥyá, who claims this station. Test both of them yourself and inform me, but do so fairly. Certainly, whoever possesses this station is true, and his words are true, and anyone else is a liar.” End of quotation.

536 By God, everyone with keen sight laughs at your words. It seems that the fragrance of the divine Riḍván has never reached you, for today some sign must necessarily be manifest and evident in every soul associated with the True One. By God, your words appear more base than those of the religious communities of the past. Because hatred of God has been in your heart, your pen has been deprived of grace in expression and writing. You have become confused about the matter, leaving the company of the All-Merciful and turning toward the company of Satan. It is clear to every discerning person that, after five years of association, we servants recount what we have seen with our own eyes and heard with our own ears, by night and day. Yet you have clung to a vile statement rejected by this servant—let alone by the inhabitants of the thrones of inner meanings—and have utterly deprived yourself of the radiance of the lights of the dawn of inner meanings, which is rising and shining in these merciful days.

537 Now, you who claim to be fair, judge fairly: is it we who have become confused, or you? As for your writing that no ulterior motive is involved, by God, that very statement is pure ulterior motive and nothing but self and desire. You yourself wrote, “Now go and test it.” You commissioned this servant to conduct a test and then inform you of whatever became apparent, while you yourself have neither been here nor conducted a test. Why, then, before testing, have you attributed so many selfish fabrications and slanders to the True One and denied Him outright? Now consider who harbors ulterior motives. By God, if you judge fairly, you will find yourself in grievous loss. O ocean of ulterior motives, fear God, who created you by a command from His presence, and do not say what every atom proves false. Fear God and be among the God-fearing. Now consider how exposed and evident your deeds and actions are before the True One, and how your understanding has been seized by the hand of power, so that you yourself write down and establish your own ulterior motives and hatred, yet remain unaware. Thus everyone in the heavens and on earth bears witness to your ulterior motives; then your own self bears witness to your ulterior motives. This is indeed the certain truth.

538 The ulterior motives of those who associate partners with God have reached such a point that some like you have found fault with the character of the Beauty of the All-Merciful and raised objections to it. Yet you have testified to His character and then, out of selfish motives, objected. One is bewildered as to which statement to believe. On the one hand, the embodiment of corruption has written to various places, made complaints, and denied that the Manifestation of Divinity possesses merciful virtues. These are the statements they have written to those outside; on the other hand, he has suggested to you that he has written in this fashion to those who circle the House. If the good character is genuine, what justification is there for those people’s denial? If its absence is true, what does this statement of yours mean? By God, every discerning person is certain that they have not spoken a word of truth and never will. According to the requirements of the moment, they say and adduce as proof whatever they consider advantageous to their vile selves.

539 In any case, we servants testify that He is indeed of exalted character. We testify that He is God’s proof among His creation, His demonstration among His creatures, the Manifestation of His Cause in His lands, and the Dawning-place of glory among His servants. Whatever has appeared from Him is indeed true, just as His own being is true; all else is nonexistent in His court and lost before the manifestations of His sovereignty. He is indeed the Beloved of those who know and the Desire of all the worlds. Whoever turns away from Him has turned away from God, the All-Protecting, the Almighty, the All-Powerful.

540 O people of religion, I adjure you by the Master of the Day of Judgment: can even the slightest trace of the fragrance of truth be perceived in these words? No, by His own true Self! These words resemble those uttered by the basest people on earth when quarreling with one another: “My power is greater than yours, and my strength surpasses yours,” and similar idle talk current among the ignorant. By God, the Cause must now be mourned, for the Point of the Bayān, with all His station and dignity, has shown such humility, reverence, submission, and contentment toward this Revelation. Yet you have uttered words of which even the base would be ashamed, and have used those words to argue that the True One is false. Indeed, the Cause of God has now been brought so low that everything in the Bayān has become dependent upon your word and that of Mīrzā Yaḥyā, Siyyid Muḥammad, ʿAlī Muḥammad the saddler, and others like them, just as, toward the end of the dispensation of the Qur’an, all divine affairs and the firmly established law of God depended upon, and were conditional on, the word of one shaykh among the shaykhs.

541 It is clear that this is one of Satan’s promptings. It seems you have no understanding of your own. You have been caught in the tempest of self and desire, which carries you wherever it wishes. You have not reflected enough to realize that, in the eyes of the discerning, this very proposal is a form of deviation. Opinions differ concerning deviation. Some hold that deviators are those who call God by names not authorized in the divine Book. Others hold that deviators are those who derive names from the divine names and devote themselves to those names instead of God, just as they derived the word Manāt from Mannān, ʿUzzā from ʿAzīz, and Lāt from Allāh, and bow down and devote themselves to those names. Others hold that deviation is distortion, and that this distortion concerns meanings, not words, for laḥd has the meaning of concealment: these are people who have concealed the meanings of the divine words with their own desires—that is, they have interpreted and continue to interpret them according to their own desires. Others hold that deviators are those who [corrupted original text] the meanings of another in the garb of their own words. These are the meanings of deviation commonly known among people.

542 Now, this thing you have requested falls within these categories. In any case, it has been and will remain deviation. The one prompting you has presented this matter to you as a miracle. Besides, this is the easiest of methods. It is surprising that it has seemed difficult to you, for when the meaning is already present, determining the wording has always been and will always be exceedingly easy and simple.

543 Now consider how lacking in understanding you are. Beyond all this, by God, besides whom there is no other god, we have seen with our own eyes and heard with our own ears things greater than can be imagined. It is certain that you have never had, and still do not have, confidence in this servant’s word. Had you done so, you would have distinguished truth from falsehood through that very letter sent to you some time ago. Under these circumstances, you must either come yourself or appoint another representative to appear in His presence with all the heavenly books. This servant pledges that divine verses will be revealed in such a manner that no one will be able to write them down. This, then, is a proposal that you yourselves have mentioned and requested. Come and see.

544 Quite apart from the fact that the True One has never been and will never be known through such things, He is the measure of all things, and His creatures have never been and will never be the measure by which He is known. As already stated, this proposal is fundamentally unsound. You do not even have enough understanding to recognize that He says, “Do not put the Lord to the test,” for it is the prerogative of the True One to test and try people, and it is neither fitting nor proper for His creatures to test Him. Yet because you have made this your measure, have not been content with God’s measure, and have not found it sufficient, I have asked Him, out of His grace and loving care, to accept, and He has accepted. Thus He now says: “Come with the books of heaven into the presence of God, your Lord and the Lord of the first and the last. Then read whatever you wish, however you wish, so that from the dominion of God there may descend, for every word, words of unassailable glory in such a manner that all the worlds will be unable to count them. In whatever wording you desire and in whatever manner you wish, God will send them down in truth from a heaven of exalted holiness—words that all created beings together will be unable to write down.”

545 These firm words have been revealed in response to your request. Yet He has said that you will certainly resort to another excuse, for the word of the True One “increases those who associate partners with God in nothing but loss.” I do not know what further excuse you will cling to, or what measure you will seize upon. By God, O servant, you are far indeed from the shore of fairness and far removed from the seat of nearness. I do not know what has prompted you to do this, when you have neither appeared in His presence nor attained the presence of the previous Manifestation. Before conducting a test, by your own account, you have occupied yourself with these slanderous words, denied those who are informed about the Cause, and believed someone who was like yourself and had no knowledge from any quarter.

546 Beyond all this, your own words prove you false, contradicting one another. Thus, at this point you have written: “Certainly, whoever possesses this station is true, and his words are true, and anyone else is a liar.” Then you have written that even if He accomplishes this, it will still not be proof that He is that promised Person, and He will be one of the holy ones of the dispensation of the Bayān—and that only after Mīrzā Yaḥyā’s confirmation. Now judge fairly for yourself: if He is certainly the True One, how can He depend upon the confirmation of anyone besides Him? There are only two alternatives: you must admit either that the first statement was idle talk uttered without understanding, or that the second statement was. By God, the sign of wrath has become manifest and evident in such souls, for you are unaware of what you write and what you say.

547 Know that today, whatever your mind has grasped or may grasp, or whatever minds above or below yours may comprehend, has never been and will never be the measure of the knowledge of the True One. The inaccessible True One is sanctified, exalted, and free from all of these. Even if a soul ascends to the highest rank of spiritual knowledge or rises to the loftiest summit of utterance, this will not benefit that soul and has never borne, nor will ever bear, any fruit except after acknowledgment of this Most Great Revelation. Thus was the command revealed in the Bayān, then in the Qur’an, then in the Gospel, the Torah, the Psalms, and the Scriptures, if you are certain.

548 His words—great are his falsehood and fabrication: “Be fair: someone who has been nurtured for twenty years in this dispensation and twenty years in the circle of the mystics may write four words, indeed a thousand words, on mystical subjects and in the form of verses. What does acquired learning have to do with innate capacity? The difference between these two is just what was explained. Even if He were to write in that same manner—which would be difficult—it would still not prove that He is that promised Person, nor would it justify denying Mīrzā Yaḥyā and declaring him an unbeliever. Rather, He would be one of the holy ones of the dispensation of the Bayān, and that only on condition of acknowledging the Fruit, not denying him and wronging him.” End of quotation.

549 First, you lying slanderer, how was it established that His knowledge was acquired? With whom did He study, and who was His teacher? Second, who were those mystics who associated with that Most Holy Beauty? Your own self gives the lie to what you have fabricated, but you do not perceive it and are indeed among the heedless.

550 What is established and clear is that, while His father was alive, He associated with no one except occasionally those who visited His father. All the people of Iran know that His father, too, was not outwardly one of the learned. Investigate, so that perhaps you may find a path to the Truth.

551 Even if some mystics came to the house and meetings took place, this had nothing to do with teaching and learning. These are words that Mīrzā Yaḥyā has suggested. Even supposing it had been so, every atom bears witness that Mīrzā Yaḥyā was in His service: in every circumstance, he too shared in it.

552 Now reflect, you unfair one, on what you have said and what you are saying. With whom did He associate during the dispensation of the Bayān? Every soul who entered His presence acquired divine knowledge and the manifold expressions of heavenly wisdom. In His most inaccessible, most holy presence, no one was ever able to speak, much less could He have learned anything from them. We servants were in Iraq and saw this with our own eyes. Ask the people of Iraq, so that perhaps you may learn and be among those who recognize the truth.

553 Beyond this, from the beginning of the revelation of the Point of the Bayān—may the spirit of all else be sacrificed for Him—until now, He has had no rest, having spent most of His time afflicted by imprisonment and in the hands of enemies. After His father’s death, He brought Mullā Mihdī Kandī specifically for Mīrzā Yaḥyā and appointed him as his teacher; he would come for an hour each day and teach him. Now make inquiries in the land of Ṭāʾ. If anyone is found who says that He studied or acquired learning, you are right, and

554 Yet none of these things has ever been, or will ever be, proof of anything. How thoroughly they have instilled hatred in your heart, and how firmly slanders have been imprinted in your breast! At the revelation of the Point of the Bayān, these very same words came from those who associated partners with God: that He had associated with the Shaykhīs and had taken what He wrote from the words of Shaykh Aḥmad. They also alleged that He had studied under someone in the land of Shīn and acquired outward learning. May God curse the one who prompted you and cast into your breast that for which he, rather than anyone else, deserved this curse.

555 Furthermore, you have mentioned “the form of verses.” This is something that those who associate partners with God have long been saying. Sometimes they have spoken of shell and kernel, and sometimes of form and matter, in connection with the verses. Everything that comes from their mouths returns upon themselves. Your Lord, the All-Merciful, is indeed free of them and of what they say. You miserable blind one! You have not distinguished right from left; how have you become a judge of what is innate and what is not? A true one whose judge you are is indeed fit and worthy of you. If any sight were to be found, it would recognize and be certain that all the words of the world cannot equal a single verse of what has been revealed by the True One. All those who have been deprived of the shore of the ocean of the All-Merciful’s knowledge have been deprived because they made their own understanding the measure of the knowledge of the True One. Thus they have been barred and excluded from the heavenly waters of meaning. Today every religious community waits according to its own proofs and arguments and has hesitated at the revelations of the Manifestations of Divine Unity. This is the condition of souls who have had at least some desire for recognition. But people like you, who have stood against the face of the True One with sheer hatred and pure malice, have never had, and do not have, any standing before God.

556 If only you could hear the cry of your limbs, and what they were saying to you as you wrote your words! Even a hair on your body has sought refuge in God from your evil, your scheming, and your deceit. Know that the inaccessible True One appears each day in a wondrous new way, and human understanding has never been, nor will ever be, the measure of the knowledge of Him. How many souls were counted among the loftiest trees of Paradise in the previous revelation, but were named among the lowliest trees of hell in the revelation that followed! The Point of the Bayān—may the spirit of all else be sacrificed for Him—has explicitly stated this matter, as has already been mentioned.

557 As for what has been written by the pen of fabrication and falsehood, that this would not justify denying Mīrzā Yaḥyā and declaring him an unbeliever, and so forth: the Tongue of God now declares, “May God curse the first oppressor who wronged the Primal Point in His latter revelation, disbelieved in His verses, denied His revelation, rejected His proof, disputed His sovereignty, fought against His own Person, and sought to shed His blood after He had come to them with verses of manifest glory.” Every atom bears witness that He opposed no one and was more merciful to every soul than that soul was to itself. Everything bears witness to this, even if you deny it.

558 By His own truth, He treated everyone with perfect love, until, after the great affair, some writings by Mīrzā Yaḥyā were seen. An outline of that affair has already been given. In those writings he attributed to the True One all the evils of those who came before and those who came after. After those slanderous, fabricated, lying writings were seen, what was revealed was revealed in refutation of the one who had rejected God. This was not from Him, but from God, the Revealer, the Almighty, the Generous.

559 You unfair one! By God, besides whom there is no god, if there were an atom of divine love in your heart, you would surely have written to him: “By what proof and argument did you set about cutting down the divine Tree and object to a Being who says: ‘My people, I am God’s testimony among you, His proof in your midst, and a mercy to you. I have come to you from the horizon of meanings and utterance with tidings that are momentous in the sight of the True One. God has raised Me up and sent Me with the verses with which He sent all the Prophets and Messengers, and then bestowed them upon Me in truth. By what argument, then, do you reject this Cause, which has dawned from the horizon of holiness with manifest authority? My people, do you oppose the One through whom your faith and the faith of every believer have been established, through whom your names have been exalted and your ranks raised, and through whom the Word of God has appeared throughout the world? God is witness to this.’”

560 Nevertheless, you did not offer him a word of counsel for God’s sake, but took up a sword and occupied yourself with his deeds. This was the occasion to object to him, not to the True One. By God, he has done what no one has done! You have regarded as true a person who has issued a verdict for the killing of all the Prophets, and have opposed the One from whom the Truth has appeared and through whom the Truth has been established. Alas for you and for what you have committed in this vain life! Alas for you, for you have wasted your life and have not been among those who perceive.

561 Any soul with less than an atom of awareness knows with certainty that, had He so willed, that man would never have existed. For twenty years in this Cause and twenty years before this Cause, He exerted the utmost effort, with perfect compassion and kindness, to protect and educate him. The result was what you have heard. The heavens and the earth have wept, and likewise the air, the waters, the stones, the trees, the fruits, and the leaves. Yet those who associate partners with God are greatly rejoicing.

562 And where, moreover, were you in this land that you perceived oppression and made this accusation? By God, besides whom there is no god, no one has so much as spoken to that soul who turned away, let alone wronged him! You have regarded the oppressor as the oppressed and counted the one who associates partners with God as a believer in His oneness. You yourself have acknowledged the character of the True One, yet you accuse Him of oppression. You do not comprehend what you say. At all times he was occupied with pleasure and enjoyment, with several women, in a place of comfort. Yes, in this land that troublemaking sayyid—may God grant that he see no good and fail to attain his purpose—instilled in him whatever he instilled, and all these matters are of his devising, although he himself is the root of the tree of rejection. By God, besides whom there is no god, he has displayed such deceit and trickery as have never had, and do not have, any equal or likeness in creation. Your discernment has become apparent: how well you understand the matter! The desire to associate partners with God and to practice hypocrisy moves you however it wishes.

563 His words—great is his slander: “In short, one is greatly astonished at you. Formerly, whoever renounced the world and its leadership was praised. Now you have called this fear, and of someone to whom the Most Exalted One, in His epistles, would write ‘From God and to God’ at the beginning of the address, you write that he hid himself in women’s veils. Astonishing, astonishing! You have been seekers of the True One, and how thoroughly you have undermined the Prophets of the past! Jesus took refuge in caves, the Prophet hid in a sack, and Zechariah found shelter in a tree. All the holy ones, from fear or for some other reason, dared not breathe and fled from city to city and from land to land.”

564 End of quotation. You have lied, by God who created all things! You have never known, and do not know, what issued from the Primal Point. Several booklets descended from the heaven of the will of the Point of the Bayān. In some, ‘From God to God’ is mentioned, and in others, ‘From God to Him whom God will make manifest.’ The intended object of them all was this most unassailable, most holy Revelation, from which you and your like have turned away and which you deny with utter rejection. One was revealed specifically for Mīrzā Asad Allāh, whom the Point of the Bayān called Dayyān and named with all the most beautiful names. Your guide reviled and cursed him and issued a decree for his death, and they martyred that hapless man. Likewise, one was revealed specifically for His Honor Āqā Sayyid Ibrāhīm from the heaven of the mighty, all-powerful Will, and your guide called him the Father of Calamities. Some of the things that befell these two have been mentioned before. Read it, then, so that you may learn of your guide’s good deeds and of what befell them.

565 These words have been mentioned so that you may not speak without awareness, be moved by every wind, or turn away from the True One, who, if He wishes, has the power to reduce all the names you have enumerated to a handful of dust. For one of the stations of the True One’s description is this: since He beholds in all things the traces of His own revelations, all names and attributes have referred, and will refer, to the lowliest sign manifest on earth. For the most beautiful names by which it is addressed refer to that divine revelation which He has manifested within it without direction. As long as this sign of revelation remains in all things, the application of all names to all things is valid; after turning away, each is deprived and returns to the abode of fire. Had you been seen to be capable of recognition, the Ocean of meanings and recognition would surely have bestowed a sprinkling upon you. But it is better that you remain deprived and speak these same limited, perverted, veiled words. Nothing issues from the people of the nethermost prison but words belonging to that prison.

566 And as for his writing, ‘Formerly, whoever renounced the world and its leadership was praised; now you have called this fear,’ and his writing that a certain person hid himself in women’s veils: what was revealed came from God, and the One who reveals it is the True One, and He is truthful in what He has said and says. What a detached soul you have found! Yes, detached from the True One, not detached from all else. By God, I am ashamed to mention his deeds, for you are unaware. The people who were with him are still alive. This is someone whose carnal desire reached such a point that he did not spare the divine household, but took possession of the Point’s wife. By God, he tore through the covering of the Most Great Veil. This shameless, insolent man—you describe him as detached!

567 In former years he designated seven people as martyrs of fire. One of these souls, upon learning of it, repented and returned to God and turned to Him in repentance. God clothed him in the garment of His forgiveness and the robe of His grace, and raised him to the All-Glorious Paradise above the Lote Tree of the Utmost Boundary—he who was named Mūsā in the kingdom of names. He is indeed the one who ascended to the Most Exalted Horizon, and now he dwells in the All-Glorious Vista, while the inhabitants of the cities of eternity circle around him. That blessed soul was Mīrzā Mūsā of Qum, upon him be the glory of God, who shattered the idol of delusion and desire and, by the permission of God, the Creator of earth and heaven, became established in the All-Glorious sphere, and afterward returned to God. Another soul never accepted this falsehood, as though the Holy Spirit had informed him and protected him from the fabrications of these fabricators.

568 In any case, to give the details would take too long. In short, three of those souls remained, and in that booklet he made these souls martyrs and explicitly wrote that each should send him a virgin girl, together with dirhams and dinars. That booklet is now at hand. Surely you have counted these things as detachment. By God, these deeds, compared with his other acts, were good deeds, and I, this servant, am ashamed to mention those. Until now the covering of divine majesty had concealed his faults. Since he tore the covering, the True One therefore disclosed them. Astonishing, utterly astonishing! Indeed, astonishment itself says, ‘Astonishing, and again astonishing!’ For he has not distinguished earthenware from pearls, and, going beyond this most holy Seat, has also fabricated slanders against the Prophets and Messengers. To establish an imaginary cause, he has opened his mouth to revile the sovereigns of existence.

569 Listen, you who are uninformed. As for Jesus son of Mary, from the beginning of His mission He conversed with the Jewish leaders in the Temple on most days and proclaimed the Cause without concealment, as is recorded in the books, which you have not seen. The Gospel explicitly states that when they brought that holy, merciful Spirit into the assembly of one of the Jewish scholars, who was named Annas, he asked Him, ‘What is Your teaching, and what do You say?’ He replied, ‘Why do you question Me? Question the Jewish people, for I have not spoken a word in secret; rather, whatever I have said, I have said in the Temple and in the gathering of the Jews.’ Then one of the soldiers present in that assembly struck His blessed face, saying, ‘Why do You speak insolently to the high priest?’

570 Then that scholar gave orders, and they took Him to Caiaphas, who was the judge that year—that is, in that year the administration of the Jewish community’s religious law rested with him. Although the Book explicitly states that every day He publicly proclaimed the Cause of God in the Temple, he has now written that Jesus lived in caves and dared not breathe from fear. He has committed a grave transgression and overstepped his station.

571 Here a saying of the Spirit is mentioned, so that perhaps, through the heavenly stream of the utterance of the Beauty of the All-Merciful, you may enter the garden of the All-Glorious. When He saw the cross, He carried it Himself, then embraced it, saying, ‘Come, My beloved cross, for thirty-three years I have awaited you and searched for you, and I wish to die nailed to you out of love for My sheep.’ Yet none but the spiritually minded perceive the fragrances of these words, and none but the people of the Vista of the All-Merciful comprehend the sweetness of this utterance. The likes of those souls have been, and will remain, without a share, just as you have not understood this station. Had you understood it, you would not have written with such audacity and disrespect about those Suns of the horizon of detachment. Listen to what He said when all the Jews had gathered to put Him to death. When they brought the cross and His blessed eyes fell upon it, He said, ‘Come, come, for thirty-three years I have awaited you and longed for you.’ How far apart are this station and the station you have understood and written about!

572 There has been disagreement on this point. What is explicitly stated in the Gospel is that they hung that Essence of Eternity upon the cross, and He yielded up His spirit; after thirty-six hours He came to life and ascended to heaven. What is related in the traditions of the people of the Qurʾān is that He ascended to heaven before that. But as for the reality of the matter, its knowledge is with my Lord in a Book in which He has omitted none of the tidings of the first or the last. For most people have spoken without understanding the meanings of the divine words, and have therefore remained veiled from the underlying intent.

573 This is a brief account of the story of the Spirit that has been related. Now this Spirit addresses you and says, “Come, come, O company of deniers, with your swords, your spears, and your arrows! I long for them just as He longed for them. By Him in whose hand is the soul of Ḥusayn, I am even more eager and more expectant, but you do not perceive.” For you have not tasted the heavenly abundance of meanings or drunk from the sweet spring of love. Alas, alas for what you have fabricated against the Prophets of God, and for the wrong you have done to His Manifestations, the dawning-places of His revelation, the daysprings of His inspiration, the repositories of His knowledge, and the treasuries of His wisdom! You have committed this only because you wished to justify your guide’s conduct. Thus your own self has made a deed seem fair to you and made you one of the transgressors. Is one who remained concealed beneath a veil like one who sacrificed His spirit in the path of God?

574 You have not even read the Qurʾān, where He says—exalted are His words—“God has favored those who strive over those who sit idle with a mighty reward.” Is one who sits at home like one who strives in the path of God? Vast is the difference between detached souls and those who associate partners with God and are confined by limitations. Do not compare them, and do not soil the hem of the Prophets’ holiness with the dust of conjectures and imaginings. As for your statement that they did not dare breathe for fear, such souls would be like you. Exalted are They above that!

575 O deluded imitator! What use would such souls be? What lies within your own reality has become evident from this statement: what your belief and that of those who turn away from God has been and will be. You have considered the jewels of existence to be like yourself, comparing the Suns of Truth to souls who associate partners with God. By God, everything has been shaken by your words! Know at least this: whatever you may comprehend at the highest degree of your own understanding is an imagining of yours that refers back to your own self, while God’s chosen ones are sanctified and free from it. By the Sun of the glory of meanings, at every moment and instant They have eagerly awaited the chance to offer up Their lives in the path of the All-Merciful. Yet you write in mockery of God that the Prophet had gone into a sack. Your words reveal your worth. Was there no other expression you could use, that you chose to mention Him with this vile expression? Shame on you and your sense of shame! Your guide’s nature, too, has been and will remain like your own.

576 By those very Manifestations of the glory of holiness, had there been even a little love in you, such base words about the Manifestations of oneness would not have issued from your halting, faltering tongue, even if all your limbs had been cut to pieces with sharp swords. Your guide, too, has said many such things, and this same Siyyid Muḥammad used to mock Him. Praise be to the Beloved, who has deprived the likes of that person of the recognition of His Manifestations and denied them the sweetness of love! Have you not heard that for twenty years the blessed life of the Seal of the Prophets—may the spirit of all else be sacrificed for Him—was afflicted at the hands of His enemies, and that on no night was there hope of morning, and on no morning hope of night? By God, at all times He awaited and hoped to sacrifice His life in the path of the Friend and hasten to the Most Exalted Companion.

577 O raven of the barren land, do not speak of the parrot of the Egypt of oneness! O buzzing of a fly, do not mention the sound of the Most Exalted Pen, for you disgrace yourself; do not trouble Us. Have you not heard with what ardor and longing Ḥusayn son of ʿAlī—may the spirit of everyone in the kingdom of creation be sacrificed for them both—gave His life in the path of the True One? If you say, as the unbelievers have said, that He fell into the hands of His enemies and had no choice but to be killed, this statement is an outrage by you and them against God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting. For when He set out from Medina, He departed with this very intention and resolve. By God, He departed longing to meet the Beloved and traversed the stages of His journey in quest of union with Him. Thus, when that Beauty of oneness was leaving Medina, He went expressly to bid farewell at the sacred shrine of His sanctified grandfather, the pure essence of existence in the unseen and the visible, and spoke these wondrous, most sweet words:

578 “You know, O Beloved, what I have intended for the sake of God, relying upon Him. My patience has run out because of my love for the unveiled beauty of God. You know what the son of adultery has deliberately intended against my blood. No, by Your glorious presence, I will not pledge allegiance to him, either secretly or openly. O God, bring near the day of my bloodshed, then my tears, as I lie upon the dust. If only my day were the day of my bloodshed, when I would lie thirsting in the earth!” By God, with a single fragrance among those that wafted from the paradise of His love for God, His Beloved, He perfumed all existence and drew to Himself the inhabitants of the kingdoms of the unseen and the visible. At His words on that occasion, the inhabitants of the heavenly Concourse were bewildered, and all existence lamented with the lamentation of those who yearn and wept with the weeping of lovers. Through the fire of His love, the torches of love were kindled between earth and heaven, and through His turning toward the land of Ṭaff to sacrifice Himself for God, all created things turned toward God. Yet only a pure lover and a true devotee has comprehended, and comprehends, this station. God has placed it beyond your understanding and that of your two guides.

579 Rein yourself in, O you who mock God, His loved ones, His chosen ones, and His friends! This Ḥusayn now says: God has made this prison the land of Ṭaff for Me. Now I stand ready for the swords of destiny and yearn for the arrows of affliction. I will fear no one, flee from no soul, nor conceal My face, which has shone between the heavens and the earths. I say: O Sinān, come with your spearhead! O Khawlī, bring your spear! O Shimr, hasten with your dagger! I have offered up My self, My spirit, and My very being in the path of God, the All-Protecting, the Almighty, the Self-Subsisting. Every day I have awaited martyrdom, and I still do. I am aware of what, at the prompting of the tree of corruption, you have said to certain leading figures. But know that the action of the True One has been clear and distinct from that of anyone else, and no one resembles Him in any matter. He is the Manifestation of unity and is privy to singleness among His servants.
