When the All-Glorious Beauty arrived in the Great City, He uttered several verses, some of which are mentioned here, so that perhaps you may take heed, stand in awe, and restrain your pen from fabrications, and perhaps be among those who return and repent to God, who created you and created all things. All will surely return to Him. They are these:

580 O morning breeze, from the Beloved’s presence, speed gently for a moment to the quarter of the people of Zawrā.

581 Then say to her: “O city of the Creator, how have you endured, now that the Friend has left your side?

582 Your Friend is afflicted in the hands of enemies, like Ḥusayn on the land of Karbalā—one Ḥusayn,

583 And against Him a hundred thousand Yazīds; one Beloved, and a hundred obstinate Abū Jahls.

584 Like Moses among the Egyptians, or like the Spirit of God among the deniers.

585 Like Joseph, fallen into a well—a well with neither bottom nor way out.”

586 He said: What has appeared has appeared, and the rest will also appear. None laid its foundation but those who fabricated lies against Him among His servants, and you are one of them. God’s knowledge encompasses what you have done. Now He says: “O people of the Bayān! Here is My head, laid bare between the heavens and the earth, awaiting your swords. Strike it, then, however [corrupted original text], and do not be among those who hesitate. Here is My breast, yearning for the arrows of hatred. Strike it, then, however you wish, O company of fabricators! Here is My throat, longing for your dagger. Cut it, for We have offered it up in the path of My Beloved and the Beloved of all worlds. In all this We thank Him and praise Him. He is indeed the desire of My soul, and whatever has appeared and appears from Him has been My desire, if only you could perceive.”

587 Know that the station of the Prophets is too exalted for the likes of you to comprehend. Those Suns of oneness have always been filled with divine ardor and longing. Whatever befell those holy Souls in the path of the Beloved, They were all content and thankful, and They did not neglect the proclamation of the Cause of God for a moment. Have you not heard how greatly Noah lamented in the Cause of God? Every day He stood before His people and summoned them to the waters of divine glory. Each day, after He had delivered His message, they persisted in tormenting that Manifestation of the Cause with an intensity beyond description. On some days, so severe were the afflictions that befell Him that they thought He was dead. He bore all these things out of love for God. If you have seen mention in the Book of the Prophets’ fear, as is recorded in the Book of God in the account of Moses, neither you nor your guides have understood His case. If you wish to understand, then ask, so that God may explain to you what is with Him. With Him is the knowledge of all things and the knowledge of the heavens and the earths.

588 As for the account of Zechariah, if you have heard His story, reflect on it a little, for the details will become clear from that very account. This servant has not deemed it advisable to mention what has been imparted from the source of innate knowledge. Know only this: all the Prophets surrendered Their lives in the path of God with the utmost longing. Even some who did not outwardly suffer martyrdom were secretly poisoned, or subjected to other deceptions; knowledge of this rests with my Lord. Your guide, too—I swear by God, besides whom there is no god—occupied himself with these very things. But God protected the Manifestation of His own Self through His sovereignty. He is indeed the All-Powerful, the Almighty.

589 As for the account of the Primal Point—may the spirit of all else be sacrificed for Him—when that divine Being was being brought from Fars with successive mounted couriers to be taken to Azerbaijan, some of His companions, including Jináb-i-ʿAẓím, upon him be the glory of God, wrote to some of the believers living in the Land of Ṭá: “We have secured the agreement of those accompanying Him to accept a little money, so that we may take Him away from among these people at night and conceal Him.” They submitted this matter to His most holy and exalted presence—glorified be His majesty. That Beauty of Oneness smiled, and from His blessed smile the companions understood that He approved of what they intended. After the arrangements had been made, while He was in Kulayn, they submitted in His most holy presence: “Tonight You must leave these people.” Again He smiled and said: “The mountain of Azerbaijan also has its share.” This was among the deeds of the True One; it proceeded from the True One, was founded on truth, and shed the fragrance of truth. Yet you and those like you do not perceive it and remain among the heedless.

590 He said: Write to that person: A word is spoken for God’s sake; listen to it. Do not, out of hostility toward this Prisoner, violate the sanctity of the Prophets, tear apart the veil of grandeur, or dishonor the sanctity of God. Fear God, who created you and all things, and do not be among those who fabricate lies against God’s Prophets, His trusted ones, and His chosen ones. By Him in whose hand is my soul! Every one of Them calls upon His Lord each day and says: “If only I had a thousand spirits, a thousand bodies, and a thousand souls to sacrifice in Your path, O Beloved of the worlds, O Desire of those who yearn, O ardent longing in the hearts of lovers!” If you wish to offer an example in these matters, refer it to this Prisoner Himself, just as you have attributed other deeds to Him. Do not sully the hems of divine purity with the dust of self and desire. This is My counsel to you: if you listen, it is for your own good; if you turn away, it is to your own detriment. God watches over us and you and is a witness.

591 Listen to this servant’s call and counsel those fabricators. Enough of their efforts to bring dishonor! Matters have reached the point where they have gone to everyone, continually uttering slanders, and they continue to do so. Say: Fear God and do not fabricate lies against Him. Be mindful of your conduct. He protected you for a number of years and concealed your deeds and actions. Yet when you felt secure, you emerged from behind the veil and fabricated against Him what no spreader of corruption had ever fabricated. To this bears witness the Tongue of God, the King, the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the Generous.

592 His words—great are his deceit and arrogance: “You have stated that Jináb-i-Mírzá arose alone to serve the Cause of God. How can one determine whether this undertaking was for the exaltation of the Cause of God or for the love of rank, leadership, pomp, and wealth? Yes, those who arose to serve the Cause of God were the people who ate grass and leather in Mazandaran, not those whose days and nights in Baghdad and elsewhere are spent drinking tea, with samovars and all their arrangements and trappings, with friends and servants; in coffeehouses with rabble and riffraff, in idle conversations, and over lunches and sumptuous meals; whose efforts are devoted to food, sweets, and banquets; and whose companions gather from door to door to take their ease. What service to the Cause of God has been undertaken?” End of quotation.

593 Every atom bears witness to this, even if your wicked self is among those who deny it. After the events in the Land of Ṭá, the fire of divine love was extinguished in every heart, and the lamp of His love had gone out in the hearts of those who knew Him. Thus, in the early years after His arrival in Iraq, no breeze of the Cause blew from any direction, nor was the fragrance of the garment of the All-Merciful’s love perceived from any quarter. Matters were so difficult that every heart was troubled and most souls were shaken. Even if a few souls remained in certain lands, they were dispirited, veiled, and concealed. Judge fairly: who stood against the enemies? At that time there was neither prestige nor a body of followers over whom leadership could be exercised. Thus He dwelt in Iraq by Himself, alone.

594 Since the people of Iraq regarded this community as lowly and insignificant, to such a degree that nothing but curses, abuse, and insults was spoken of it, no one associated with them to receive sustenance from the heavenly stream of the exposition of divine knowledge and the sweet waters of boundless divine wisdom. Solely for the sake of mingling with people, He went for a time to a coffeehouse situated by the waters of the river. There He entered into conversation with some of the people of Iraq, who then began to visit Him. The Cause of God became so exalted that all spoke in praise of God and turned toward the Desired One. O blind one! If you had even the sight of a bat, you would assuredly acknowledge that His sitting in the coffeehouse for less than a moment is better than you, your existence, all you have done and will do, and the deeds of those like you.

595 How closely this objection of yours resembles the objections the Jews raised against Jesus, son of Mary! In protest against Him, they used to say: “This Nazarene sits with tax collectors in the tax houses.” My Beloved and the Beloved of those who know Him spoke truly: all that appeared in the past in the ages of all the Messengers must appear in this wondrous divine Revelation, letter for letter. Moreover, souls must arise in opposition who are the most wicked of all people, first and last, as can be seen. But since you are utterly deprived of both outward and inward sight, you have denied, and will continue to deny, the radiance of the Sun of Truth and Inner Meanings.

596 After the Cause had been exalted in that land, pure and holy hearts turned from every direction toward the One. Every soul who entered His presence, whether from Iraq or elsewhere, attained what they sought in whatever they asked. Yet the bat’s denial cannot veil the light of the sun, just as your denial, born of malice toward God, protest against Him, and rejection of Him, cannot conceal the light of His countenance. Throughout His stay in Iraq there was not an hour of ease, nor a moment’s rest, for enemies lay in wait on every side. You yourself know this, yet deny it. You are among those who recognized God’s favor and then denied it. Say: Woe to you, O rejected one who associates partners with God!

597 At certain times it was reported that orders had been issued to arrest this community and send them to the Land of Ṭá. The Abhá Beauty expressly commanded everyone to leave Iraq. He enforced this firm decree so strictly that when the well-known Zaynu’l-ʿÁbidín Khán came into His presence and interceded for Áqá Siyyid Ḥusayn the confectioner, asking that his departure be suspended and that he be permitted to remain, no permission whatsoever was given. He too left Iraq. He said: “I wish to bear everyone’s afflictions alone, so that they may inflict on this servant whatever they have intended, while the loved ones of God remain safe.” Yet you, a fabricator, seek with such words to sully the Sun of Holiness and Detachment with expressions of malice. Have you not heard what happened and what befell Him during the last two years in Iraq, when most of the people and all the religious scholars arose in opposition? Judge fairly for yourself: in such circumstances, would anyone retain enough hope of surviving to think of leadership? The deeds of the True One have been, and will remain, a proof, testimony, and witness to His Cause.

598 Yet you felt no shame in writing what you wrote about One who was detached from all who are on earth, who shone visibly and brilliantly like the sun among His enemies, and whose blessed body was struck from every side by the arrows of hatred—words over which the realities of all things have lamented. Besides all this, have you not heard of the afflictions of the Beauty of Oneness in Iraq? What do you say of the Land of Ṭāʾ, you who are unaware and blind? This same Mīrzā Yaḥyā was with Sulaymān Khān in Dizāshūb. He then came to the Land of Ṭāʾ and remained concealed in Sulaymān Khān’s house for a few days. Once the means of sedition had been assembled, he fled, until what happened came to pass. His Holiness the Most Glorious remained openly and visibly in His own residence, so that they came and arrested Him, although, by God, besides whom there is no other god, He had only just returned from the Land of Ṭaff in those very days and had no knowledge of it at all. He spent four consecutive months in chains and fetters. The harm inflicted in those days needs no recounting—what they inflicted—and

599 Likewise, before that, in Mazandaran, when this same Mīrzā Yaḥyā was with Him, they took Him captive and led Him from city to city and region to region. On the day when all the religious scholars and the rest of the people gathered to stone the Beauty of Oneness, and brought that blessed Beauty before the oppressors, what happened came to pass. The Pen is ashamed to recount what befell the Manifestation of God’s own Self. Afterward, they intended to punish Mīrzā Yaḥyā. He said, “He was with Me. If any fault has been committed, it was Mine.” For this reason they did not molest him, but inflicted on that Ancient Beauty what they inflicted. He likewise interceded for His other companions, among them the honored Mullā Bāqir, the Letter of the Living—may the glory of God rest upon him—who is still alive. Ask him.

600 Beyond all this, in Niyālā, a village among the villages of Dār al-Marz, five hundred people or more suddenly attacked that Beauty of Oneness, and what befell Him in that land befell Him. If you judge fairly, from the beginning of this wondrous Revelation until now He has never known ease. Yet you have written what you have written. Why did these things happen, and for whose sake did they happen? Perhaps you will deny these things as well, for that would be no surprise from such people.

601 Now tell us about your guide, who has turned away from God: what has befallen him in this Cause? It is certain that he has continually occupied himself with eating and drinking, taking possession of other people’s virgins and wives, and committing deeds that, by God, I am ashamed to mention. After the sedition he stirred up in the Land of Ṭāʾ, he fled to Iraq and entered His house. He was so fearful that, despite the presence of His Holiness the Most Glorious—whom everyone knows all the people were seeking to harm—by Him in whose hand is my soul, whenever a single report arrived from Iran, he would leave Iraq for the surrounding regions. All the while he was occupied with sensual pleasure and enjoyment. Yet you have denied what is plain to see and written, “How was it established that this uprising was for God’s sake?”

602 You malicious denier! How did you establish that it was for anyone other than God? He has never, for a moment, sought to safeguard His sacred Self. Here a passage from His supplication to God is recounted, so that you may reflect within yourself and be among those who take heed. Its meaning in Persian is this: “O my God, I have never sought to safeguard Myself in Your path, nor have I compromised with Your enemies. At all times I have suffered in the hands of enemies, and amid the events that befell Me, My intention was to offer up My life. But You, O my Lord, through consummate wisdom and purposes hidden from the eyes of Your creatures, have preserved Your servant. Yet this preservation was not for His ease, but for another decree. You are praised in all Your actions and beloved in Your will.” Now, if people like you had considered the divinely revealed words, they would have become aware of things of which no one has become aware. But it has been decreed concerning you and them.

603 As for your statement that those who aided the Cause of God were the ones who ate leather in Mazandaran, He said, “During the days when We were imprisoned in the Land of Ṭāʾ, for the first few days there was not even leather for anyone to eat.” Alas for you, you who are heedless of every good and commit every evil! You speak of what you do not know. This did not originate with you, but with what Satan cast into your breast. We ask God to seize him with the grip of One who is mighty and all-powerful. Every atom bears witness that the Most Glorious Beauty stood alone against His enemies. Indeed, in those days some of the loved ones wrote from the Land of Ṭaff, imploring Him to remain concealed for a few days, for matters had become so grave that everyone who entered His presence each day brought fresh news of the schemers’ plots and the deniers’ intentions. The enemies rose in such opposition that they stationed men in the lanes and markets to shoot Him dead as He passed. Nevertheless, He did not conceal His blessed face for a moment, but went out of the house alone, paying no heed to anyone. He would always say, “Oh, how I long to meet the Beloved!” The dwellers of the heavenly Assembly marveled at His conduct, and all proclaimed, “Blessed is God, the best of originators!” Yet you have denied it all, for you have befriended wicked souls and disowned the True One.

604 Yes, the difference was that this most wondrous Revelation forbade people to kill, dispute, or fight. Thus, throughout the years of His stay in Iraq, no occasion for battle arose. It was expressly revealed in the verses that the Cause has been, and will be, aided and taught through good counsel and healing words, not through contention and warfare. So emphatically was this binding command revealed that in some of the divine words it is stated that

605 “If someone were to inflict harm on My children or My relatives, it would be better and more pleasing in the sight of this servant than for him to assail another soul.”

606 Consider, then, what has been revealed in the Tablets, so that you may judge fairly within yourself and be among the just. Accordingly, throughout this period nothing happened except that a few members of this community killed one another at your guide’s command. They assailed no one else. In all the Tablets it has been revealed that the purpose of this Revelation is unity and concord, not division and discord.

607 As for what you wrote, “Not those people who, in Baghdad and elsewhere, spend their days and nights drinking tea with samovars and all their arrangements and equipment,” and so forth: you most abject of beings! By God, besides whom there is no other god, if you and your guides possessed less than an atom of decency, you would never have uttered such words or hurt God’s own Self to delight wicked souls. Has any vile person ever uttered words like those you have uttered? No, by the Lord of the worlds, none but you and those like you! Hear the call of the Point of the Bayān, then feel shame before His beauty. Do not strike His Beloved with the swords of hatred, nor the One He seeks with the spears of indecency. Beware of a day when all existence, unseen and seen, will tremble, and the skins of all the world’s peoples will shudder.

608 His words—exalted be His honor and great His majesty—are: “Then, on a night, among the favors of God, let nineteen be numbered before you, that you may count up to the number of al-Mustaghāth. Permission is given to whoever is able; do not grieve if you are unable.” End of quotation. He says that you have been given permission, if you are able, to bring together in one gathering as many as the number of al-Mustaghāth. Yet, without understanding, you have mentioned tea, samovars, food, sweets, and hospitality as an objection against God, although it is the command of the Point of the Bayān, who says—exalted be His remembrance—“You must entertain nineteen souls every nineteen days, even if you can offer only water to each one.” He likewise says that every soul is required to have in his house a number of crystal vessels equal to one vāḥid. He also says—exalted be His remembrance—“You shall make of gold and silver the furnishings by which you live in your private life.” Yet you, base-natured and most abject, have objected to tea and samovars. The Primal Point uttered all these statements only so that no one would claim, “I ate barley bread and thereby attained the highest rank.”

609 Yes, this much is required: a person should be content with little and be thankful, and should not let abundance make him heedless of God. This is the essence of the matter; otherwise, neither asceticism nor its absence has been a sign of truth or falsehood. The mark of God’s loved ones is plain and visible on their foreheads, just as today the agate seal of inner meanings has inscribed on the foreheads of God’s loved ones: “This is one of the people of God among the worlds.” By God, His only purpose in this utterance was that, through divine favor, His servants might perhaps be freed from these restrictions. He said, “If it were possible, today I would command all the people of God to sit on seats of gold,” for everything that has been created was, in its primary reality, intended especially for those who believe in God. How far removed is this outlook from that of someone who objects to drinking tea, even though he belongs to the religion of the Bayān!

610 People of the Bayān! Reflect on the objections of those who turn away, and on what they have brought forward. Reflect, so that you may be among those who discern their ways. How quickly they have altered all the divine observances! For the Most Exalted One Himself—may the spirit of all else be sacrificed for Him—drank tea every day, commanded all His loved ones to drink tea, and says, “I have not attained this station through asceticism.” Likewise, the Abhā Beauty says, “I have never practiced asceticism, nor have I belonged to the learned. Rather, this favor that has appeared has come from the grace of the Lord, without any merit on My part. Yet, out of necessity, on many nights none of God’s bounties lay before Me, not even bread. Likewise, for two consecutive years, to outward appearances there were no means of subsistence at all. Yet in every condition We have been thankful and will remain content.”

611 Today God’s loved ones should in no way impose hardship on themselves. Where possible, they should enjoy fine foods and in all circumstances conduct themselves with moderation. If there is only one dish, this is more beloved in God’s sight; yet they should endeavor, according to their means, to make that one dish a fine one. Those who possess wealth must not forget the poor, for the poor are God’s trusts among His servants. Spend, My people, from what God has provided for you, and be among those who prefer others to themselves, even though they are in need. Thus was it revealed in the Bayān, and in the Qur’an, and in the Gospel, the Psalms, the Torah, and the scriptures of God, the All-Protecting, the Almighty, the Self-Subsisting.

612 In short, the Cause of God is sanctified above all these matters, and everything created between the heavens and the earth has been created especially for the Manifestations of the Cause. Yet, by God, besides whom there is no other god, those Souls who shine from the horizon of oneness have always contented themselves with little and, in these matters, have given others precedence over Their own blessed selves. It has often happened that They sent whatever was available in the house expressly to the loved ones, while the members of Their household went hungry that night or day. Praise be to God in every circumstance! He knows what you do not know, you company of slanderers.

613 If everyone on earth, together with all the fine blessings and good fruits created upon it, were brought into a single gathering for the sake of one believer in God, out of love for God, this would not be, nor would it ever be, extravagance. But if you and those like you consume a morsel of bread, you have been and will be extravagant, for extravagance is manifest in heedlessness of God. That very morsel of bread, tormented by that person who turns away from God, takes refuge in God from having become his portion. By God, everything he eats laments, cries out, and says, “My God, for what crime have You made me the sustenance of this wicked man who has denied You and Your signs?” Yet you have not perceived this, nor will you. And leaving these matters aside—

614 By God, besides whom there is no other god, and by His own blessed countenance, most of the time there was no food for the day in the household. On many days, bread for the household was obtained only toward the afternoon, and even then on credit. Sometimes, when some of the companions offered hospitality, He would invite some of the loved ones out of love for God. After the recitation of divine verses and words of everlasting glory, each person would receive his share. The purpose of this was to express love. In short, in those days it never occurred to anyone that a veiled opponent like you would arise and object to such things. Yet, by God, besides whom there is no other god, that person who turns away from God and has put all these words into your mind has lived in comfort all his days, occupied with pleasure with numerous women. By His own truth, worldly finery has always been at that man’s disposal, and whenever there was the slightest commotion in Iraq, he would set off for Basra or other lands, as everyone knows. Yet the words of the True One only increase your rancor and aversion.

615 You who are unaware and blind! What do you say of Ḥusayn ibn ʿAlī, who, when departing from the good and blessed city of Medina, set out with golden howdahs and every outward provision? Indeed, people like you say that, because His camel driver had seen that His blessed waistband was embroidered with pearls, he went there after His martyrdom, and then there occurred what this servant is ashamed to mention. No doubt you will attribute that, too, to worldliness.

616 Apart from all these matters, in this Revelation worldly means have not been available up to now for you or anyone else to object to. At every moment We have been in affliction and great hardship, if you are fair-minded. As for what you have written at the prompting of that opponent who puts words into your mind—that the Beauty of Oneness associated with rabble and scoundrels and engaged in idle conversations—your later words and your earlier words give you the lie. You write that for twenty years He sat and enjoyed companionship with mystics, and then you write, “with rabble and scoundrels.” Die in your rage, heedless one! You are the one mentioned in the former Book in His words—exalted is He—“Like a dog: if you assail it, it pants, and if you leave it alone, it pants.” In every condition they speak from desire and tread the paths of self. Yes, by God, had He not associated with rabble, He would certainly not have associated with Siyyid Muḥammad and his guide. People like these have been the rabble and scoundrels. Otherwise, who were that rabble, and who were those scoundrels?

617 Clearly, you have regarded the divine words as idle talk, for He spoke nothing but divine verses. Go and ask the people of Iraq. What a wealth of truthful words they have put into your mind! Everything gives you the lie, yet you do not perceive it. Furthermore, you unjust oppressor, where were you, and where did you see Him sitting with rabble and scoundrels? Clearly, you have taken the believers in God to be the lowly and the scoundrels. In the days of every revelation, the idolaters have always uttered precisely these words, as in His saying—exalted is He—“We see that none have followed you but those who are the lowliest among us, at first sight.” As mentioned before, it is fitting for you to utter such words. By God, this very statement of yours has been and will remain a proof of the truth of the True One. May God curse the first oppressor who wronged the Primal Point in His latter manifestation, slandered His person, denied His signs, and filled His breast with his rancor and hatred. With this curse, all who are in the heavens and the earths curse him.

618 By God, besides whom there is no other god, afflictions have befallen this wronged Beauty such as have befallen no one from the beginning of creation until now. You have denied something witnessed by all the world. For example, everyone has seen that in the Cause of God He has flattered no one and sought refuge with no one. In Iraq, when His Excellency the Mushīr wished to meet with Him, He did not accept, until at last He went to the mosque, as everyone has heard. After His arrival in the Great City, too, He visited no one and turned to no one, and all are witnesses to this. Despite this, you write of such a Person that He associated with rabble and scoundrels. Yet there is one who complained to everyone for a morsel of bread—the copies of his petitions still exist—who sent a woman to the government residence specifically for bread, ran to Istanbul, and sought refuge with every person. The deeds of such a person are acceptable, while the deeds of the True One are unacceptable! Yes, by God, this is what befits you and your own selves.

619 Reflect a little on how grave this very statement by people like you is, and how you have conceived the idea of concealing the radiant Sun behind the veils of self and desire and rendering His deeds fruitless. No, by God, no one but people like you would pay attention to such words, for all have seen the deeds of the True One with their own eyes. One day He said that afflictions greater than these lay concealed in the tabernacle of destiny and must descend, as He had seen a dream in Iraq from which this could be understood. He said—and His word is truth—“I was asleep on a dark night following a bright one, when I saw that the Prophets and Messengers had gathered around Me. They were seated on every side of Me, and all were lamenting, weeping, crying out, and wailing. I was bewildered within Myself and questioned them. Then their weeping and cries intensified, and they said, ‘For You, O Most Great Mystery, O Temple of Eternity!’ They wept so much that I wept at their weeping, and

620 when I heard the weeping of the people of the Supreme Concourse, in that state they addressed Me and said, ‘Great indeed is Your affliction, O Tree beyond which there is no passing, and momentous is Your destiny, O Mystery of the next world and this one. Be patient, O Most Great Sign and Manifestation of the Primal Point. Again, be patient, O Uttermost Tree and Manifestation of the Decree in the kingdom of its fulfillment. You will see with Your own eyes what none of the company of Prophets has seen, witness what no one in all the worlds has witnessed, and hear what the ears of the chosen ones and the beloved have never heard. Be patient, be patient, O hidden Mystery of God, His treasured Symbol, His irrevocable Word, and His sealed Book.’

621 “I was with them that night, speaking to them and hearing them speak to Me, until dawn drew near and I raised My head from sleep. I wondered within Myself what this affliction could be that no one in creation had witnessed. I told no one of it until one of the scholars of Iraq, named ʿAbd al-Salām Afandī, came into My presence. When he arrived, We related it to him, informed him, and recounted to him what God had shown Me in the dream. He was deeply saddened in Our presence. Such was the matter, yet the people are in manifest heedlessness.”

622 This is a dream that He saw in Iraq and recounted, and in these days part of it has been fulfilled: We have heard from your utterances what no one has ever heard before and have seen what no one has seen. He said, “In every circumstance We are patient and thankful.” The entire concern of those souls is why the True One has appeared. By God, this did not originate with Me, but with Him who sent Me with the truth and made Me a mercy to all the worlds.

623 His words—great is his rancor: “You mentioned that they had sent a woman to the government residence to present a petition. First, oppression will lead to more than this. Moreover, bravo to you! With your claim to being in the right and your cleverness, you have made yet another attempt to undermine the Prophets and holy ones. Did not Her Holiness Fāṭimih, for the sake of Fadak, take Ḥasan and Ḥusayn by the hand and bring them to the doors of the Emigrants and the Helpers? Well done!” End of quotation.

624 Silence your tongue, you who disbelieve in God! Again, silence your tongue, you who associate partners with God! Again, silence your tongue, you who wage war against God!

625 Even if I repeated those words from this moment until the end that has no end, you would deserve them. Indeed, they are nothing beside what you have committed against God, you who are heedless of the Cause of God and arrogant toward God Himself.

626 After arriving in this land, the Blessed Beauty, His Holiness Kalīm, and Mīrzā Yaḥyā each resided in a separate house. This land is not a place where one person can oppress another. The wrong committed by the one who associates partners with God took place before the Most Great Separation had occurred. After what lay within his breast was revealed, the division and separation took place, as has been mentioned. Since then there has never been another meeting, nor has any spoken exchange taken place. Ask the people of that land, so that you may find a path to the truth.

627 Moreover, the country belongs to the Ottoman government, and in this land all are prisoners and strangers. After he disclosed what lay hidden within him, no one associated with him except Darvīsh Ṣidqʿalī, who visited him to attend to his needs. Likewise, one person served the Most Holy Beauty and one served His Holiness Kalīm, while all the other companions were dispersed in separate dwellings. What, then, was this oppression, and how did it come about? Every atom knows that the cause of the wrongdoing was that oppressor, who consented to his own disgrace and brought about the separation.

628 Yet you say what you do not understand. You have accounted the very root of the tree of oppression to be justice, and have judged the tree of grace and justice to be oppressive. This is a plot by you and them against the Cause of God. You do not have enough discernment to reflect on something you have heard. In those very days when his wife went to the government residence—by God—at the beginning of the month, Darvīsh, who acted as his agent and is still alive, had taken his allotted portion of the money brought in each city, together with a bundle of belongings. Several people had also newly arrived in this land, and all knew of it, including Jināb-i Mīrzā Ḥaydar ʿAlī, Salmān, and Ustād ʿAbd al-Karīm of the people of Ṣād. Yet he deliberately sent his wife to the government residence, and she said these very words about the All-Glorious Beauty: “He has brought us here and gives us neither bread nor clothing.” Such a scandal arose in this land that one of its consuls was greatly astonished and remarked to someone, “A strange thing has happened today.” All the Persians began to gloat, saying that this community had neither chastity nor decency.

629 Your own heedless self knows at least this much: if a Jew came to Him, such perishable wealth as was available would not be withheld from him. Yet you wrote what you wrote and sullied Fadak. But rest assured that in every land you have a “Mistress of Women,” and many of that succession of “perfect mirrors, one after another,” are to be found in every land. The All-Glorious Beauty resided in Iraq for twelve years, and no one saw Him do anything even remotely improper or contrary to what is right. Ask all the people of Iraq. Now, one of that succession of “perfect mirrors, one after another,” is in Iraq: you should ask the people of Iraq about him. He wrote to him, “We have made you a shining light to all the worlds,” and likewise, “He is the light within the light, and the likeness of his light is as the light of a light from the light of God.” To the Mother of Obscenities he wrote this blessed verse of the Quran, word for word: “O Mary, be devoutly obedient to your Lord, prostrate yourself, and bow with those who bow. God has purified you and chosen you above the women of all the worlds.” Now take pleasure in that “Mistress of Women” and that “perfect mirror”! If only you had gone there too and performed a prostration! May God curse those who have displaced the word of God from its proper place, turned away from His Beauty, and taken a calf for themselves in His stead. What is wrong with these people that they can scarcely understand a word from God, the Most High, the Almighty?

630 By God, I lament as I write these words, for matters have reached a point where such things must be discussed. You have dishonored God and yourselves. Purify your heart of the filth of the delusions of those who associate partners with God, and do not link mention of the Prophets and Messengers with accounts of those souls who associate partners with Him. All those Countenances of the All-Merciful gave their lives in the path of the Beloved and paid no heed to anyone on earth for the sake of bread. Now judge fairly: you are one who draws comparisons with these servants—what sort of deed have you compared to what other deed? Be fair, that you may be treated fairly, and have mercy on the Cause of Him in whose path the blood of those brought near and of God’s sincere servants has been shed.

631 His words—great is his falsehood: “Another point I submit concerns the arrival of Ḥājī Siyyid Muḥammad in Istanbul. I myself know all about it and was present. On the first night he came to the embassy, Jināb-i Mushīr al-Dawlih was there, together with His Honor Ḥājī Mīrzā Ṣafī and myself, and no one else. Mushīr al-Dawlih asked him why he had come. He said, ‘To secure my release.’ I also saw the petition to ʿĀlī Pāshā. Then their messengers came and began to speak ill of him, and in response to this disparagement he too said as much as was necessary.” End of quotation.

632 God has blackened the face of the liar. If he came solely to secure his release, who supplied the list of people, of which no one had any knowledge? There were eight of them, but he submitted in writing, “We are twenty-one.” He spent six months waiting until, through a thousand deceptions, he attained his own purpose and that of his companion. They sold the Joseph of the Cause for a few dirhams. Shame on them and their faithfulness! By God, through their actions the Cause of God was dishonored among the people.

633 In the official order that came from Istanbul, they wrote that some of his companions had come and complained, “He does not give us bread.” That order is still in existence, yet he has written what he has written. May God curse those who have bartered the honor of God for the vanities of this world, and who, when alone with the devils, disavow God and His Cause, then write to fools like themselves, “We are the leaders of the Cause, the mirrors of the servants, the fruits of divine unity, and the leaves of detachment.” But those whose sight God has made keen witness and perceive what they have committed in this vain life. They are not deceived, for in every one of their actions they detect the odors of falsehood, hypocrisy, deceit, and dissension. They are among the gems of creation amid the servants of God and His creatures. May God’s remembrance and praise rest upon them at every moment, time after time, and at all times.

634 By God, what a fine disciple you are, and how diligently you have occupied yourself with concealing indecency after witnessing it! But the matter is not hidden from the All-Seeing, the All-Informed. We know what slanders he has uttered and how he has resolved upon murder. They have even said that He sent someone to Tehran expressly to carry out a shameful deed. May God’s curse be upon the slanderers and liars.

635 Now observe the conduct of the True One: He entered that same city and had no dealings with anyone, and no compromise was made with anyone concerning the Cause of God. But when that wicked man saw that the Persian ambassador was displeased with Him, he went to that residence and sought refuge there, solely to deceive, dissemble, and act hypocritically toward the True One, and said what he said. If he had wanted his release, no one would have objected. He was by no means better known than Ḥājī Mīrzā Aḥmad, nor more esteemed than the others who left; indeed, some had never even heard of him. By God, he came expressly for a stipend, as the petitions that he and Mīrzā Yaḥyā wrote to the office of the Mushīr are also here in this land, and

636 From Istanbul they inquired here about all these matters, saying, “He says that seven people have left and the rest are occupied in earning a living, while Mīrzā Yaḥyā and I number twenty-one and have no stipend.” His Excellency the Governor sent someone expressly to inquire, saying that the Sublime Porte had reported this. Yet you write that it was to secure his release. Now that they have attained the utmost of their desire, if only they would stop writing far and wide that they have no bread!

637 Now judge fairly: that is the conduct of the True One, and this is the conduct of this group. From which can the fragrance of truth be perceived? By God, such leaders are very well suited to people like you. Do not imagine that the Persian ambassador is unaware. No, by God! A day will come when he himself will affirm the conduct of the True One and repudiate the conduct of those people. Even if you are deceived, he will not be.

638 As for the messengers, I do not know whom he meant. If he meant His Honor, the servant present before the Face, by God, he came to that city for a particular matter that had nothing to do with him, and he visited no one. All are witnesses and can testify that in this land that wicked man attributed every shameful and vile deed to the True One, and [corrupted original text] that land too they have said what no one has ever said of anyone. The servant present before the Face now says: “Did you not come yourself, liar that you are, and report that Siyyid Muḥammad was saying such and such? How many unseemly statements about Him did you repeat, saying that he was uttering them! You even said that it was not good for talk to arise over the stipend and that the matter should be settled somehow.”

639 “I replied that there had been no dispute and would be none. Our turning away from those people was for the sake of the Cause of God, not for outward concerns. Let them give the entire stipend to those idolatrous souls; there has been no quarrel or contention. On several occasions I asked you to arrange for us to meet with him in one place, so that it would become clear to you that everything he had said was sheer slander. You came back and said that he would not agree.”

640 By God, all existence is bewildered by your lies and our fabrications! You who lack understanding! For years this Mīrzā Yaḥyā was in His service. Has he ever heard anyone associated with Him say anything bad or offensive about him? Even an ignorant person knows that whatever happens has brought, and will bring, disgrace upon the Cause. By God, He has always said, “I would willingly be martyred a thousand times rather than have these matters spoken of among the people—that one of those associated with Him has complained about bread and sent petitions to the cities.” May God curse those who have committed what has caused all things to lament and the hearts of those brought near to Him to burn.

641 By God, besides whom there is no god, all of us servants would willingly have been killed rather than have such things brought up. We are duty-bound to say what we now say, for we see that a group has resolved to violate the honor of the Cause of God. They have never adhered to any religion and still do not, and for the sake of leadership they have cast themselves, and continue to cast themselves, into destruction. We have resided in this land for nearly five years, and He has never sent anyone to the Sublime Porte, nor has any request been made. Whatever has befallen us, we have borne patiently out of reverence for the Cause of God. But that wicked man has committed what no one has ever committed before. We hope they will not attain their aim, for he has utterly destroyed the honor of an entire community. He himself has never had, and will never have, any standing.

642 Judge fairly yourself: where had his name ever been mentioned? How had the officials of the exalted government ever known him? You yourself know through whom he became known, but out of hatred for God you conceal it. You are among those who have covered up the faults of the idolaters and what they have committed to dishonor God. Thus has the matter now issued from this speaking Pen, the truthful, the trustworthy.

643 His words—great is his folly: “It is very, very astonishing that for nearly twenty years you worshipped Mīrzā Yaḥyā as God. I know this myself, for you admitted it in my own presence in Isfahan. Now he has been rejected. What answer should I give Āqā Mīrzā Muḥammad ʿAlī, Mīrzā Abu’l-Ḥasan, the others, and Mīrzā Ḥusayn of Varzanih, who say that these people attach themselves to someone new every day, consider someone to be the truth, and then declare him false? This is due to their lack of discernment, because truth does not become falsehood, and this is a contradiction. Their religion is caprice. As Mīrzā Abu’l-Ḥasan said to me in Tehran, ‘What a pity for you! On what grounds was he at first the truth and God, and now false and an unbelieving servant?’ Oh, bravo! How astonishing! I ask you for just one fair judgment, for God’s sake! This is someone who, on certain occasions, associated with you at a time when we were on an equal footing. At present neither of us can claim superiority over the other, however much we may have advanced in perfection.” End of quotation.

644 First, this objection applies to you and those like you, for years you regarded as leaders people more wicked than any who have ever appeared in creation. It was at their command that the Manifestation of the divine Self was martyred with the utmost cruelty. Second, we admit that we servants were sincerely devoted to him, for certain descriptions appeared in the words of His Most Glorious Presence, and we supposed that those descriptions referred to that imagined person, until God revealed to us what He revealed, and we learned of deeds of his that neither Nimrod nor Pharaoh had committed. Therefore we shattered the idol of delusion and recognized what was intended in His most wondrous and incomparable words. Then we learned about him and the true nature of his affair what had been concealed from you and those like you. If you knew, you would flee from him, tear through the veils of glory, turn toward the Dawning-Place of Beauty, and be among those who are certain.

645 We praise God that, through the strength of trust in Him and detachment, we shattered the idol of delusion and reached the waters of certainty. Would that you too had been there and seen with your own eyes! We praise God for this and thank Him at all times. By God, if you had a particle of fairness, you would surely speak day and night of the qualities of these servants, for our conduct is a truthful testimony to our faith and a clear and evident witness. We broke the chains of imitation, attained the True One, and freed ourselves from the delusion that had seized every heart. You poor, destitute one! You are deeply, deeply immersed in delusion. If only you had seen him, known him, and learned what he possessed! Would that a meeting had taken place, that the spiritual knowledge of which he spoke in gatherings had been discussed, and that you had learned what remains veiled from you concerning him in these days.

646 If you do not know us, we will make ourselves known to you, so that you may understand and be among those who know. We are servants whose sight God has made keen; we have recognized Him through Himself and detached ourselves from all the worlds. No obstruction by anyone, no schemer’s plotting, no deceiver’s deception, and no doubt in the hearts of the malicious have held us back. We praise God for making known to us His own Self, who was promised in His holy, firmly established, wondrous Tablets. Neither the veils of those who disbelieved in God nor the veils of glory of those who associated partners with His Self, the One, the Unique, the Almighty, the All-Praised, prevented us from recognizing Him.

647 It is astonishing that you have occupied yourself with investigations of this kind. When the great river has appeared, is it fitting for anyone to turn toward the water of a pool? Be fair, then, you ass! When the sun has risen, should anyone seek illumination from a lamp, even if it is a real lamp? Be fair, heedless one, through whose heedlessness all things have lamented, though you remain within thick veils. When the Most Great Ocean surges, would a rational person turn toward a mirage on a plain? No, by our Lord, the All-Merciful, the Ever-Merciful! Yet you will never grasp what We recount, for your heart has been deprived of the breezes of God, the All-Powerful, the Most High, the All-Wise.

648 By God, women are ashamed of your arguments. Satan was known by a name in every heaven and was a teacher of the Kingdom. For more than a thousand years he bore every name, and then, all at once, he was cast out. These things are among the wonders of divine power. Do you deny them? Even if you deny them, His servants who are certain affirm them.

649 Have you not heard that Judas Iscariot was one of the twelve Letters of the Gospel, and afterward displayed such wickedness that he delivered the Word of God into the hands of the Jews? Would that your leader had committed his deed, for Judas repented after that heinous act. Your guide, however, has exchanged and sold the Point of the Bayán, all the Prophets and Messengers, and the sanctity of God for earthly vanities, as has been recounted, and you are aware of it all.

650 And what do you say about Ismaʿil, the son of His Holiness Ṣádiq? After he had written to all the provinces that Ismaʿil would be the Imam and leader of the people after him, certain things appeared from Ismaʿil after a time, and His Holiness dismissed him. In particular, Zarádih and some of the companions submitted: “O son of the Messenger of God, this is a weighty matter. How can someone be the Imam of the people and be true, and then become false?” His Holiness spoke of badá. Go and read Kulayní’s account.

651 You poor man, you know nothing of any matter—neither of the Bayán, nor of the Qur’an, nor of the Torah, nor of the Gospel. Many reversals have occurred in the unfolding of this matter, and this servant has not recounted them for the sake of brevity. They are recorded in your own books; even now, people in some regions regard Ismaʿil as the Imam. You have not even seen the readily available books and are uninformed. Your way is to utter words of [corrupted original text] and to argue with proofs of this kind. The discerning and well-informed have not been misled, nor will they be. Many things have happened of which no one is aware, for God concealed them in the past. If only you would abandon your desires and dwell for a time, for God’s sake, in the Beloved’s neighborhood, so that some hidden matters might be disclosed to you.

652 Nor do you know about Balaam son of Beor, who was far more renowned than your leader. His spiritual authority was acknowledged; nevertheless, after the sun of Moses rose from the horizon of your Lord, the All-Merciful, the Ever-Merciful, he did what he did, and through this God changed his light into fire. He indeed has power over all things. Would that you knew about your own leader! By God, besides whom there is no other god, the hand of power and might took a handful of dust and breathed into it a spirit from among the spirits created by His will. When it stirred and stood upright, He clothed it in the garment of names between earth and heaven, for a wisdom known to no one but His own Self, the All-Knowing, the All-Informed. It has never been, nor will it ever be, more than this. Then, when he turned against God, God turned against him as he had turned. Thus does He recompense His transgressing servants.

653 Listen to the words of this servant, and do not cause people to fall into delusion. Have mercy on the Cause of God and on the people. In every age, deluded souls have gathered together and deprived people of the shore of the ocean of oneness. Reflect a little on how many misconceptions existed among the people of the Qur’an concerning the appearance of the Qá’im, the advent of the Resurrection, and the coming of the Hour. After the appearance of the Primal Point—may the souls of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—it became clear that they had all been mistaken and had not drunk a single drop from the ocean of knowledge. That Ancient Beauty shattered the idols of delusion and desire with immense difficulty, and now others like those souls have set out to afflict people with new delusions. Say: Fear the one God, and do not commit a deed that would cause blood rather than tears to flow from the eyes of those near to Him. Do not regard truth as falsehood, or reckon falsehood as truth. Yes, the True One has always been true, but you have not recognized that True One.

654 It has been acknowledged that, after the Point, truth is confined to the Letters of the Living, for the entire creation of the Bayán has been and continues to be created beneath the shadow of the First Váḥid. The right of everyone possessing a right is established through these souls, as are all the attributes and most beautiful names. Nevertheless, addressing one of the Letters of the Living who had asked about the next Revelation, He says: “If I know that, on the day when the Luminary of inner meanings rises, you will not attain faith in Him, I pronounce upon you now the judgment of unbelief. And if I know that one of the Christians will believe in Him on the day of His Revelation, I would pronounce upon him now the judgment of faith, even though he is not a believer and no pleasing deed has appeared from him.” Reflect a little and recognize the True One.

655 These souls are true as long as they remain beneath the shadow of the True One, for at that time nothing is seen in them but the True One. After they hesitate, sovereignty remains within sovereignty, and the creature returns to its own kind and likeness. That True One who has never become false, nor ever will, is the Tree of Reality. Recognize this, if you are among those who know. Thus has the matter been expounded by the Ancient Revealer. Reflect, O servant, so that you may be among the rightly guided.

656 O heedless, immature one! Know that resurrection has had and will have limitless degrees, and distinguish the resurrection of names from other kinds. Many are the souls whose names have been raised without any outward trace. This is for a wisdom that no one will know. If you wish to know it, ask God your Lord with humility, reverence, and manifest repentance, so that He may teach you what is with Him. He is indeed the All-Knowing, the All-Informed. In earlier Tablets, something on this subject has been revealed by the Pen of the Cause. If you wish, consult those Tablets; perhaps you will be content with that much. The purpose of this mention is that you should not remain veiled by the names from the One who reveals them, although you have remained so veiled that it is inconceivable that the veil should ever be torn away except through God’s purpose and will.

657 The Point of the Bayán—exalted be His grandeur—has spoken even of the transformation of essential realities, let alone names, as some of this has already been mentioned. He also says—His words, exalted be His mention: “At every Revelation, the exponents of its affirmation are changed into negation. Yet in that Revelation God establishes His Cause and perfects His handiwork, for in it light is not changed into fire except at the Revelation of God. My sorrow is indeed for everyone in the Bayán from [corrupted original text].” End of quotation.

658 Observe that He explicitly says that light is changed into fire. Thus the light you have assumed to be light has had its essential reality changed into fire, yet he is unaware, and in utter heedlessness has been and remains occupied with distorting the divine words. Do not understand His statement, “except at the Revelation of God,” to mean that light is not transformed before the Revelation. Yes, by His own Self, the True One, all except the Manifestation Himself have been and continue to be transformed. These imaginary notions existed among the people, and the matter had not been disclosed until this Revelation. But when you tore through the veil of God’s sanctity, He therefore tore away the covering of your veil with the fingers of power and might.

659 The difference is that, from the time when the Manifestation of Oneness ascends to the Companion on high until the next Revelation, the manifest Will is not visibly present to pronounce judgment, although He is ever alive in the All-Glorious Horizon. But since outwardly no one knows Him and all souls are denied access to His presence, the judgment of transformation remains concealed until the time of the Revelation. At that time, if a light hesitates for less than an instant to say “Yes,” the judgment of fire is pronounced upon it from the source of innate divine knowledge. This was witnessed in the previous Revelation, and in this Revelation too it is seen with both the outward and the inward eye that souls who called themselves light are judged as fire in God’s sight, yet they themselves go about unaware.

660 At every moment, seek refuge in God, that you may not remain veiled from the Manifestation Himself, for He has always been and will ever be the One sought in His own right. He is the verse, “Nothing is like Him,” and He is the verse, “He neither begets nor is begotten.” Indeed, the manifestations of “He neither begets nor is begotten” are created beings in His sight, if you are certain. Yet not every soul can comprehend this station, but only those who are detached, who dwell in exalted understanding.

661 For this reason, the Point of the Primal Will—may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—when speaking of the Letters, the Mirrors, the Light, and the like, from the highest degrees of the names and attributes down to the lowest rank of things, states that these had no independent reality of their own. When they faced the true Sun, the radiance of infinite revelations shone upon them. In this station, nothing is seen in those manifestations but God. He says that you should look not to the names, but to that through which the names subsist, and likewise to that through which the attributes subsist. For all, through their turning to God and their faith in God, have been and are adorned with the wondrous, exalted robes of the names. Some are called Letters, some Mirrors, and some luminous Guides, and likewise manifestations of Beauty, Majesty, the Living, the Ancient, and other such most beautiful names of God. The manifestations of the revelation differ according to their respective conditions and capacities; otherwise, the revelation is one, and the One who reveals it is one.

662 One who is discerning and well informed understands that all these names refer to the faith that has existed and continues to exist within them. Set aside illusion and consider the first to believe, and likewise the second to believe, for the Bayán has praised no souls more highly than these two and the other Letters of the Living. Thus, in their visitation prayers, He has addressed them as “O pure Essence of God,” “O Being of God,” and “O Essence of God,” and in similar words. Now, if the first to believe had not been the person in question, but someone else, this station would assuredly have been bestowed upon that other person. If you immerse yourself in this ocean, you will comprehend some of the veiled, shrouded, hidden mysteries, distinguish the Sun of inner meanings from its shadow, and refrain from committing that over which every atom is lamenting.

663 Consider, for example: if He takes some dust in His blessed hand and declares that this handful of earth is the object before which all worlds bow down, it is true beyond any doubt. Likewise, if He takes a hundred other handfuls of earth and pronounces the same decree concerning them all, it has been and will remain in force. For in this station, regard is given to the command that has issued from the Source of command, not to a thing considered merely as a thing.

664 As for such veiled souls becoming heedless, through mention of successorship, of that through which the successor subsists, and of the higher degrees of guardianship, prophethood, messengership, and lordship: first, know—by God, besides whom there is no god—that the Primal Point never spoke such a word in the Bayán specifically concerning any individual. He did, however, graciously send a Tablet to Mírzá Sulaymán. At the beginning of that Tablet was a word that everyone was unable to read. Mírzá Sulaymán himself, during his return from Mákú, would say, “This word is ‘successorship,’ and He has written it to me.” In the utmost secrecy, he laid claim to this rank. Would that you had held fast to him in this matter! In God’s sight, he is more deserving than the person to whom you have attached yourselves. There has never been any other such mention, except that some Tablets have been written and circulated in the name of the Primal Point. They are all false, by the Lord of Bahá!

665 Second, this rank is not a rank that We would wish either to take away from someone or to affirm for someone. For the glory of the successor has always been and will ever be that through which successorship subsists; once severed from it, he has had and will have no standing before God. Thus the Primal Point—may the spirit of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him—states in this connection, His words, exalted be His mention: “For the essence of the whole matter is precisely this: he has remained veiled from that by which the successor is a successor. It is not that you should regard even this station of successorship as a special distinction; rather, look to that through which it subsists, which is the command of the Tree of Truth. For if, hypothetically, the Messenger of God had not appointed him under the name of successorship, today he would be spoken of by a name other than successorship. All this is so that, on the Day of Manifestation, you may not remain veiled by names, but may instead look to that through which the names of all things subsist, even the designation ‘Prophet.’” End of quotation.

666 Now reflect a little, that you may recognize that all are exalted through His command and honored through His mention. This condition holds true so long as they do not overstep their bounds. At the raising up of the primal Cause, all these names are withdrawn from those who bear them and return to the Kingdom. If a binding command descends anew, all the most beautiful names of God apply to them; otherwise, they return to their abode before one can say “Why?” or “By what?”

667 In short, turn your gaze away from these limitations and look to that Point which pervades all things. Do not regard that Point as imaginary, as all the servants of former times remained caught in imaginings and, to this day, have not attained recognition and never will, except those whom your Lord wills. This very Mírzá Yaḥyá, on whose account you have turned away from the True One, used to stand in My presence like one of the attendants, and the words of God were imparted to him, just as a number of people now stand before My face and receive them in the same manner every night and day. The difference, however, is that when a mere two words flowed from the tongue of that deluded soul, he committed what all the manifestations of negation had never committed. These souls now present before My face, by contrast, utter the most wondrous words, yet undertake nothing without His permission. Such has been the matter, if you are among those who know.

668 Although the words of those who associate partners with God have lodged in your heart, and it is difficult to efface them unless you drink from this Salsabíl of loving-kindness that flows and courses through the wellsprings of the words by means of the words themselves. Thus have We told you, informed you, and recounted to you what you were veiled from, that you may rend the veils through the power of your Lord, the Almighty, the All-Bountiful, witness what you have never witnessed before, and recognize what you were heedless of. Yet you will not succeed in this unless you detach yourself from all that has been created between the heavens and the earth and turn toward God, the King, the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the One who chooses.

669 O servants! In seeking to recognize this Revelation, do not cling to the words and allusions of those who turn away, or to those souls’ affirmations and denials. Look instead to the foundation of the Cause itself, for most people have always been and continue to be seen veiled and deluded. The leaders of the age, intent on consolidating their own authority, have barred people from the realm of oneness and the recognition of God’s verses, and have deprived them of hope, and will continue to do so. Consider, for example, the Revelation of the Point of the Qur’an: He appeared with the verses of God, and in the Qur’an no proof or testimony was revealed other than the verses. Nevertheless, after the Point of the Qur’an ascended to the Companion on high and the fragrances of the All-Merciful ceased, the matter fell into the hands of the learned. Those souls completely barred people from recognizing the verses, saying, “You cannot comprehend the verses of God; knowledge of them rests with us.” Thus the evidentiary authority of the verses disappeared, the reins of the people passed into those souls’ hands, and they instructed people in imaginings that cannot be recounted, as you have seen and heard. They interpreted all the verses revealed concerning the next Manifestation, the Resurrection, and the like according to their own desires. The people, too, followed those souls unawares, until matters reached the point where all the harm of those imaginings befell the Tree of the next Revelation, and

670 After the appearance of the Promised One in the year sixty, the winds of grace blew anew, the heavens of knowledge were raised, the earth of recognition was spread out, and the verses of God descended from the heaven of His will. Yet, because of their former imaginings, people hesitated to acknowledge the evidentiary authority of the verses, saying, “We do not comprehend them.” The True One—exalted be His mention—is, however, far too great, generous, and merciful to send the Manifestation of His own Self, command everyone to recognize Him, which is itself the recognition of God, and yet send a proof beyond people’s comprehension. Far exalted is He above such a thing!

671 In the end, they were so absorbed in and veiled by their own imaginings that they did not regard the verses of God as sufficient, even though the explicit text of the Qur’an states, “Is it not enough for them that We have sent down to you the Book?” All argued, “Comprehension of the verses is beyond our reason and understanding; something else must appear.” They altered God’s blessing and His proof, and desired something other than what God desired. Thus most turned away and returned to the abyss. The more fiercely those who associated partners with God turned away, the more firmly that Sun of inner meanings revealed the evidentiary authority of the verses, to the point that He confined all proof and testimony to the verses alone.

672 And despite those who associate partners with God, this verse descended from the heaven of oneness: “If an ant wished to exercise power over the Qur’an, its inner meaning, and the inner meaning of its inner meaning, as over the black of its own eyes, it would be able to do so, for the mystery of self-sufficiency and the revelation of oneness have stirred within every thing.” This verse was revealed so that all might realize that the doors of mercy and loving care are not closed. Whoever turns toward God perceives the evidentiary authority of the verses of God through the verse itself. Understanding this station has never depended, nor will it ever depend, on outward learning; rather, it depends on pure souls and unsullied hearts turned toward Him. Blessed are those who turn toward Him.

673 Likewise, that Quintessence of meanings tore through all the veils and imaginings that had existed before and by which people had been veiled and deluded, and returned to the horizon of “Indeed, I am alive in the all-glorious horizon.” Now a number of deluded souls, intent on establishing and preserving their leadership, have resolved first to erase from the Bayán the evidentiary authority of the verses. They have striven greatly in this matter and continue to do so, employing every trick and stratagem. But God refuses to do anything other than establish His verses in truth, even though those who associate partners with Him deny them. Their hypocrisy has now reached such a degree that if anyone speaks of the evidentiary authority of the verses, they ridicule and mock him, exactly like the religious communities of the past. Indeed, these people are more ignorant and greater in hypocrisy, if you only knew.

674 Veiled souls have always confined the all-embracing knowledge of God, and for this reason most people have remained deprived of the outpourings of the All-Merciful and the loving care of the Lord. Thus, one day, the very person who put this notion forward said in mockery of the Point of the Bayán: “He has stated that no one is capable of producing a single verse, yet we see that some people are able to utter two words of verses. On this basis, these people too would be the True One.” By God, anyone who has caught the fragrance of the paradise of meanings will recognize from this statement his unbelief, hypocrisy, rebellion, and opposition to all the Prophets and Messengers.

675 It became evident that he had never been nourished by the heavenly waters of meanings and had remained utterly deprived of the divine paradise of spiritual understanding, for what His Holiness intended by this statement is abundantly clear. The word of the True One, even if it consists of only two letters, has always been and will always be sovereign over the words of the people of the earth. By God, from the fragrance of that word alone, those endowed with understanding perceive the fragrances of the True One and turn toward His paradise. Consider: if a cup is drawn from a sweet, refreshing river, those with a sense of taste perceive the source of this water and recognize its source from its sweetness, although outwardly it is only a cup of water. And since the wellspring of knowledge, recognition, loving care, generosity, grace, power, glory, and grandeur flows and pervades all things, the sincere and those brought near perceive through that word and recognize its source. Thus He has explained it to you, so that you may be guided by the guidance of God, your Lord, and pay no heed to the hosts of devils.

676 If someone utters two words but is at a loss for a third, it is evident that these have not come, nor will they ever come, from this flowing, all-pervading wellspring. By God, O servant, the True One has always been and will always be distinguished from all others in every aspect of His being. Do not confuse the matter for yourself. If you wish, put it to the test: enter My presence and ask for a single verse of the verses of God. Then you will behold the ocean that surges of itself and for itself, whose waves have no end, and you will discover the sanctuary of meanings and recognition and the treasury of knowledge and wisdom, and be among those who are certain.

677 Since the Point of the Bayán—exalted be His majesty—specifically stated throughout the Bayán that this Most Great Revelation would appear through verses, it has therefore appeared through verses. Otherwise, everything that has appeared from Him is a proof to all the worlds. In this Most Great Revelation, all the signs of power, all the signs in the world and within human souls, have been witnessed, and none denies them except the treacherous and deceitful.

678 Today everyone must avoid these wicked souls who associate partners with God, for they have appeared with a deceit whose like is impossible in creation. Thus, a letter has been written from the land of Ṣád to His Honor Ismu’lláh, Áqá Siyyid Mahdí—may the glory of God rest upon him—reporting that the Mother of Abominations and Sins has sent a letter to that land. Its purport is that a Tablet in the handwriting of His Holiness the Most Exalted had been buried in their house, and that “I have brought that Tablet out.” In that Tablet, certain passages are inscribed and attributed to the Primal Point, purportedly written to the All-Glorious Beauty in commendation of Mírzá Yaḥyá. By God, besides whom there is no other god, we were all astonished at the fabrications of these souls and the deceit of these people.

679 Afterward, the Source of authority commanded that they go and ask her from which part of the house she had brought out that Tablet, and tell her, “Show it,” so that she might come to her senses and be among those who repent. They have risen to fabricate falsehoods with such vehemence. These actions follow instructions that Mírzá Yaḥyá writes and sends from here. May God’s curse rest upon the liars and fabricators. Thereupon the Point of the Bayán, on the throne of paradise, disavows them and says: “Ruin upon you, you who associate partners with God! Fire upon you, you people of malice!”

680 Today one must sharpen one’s sight, purify one’s heart, and sanctify one’s breast, so that a person may recognize and distinguish himself from others. Although this rank is very lofty and great, it is very easy and simple for the fair-minded. Yet the people of the Bayán have now reached such a state, and have objected to the True One over such matters, that from the beginning of creation until now no one has objected to the True One over anything like them. All of us servants have circled around Him for years. By God the Almighty, He has never possessed anything of this world’s finery, and if there has been anything, we have all shared in it. Anyone who enters the Most Great House is treated as though he himself were its owner, and the utmost loving care is lavished upon him. Nevertheless, the people of the Bayán, in opposition to God, have objected to everything that has appeared from the True One, even the drinking of tea, and likewise to His clothing and similar things. Shame on them and their sense of shame!

681 I do not know what has happened that every soul has halted at the mountain pass and become afflicted. In these days a Tablet has dawned and descended from the dayspring of the will of the All-Merciful, addressed specifically to one of the loved ones. It is quoted here to awaken them, so that they may perhaps come to their senses and hear once again the words of the Primal Point from a wondrous, all-glorious tongue, feel ashamed at His cries, lamentations, and weeping, and content themselves with what they have already done. This is the text of what descended from the dominion of God, the Most High, the Almighty.

682 In the name of God, the Most Holy, the High, the Most Exalted.

683 This is the Book of the Primal Point to those who have believed in God, the One, the Unique, the Almighty, the All-Knowing. In it He addresses those among the people of the Bayán who have hesitated in this Cause, so that they may become aware of the wonders of God’s words and rise from the sleep of heedlessness in this radiant, luminous dawn. Say: We commanded you in the Book not to take precedence over the community from which would appear the Beloved of those endowed with understanding and the Desire of all who are in the heavens and on earth. We commanded you, should you attain the presence of God, to stand before His face and then utter on My behalf these mighty, exalted words: “Upon You, O Bahá’u’lláh, and upon Your kindred, be the remembrance of God and the praise of every thing, at every moment, before every moment, and after every moment.” We made these words an honor for the people of the Bayán, so that through them they might ascend to the heights of holiness and be among those who attain their goal. Yet they abandoned what they were commanded, so that no one appeared before His face with what We had enjoined upon them in Tablets of great glory. Instead, from every horizon they hurled the shafts of hypocrisy at Him. At this I wept, and the inhabitants of the dominion of grandeur wept, and then the faithful Spirit.

684 Say: My people, feel shame before My beauty! He who has appeared in truth is indeed the Glory of all the worlds, if you are among those endowed with understanding. He is indeed Bahá’u’lláh, and upon Him be the remembrance of God and His praise, and the praise of the inhabitants of the highest assembly, the praise of the inhabitants of the dominion of eternity, and the praise of every thing at every moment. Beware lest you be veiled by anything created between earth and heaven. Hasten to the paradise of His good pleasure, and do not be among those who sleep.

685 Say: His beauty was truly My beauty, and His own Self My Self, and everything We sent down in the Bayán was revealed for His firm and wondrous Cause. Fear God, and do not dispute concerning Him of whom We informed you and whose appearance We announced to you. I took the covenant concerning His Self before the covenant concerning My own Self, and every thing bears witness to this, if you are among those who deny it. By God! Through one of His melodies the realities of all things were born anew, and through another melody the hearts of those brought near were drawn to Him. Beware lest anything veil you from Him, meeting whom was the very essence of meeting Me. He sacrificed Himself in My path, just as I sacrificed Myself in His path, out of love for His mighty, unassailable beauty.

686 Say: Were it not for Him, the Ḥāʾ would not have been joined to the Bāʾ, nor would the form of the Hāʾ have been established upon the Wāw, nor would anything that has been or will be have been created, if you are among those who perceive. Were it not for Him, I would not have cast Myself into the hands of those who associate partners with God, nor would I have been suspended in the air. By God! In My yearning for Him and My longing for His Self, I bore what the Prophets and Messengers did not bear, and willingly accepted all this for Myself, lest anything befall Him that would grieve His most tender, most delicate, gracious, unassailable heart. Throughout the Bayán We enjoined you not to cause anyone sorrow, that perhaps no sorrow might befall Him. Otherwise, what have I to do with mentioning you or concerning Myself with you, O assembly of those who have turned away?

687 In the Bayán I intended none but His Self; by its remembrances, none but His remembrance; and by its names, none but His blessed, most unassailable, most holy, most wondrous, incomparable Name.
