By My life! If I made mention of lordship, I intended only His lordship over all things. If mention of divinity flowed from My pen, My purpose was none but the God of all worlds. If mention of the Desired One flowed from My pen, He was My Desired One; and likewise with the Beloved: He was My Beloved and the Beloved of those who know. If I made mention of prostration, I intended only prostration before His exalted, mighty, unassailable countenance. If I praised anyone, My heart intended only to praise His Self. If I commanded people to perform a deed, I intended only that it be done for His good pleasure on the day of His appearance. Everything sent down to Me from the dominion of My Lord, the All-Knowing, the All-Wise, bears witness to this. I made everything dependent on His confirmation and good pleasure. He is indeed the One who in His own Self has been the God of all worlds and the Desire of all who seek. If you look closely, you will surely see the Manifestations of “He does whatever He wills” among those who worship in His shadow. Yet you have done to His Self what neither the people of the Qur’an did to Me nor the assembly of the Jews to the Spirit.

688 Alas, alas for the burning of My heart and the lamentation of My soul at what has befallen My Beloved at the hands of those who associate partners with God! Shame on you and your faithfulness, O company of wrongdoers! We created faithfulness and courtesy for His sake, so that at His appearance you might not do anything that would cause My reality and the realities of all things to grieve. Yet you have overstepped the bounds set in the Book of God, the King, the Most High, the Most Great. You have torn the veil of modesty and then the covering of reverence, and have done what the Pen of creation is ashamed to mention between earth and heaven.

689 Alas, alas for what you have inflicted on this Wronged One, alone and a stranger! I do not know what you will do to Him hereafter. No, by My all-knowing Self! Rather, I do know, and with Me is the knowledge of all things in a Tablet that God has kept safe from the [corrupted original text] of those who associate partners with Him. We told Him beforehand what had befallen Him and what would befall Him, although He Himself knew what lay within the hearts of all the world. Nothing will escape His knowledge, nor will anything created by a word from Him elude His grasp. There is no God but Him, the Unique, the Raiser of the dead, the Giver of life, the Bringer of death.

690 Say: My people, He is indeed the One who, if He wished to make everyone in the heavens and on earth an enduring testimony from Him, would have the power to do so. This is easy and effortless for Him. He is indeed the One who created the Paradise of the Bayán for Himself. From Him all things originate and to Him they return, if you are among those who know. Yet you were unwilling that He in whose grasp was the kingdom of creation should call Himself by any name, even though those names and their kingdom were created by His mighty, unassailable command. Alas, alas for your heedlessness, O people of the Bayán! Alas, alas for your veiling, O assembly of those who associate partners with God!

691 When you went to excess in your own estimation and, as you imagined, attained the heights of knowledge, you invoked a succession conferred by testament upon one of His enemies, and used it to argue against God, through whom the laws of the religions were ordained among earlier and later generations. You returned to the arguments used by the people of the Qur’an, even though We had forbidden you to make any mention in His presence except with His permission. God is indeed a witness to this and fully informed. Consider, then, your condition and your knowledge! Shame on you, your reason, and your understanding, O assembly of those who have suffered the greatest loss! Did you not know that We rolled up what people possessed and spread out another carpet? Blessed, then, is God, the King, the One who spreads it out, the Almighty, the Generous.

692 Say: My people, do not fabricate lies about Me. I spoke only in remembrance and praise of this Revelation; I breathed only in love for Him; and I turned only toward His radiant, luminous countenance. I made the Bayán and all that was revealed in it a leaf from the leaves of the garden of Paradise for His Self, the All-Protecting, the Almighty, the All-Powerful. Beware lest you seize it and turn it over to the one who wished to shed My blood once again, because he followed self and desire and was among those who waged war.

693 We unfolded the Bayán from a word, then returned it to that word, and commanded the word to appear before the Throne, that it might witness creation before Him and that His all-knowing, all-wise Self might rejoice in it. Judge fairly, then: should its possessor exercise authority over it, or someone other than it? What is the matter with you, O company of the veiled? We commanded the people of the Bayán to wear silk and to cleanse themselves and their clothes, lest His eyes fall upon anything He dislikes. Likewise, concerning every thing, We set out the details in a clear Book. All this was for His sake, if you are among those who judge fairly.

694 We created the heavens and the earth and all that was ordained between them for His loved ones—how much more, then, for His radiant, mighty, luminous Beauty! Yet you clung to what We ordained for Him and used it to object to My Beloved. What is the matter with you, O assembly of hatred? What will avail you today, O company of those who spread corruption? You objected to Him and to everything that appeared from Him, even though We had enjoined you in the Tablets that whoever calls to mind His most great and wondrous Name should rise from his seat and say, “Glory be to God, the Possessor of sovereignty and dominion,” nineteen times, and then, “Glory be to God, the Possessor of might and supreme power,” nineteen times, and so on to the end of what We revealed in a Tablet of great glory.

695 You disbelieved in Him and His verses, and were not content with that. You did not regard the rights of God in relation to Him, nor did you observe God’s command concerning His exalted, all-knowing Self, until you objected to all His actions, one after another, and became mockers. Some of you said, “He drinks tea”; some said, “He eats food”; and some objected to His clothing, even though every thread of it bears witness that there is no God but Him and that He is indeed the Desire of those brought near.

696 I Myself bear witness that at times He did not possess two garments so that He could change from one into the other. Thus bears witness a truthful, knowing tongue. On some nights the family of God had nothing to sustain them. Yet He concealed His circumstances to safeguard the firm and steadfast Cause of God, even though all things were created for His sake and He holds the key to the treasuries of the heavens and the earth. Shame on your modesty, O people of the Bayán! By God! I am ashamed of your conduct, and I therefore disown you, O assembly of devils! Alas, alas for His affliction among you! Alas, alas for what has befallen Him and befalls Him at every moment!

697 O people, be fair, then reflect. At the very least, had you remained in those veils, I would not have manifested Myself. What is the fruit of My appearance, O assembly of hypocrites? God sent Me to tear away the veils and purify you for this Revelation, yet you have done to Him what makes My eyes and the eyes of the sanctified shed tears. Your conduct has vindicated the peoples of the past, for you are more veiled than they and more heedless than the people of the Torah, the Psalms, and the Gospel. If only My mother had not given birth to Me, and I had not manifested Myself among you, O assembly of the disappointed! By Him who sent Me with the truth! I have encompassed the knowledge of all things, everything treasured in the repositories of God’s keeping, and whatever is concealed from the most discerning of the world’s people. Yet I have not counted any souls more wretched than you or more distant from you. For after all We set forth in detail in the Tablets, and all the counsel We gave you in every page, We did not think that anyone would appear in the world to object to God, in whose grasp is the dominion of the kingdom of the heavens and the earth. Thus We were bewildered at your creation, and I do not know by what word you were created, O you at whom, and at whose conduct, the hearts of the inhabitants of the exalted Assembly, and then the hearts of the sincere and those brought near, are bewildered! Thus, O servant, We have recounted to you in this Tablet what the Dove of Utterance sang at that time before the throne of your Lord, the Almighty, the All-Praised. Read what has been revealed in it, then guard the pearls of meaning from every treacherous thief among the assembly of devils.

698 If you find someone with insight, spread them before his eyes, so that he may witness and be among those who attain their goal. Perhaps those endowed with insight among Our righteous servants will learn what has befallen the Beauty of the Chosen One at the hands of these wicked people, who have taken the calf as their lord instead of God, prostrate themselves before it evening and morning, and rejoice. Do not grieve over what has befallen Us, but be patient as We have been patient. He is indeed the best helper and supporter. Remember your Lord by night and day, then voice His praise among His servants, so that through His praise the fire of His love may arise in the hearts of those who do good, and all may arise to praise God, their Lord and the Lord of all that is seen and unseen, and the Lord of your forefathers. Praise be to God, the Lord of the worlds.

699 Anyone endowed with sight and understanding will surely, on reading these verses, lament both for God and for himself: for the exalted God, because He has been afflicted at the hands of such oppressors in a manner that the eye of creation has never seen and the ear of existence has never heard; and for himself, because he has not arisen to serve this wondrous Cause as he should. We ask God to raise up, through His sovereignty, a people who will make good what these people have failed to do, so that those sanctified souls may arise to do what is fitting and worthy of this Revelation. The one who circles around the Spirit says, “Do not conceal it! Tear away the veils, then reveal what has been secretly treasured of the knowledge of God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting.” And those who circle around the intellect say, “Beware, beware! Be patient, and do not divulge what would terrify the embodiments of malice and tear apart the breasts of those who associate partners with God. Impart to the servants what befits their condition and station. Beware of imparting the stations of maturity to a suckling infant, or the mysteries of God’s sanctuary to one who is deprived, or the melodies of God, the Almighty, the Beloved, to the deaf.”

700 O unjust and undiscerning one! Why did you not ask the tree of aversion why he has now issued a decree for the death of the One by whose name you were known and designated for twenty years, and by whose words they established their own standing? He has done what no one has ever done, and at every moment he is occupied with a new scheme to undermine His Cause, even though you and all those like you were created by His word. But what can be said? Whatever is said has never had, and will never have, any effect on malicious souls and those who associate partners with God.

701 It is abundantly clear that if a soul had attained even a spark of the fire of divine love, he would never have occupied himself with these words or, through these objections, inflicted on the Sovereign of names and attributes what has no precedent in creation. God will assuredly raise up a people who will be awakened by His words, recognize His intent, turn toward Him, and detach themselves from all the worlds.

702 As for what you wrote, “What answer should I give Mírzá Muḥammad ʿAlí, Mírzá Abu’l-Ḥasan, and the others?”—you have never been, and are not, the judge of all creation. Be the judge of your own self, and consider what answer you will give God. Answering His creatures is easy. O shameless and immodest one! You have expressed bewilderment about answering a few created beings, yet have never given a thought to answering God. Now be fair: you have expressed shame before a few created beings and bewilderment about how to answer them; what answer are we servants to give at the seat of the Most Great Throne, where all the Prophets and those brought near are present? Behold yourself at that gathering, and then the entire assembly of the Bayán, all together, while the Primal Point, at the right hand of the throne, addresses you and them in Persian.

703 It is recounted so that you may understand correctly. He says, “O assembly of the Bayán! Did I not inform you in all the Tablets that after Me a Revelation would appear?” You will all surely say, “Yes, by Your own true Self! In all the Tablets You gave these glad tidings and received from everyone a covenant concerning that Most Great Luminary.” He then says, “Did I not say that that Ancient Beauty would appear with the verses of God? Did I not say that the people of Sinai would be struck down and die in that presence? Did I not say that the highest tree of affirmation would become one of the lowest trees of negation? Did I not say that every luminous being would be seen as dark before the radiance of the lights of His face? Did I not say that no one but His own Self knows the Revelation itself? Did I not say that, at the time of His appearance, you should not let what has been revealed in the Bayán veil you from Him? Did I not tell the Letters of the Bayán, who are the first of the creation of the Bayán, not to remain deprived of that Ancient Beauty? Did I not say that the whole Bayán is but a leaf among the leaves of paradise before that Tree of Oneness? Did I not say that He is able to reject a soul who is the most exalted of all creation? Did I not say that all the people of the Bayán are His creation in His previous Revelation?”

704 “You will surely acknowledge all of these, for they are all explicitly revealed and recorded in the Tablets of the Bayán. Despite these countless counsels and firm, conclusive admonitions, by what evidence or proof have you drawn the sword of oppression against this blessed Beauty, all arisen to violate His sanctity, sought to shed His pure blood, and attacked Him from every side with the swords of hypocrisy? Yet He has appeared with the very proof with which I appeared before, and with which Muḥammad, the Messenger of God, appeared before that, and the Spirit before that. If you ask, ‘How do we know that these verses are from God?’ I say: by the same evidence through which you recognized that My verses are from God. Moreover, you all acknowledge that these wondrous verses have spread throughout every region, and that at every moment He has uttered verses before a single word of which all have been, and will be, as nothing. Despite this, you have taken as your lord someone who is at war with God. Some of you cling to the fact that I called him a fruit, although it is revealed in My Book that if a branch, a leaf, or a fruit is seen upon Me that does not prostrate itself before that Sun of Revelation on the day of His appearance, it is not of Me and does not return to Me.”

705 “Some cling to the fact that I designated him a mirror, although, according to My explicit text, a hundred thousand like him appear and shine forth through a single effulgence of the Sun. Yet you have not even understood the matter of that mirror. Even supposing that he is that mirror, there is no doubt that you have recognized him as a mirror because of My words. And I explicitly stated that you should not allow what has been revealed in the Book to veil you from that Manifestation of the Lord of Lords. I also stated that the people of Sinai would become dead and as nothing before that Most Great Revelation. A hundred thousand like those mirrors have been, and will be, sheer nothingness before a single one of those Sinaitic souls shining from the horizon of oneness.”

706 “If you say that this mirror has now made the same claim, there is no doubt that he has lied in saying this. For I specifically revealed that if a mirror should claim to be the sun, it is clear to the Sun that it is only an image of Him. From this very statement of Mine, it is established that the mirror, as you regard him, would claim to be the sun. His very claim in these days is proof of his falsehood, for after the dawning radiance of the Sun of Oneness appeared, he uttered these words in opposition to His Self, in rebellion against His Being, in arrogance before His Essence, and in impiety toward His Cause. And most of you have followed him.”

707 “Shame on you, O people of the Bayān! Again, shame on you, O people of the Bayān! Again, shame on you, O people of the Bayān! Again, shame on you, without end! For because of your deeds My heart has melted, My liver has burned, My entrails have become entangled, My limbs have been torn apart, My frame has been shaken, My skin has shuddered, My soul has trembled, My being has been driven mad, My inmost self has cried out, and My outward and inward being, My beginning and My end, have been overcome with sorrow.”

708 “What you have mentioned and continue to mention, you attribute to Me, and it is on the strength of My words that you have clung to him. The authority of My own Self is established through the Bayān, and I have specifically stated that you must not let the Bayān veil you from the Person of the All-Merciful. I do not know, after this, what excuse you will cling to or what cord you will grasp. You pay no heed to My command and have not accepted God’s counsel. You have designated a soul who is now at war with God by one of the names, and you wage war and dispute with My own Self, even though I have explicitly stated that the manifestations of all the names are servants in their duties in that Revelation of sanctified glory, as I have stated:”

709 “If I say that He is One, that is one of His names; and if I say that He is All-Glorious, that name appears as one of His names; and if I say that He is Most Holy, He is the One who reveals Himself through that name to those like Him. If I say that He is Mighty, every mighty one prostrates before the loftiness of His might; and if I say that He is Beloved, every beloved one is consumed with love for the recognition of Him and the majesty of His might. If I say that He is All-Protecting, He is the One who, by His command, causes God to make whoever adduces proof on the day of His appearance a protector over all things. If I say that He is Self-Subsisting, that is the manifestation of one of those who stand upon the expanse of His oneness. Exalted, exalted be His remembrance!”

710 “And exalted, exalted be His station!” Its meaning in Persian is this: “If I say that He is One, this One is one of His names. If I say that He is All-Glorious, the locus of the manifestation of this name is one of His names. If I say that He is Most Holy, He is that very Person who has revealed and continues to reveal Himself through this name to those like Him. If I say that He is Mighty, every mighty one prostrates before the loftiness of His might. If I say that He is Beloved, every beloved one has torn the veil of his own heart in affection and love for Him. If I say that He is All-Protecting, He is that Sovereign by whose command God assuredly makes every soul who adduces His proof on the day of His appearance a protector over all things. If I say that He is Self-Subsisting, that manifestation is one of those who stand upon the expanse of His oneness.” “Although I thus explicitly described all the most beautiful names as servants at His threshold, and that desired Essence and promised Countenance as sanctified, exalted, and free from them all, you have nevertheless taken a name from My Book, clung to that name, and inflicted what no religious community has ever inflicted before.”

711 Thus has spoken the Tongue of God, the King, the All-Powerful, the Most High, the Most Great. These are the words of the Primal Point—may the soul of all else be sacrificed for Him. Is any soul capable of answering them? No, by God! Rather, we are all abashed, ashamed, and embarrassed, for those souls who have turned toward Him have not fulfilled the conditions of turning toward Him as they truly require, while it is evident what the souls who turn away have perpetrated and continue to perpetrate. Yet, according to the explicit text of the Primal Point, all the most beautiful names, from the All-Protecting and the Self-Subsisting to those above and below them, are all souls who establish the truth of this wondrous Revelation by the proof of this Revelation itself. Consider, then, the station of those souls who, through speech and writing, have adduced proofs establishing this Most Great Revelation and have written what has been cast upon those detached hearts. By God, they are among the most exalted and most glorious of creation, such that no one before them has surpassed them, nor will any soul among those to come surpass them.

712 O commanding self, why did you occupy yourself with mentioning those souls and express bewilderment at answering, yet take no heed of the deeds of souls who gave their lives in the path of this Cause? The deed of each was a clear proof and a shining testimony, between the heavens and the earths, establishing this most wondrous, most unassailable Revelation. Among them was the Beloved of Martyrs, Āqā Siyyid Ismāʿīl of Zavārih, who sacrificed his life by his own hand out of love for this Revelation. No soul denied his asceticism, piety, devoutness, learning, or knowledge of God. You were not guided by the deed of such a soul, but sent before the Throne an account of souls whose present path is unknown. May God strengthen them in His Cause, detach them from you and those like you, and make them among the sincere.

713 It has therefore become necessary to recount here the story of the martyred Siyyid—upon him be the glory of God, the might of God, the light of God, the grandeur of God, the mystery of God, the greatness of God, the radiance of God, the praise of God, the praise of all things, and the praise of the worlds—so that perhaps you may learn of those souls of whom the Point of the Bayān—glorious is His power—foretold that, before the appearance of that Sun of Oneness, they would be set ablaze with the fire of His love. Thus, addressing the subsequent Manifestation, He says, in words whose substance in Persian is as follows—His words, exalted be His honor: “Although the servants have all been, and will remain, veiled from You, yet servants will also be created for You who, before Your appearance, will be set ablaze with the fire of Your love.” Thus the aforementioned Siyyid was set ablaze before the Revelation, and others like that soul will assuredly appear, even though you and those like you deny or disbelieve. The story of that martyr is this: he arrived in Iraq and took up residence near the Most Great House, in the house of Āqā Muḥammad Riḍā—upon him be the glory of God. The aforementioned person requested that the Most Glorious One visit his house at some time. Several days passed, and He deferred His reply until one day He accepted and went to that dwelling. The aforementioned Siyyid arranged a gathering according to his means and brought into His presence several trays of citrus fruits, other fruit, and sweets. Then the Face of God turned toward the face of him who was martyred in His path and addressed him: “Come and eat what has been set before you of God’s favors and bounties.”

714 He spoke before God with humility, reverence, cries, and supplication, saying, “I desire, from Your wondrous gifts, that You grant me a share of Your spiritual bounties.” Then the face of the Spirit blazed, and He said, “Come, O servant,” and bade him sit before His face. Then the Tongue of God spoke words from which drops of meaning rained upon all that has been and will be. I could neither describe nor recount them, nor do I know what hidden spiritual bounties the hand of providence bestowed upon him, such that his soul, his spirit, his being, and his essence were enraptured by them. The overpowering force of longing seized him so completely that he became oblivious to himself and to all who are in the heavens and the earths. Inwardly and outwardly he turned toward the Beloved of the worlds, until the gathering ended and the Spirit returned to His dwelling.

715 But after he had heard the words of God and tasted what he had desired, no one saw any calm or repose in him. A few days passed, and at every moment his longing increased and his ardent love for God, his Maker, grew more intense, until one day he came at dawn and swept the courtyard of the House with his turban. He returned, took a knife, withdrew from the people, and left the city for a watering place on the riverbank. He stood facing the House, grasped his beard with one hand, and with the other cut his throat out of love for God, the All-Powerful, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting. Then an uproar arose among the people, cries went up from every soul, and a great crowd gathered around him. They saw that the knife was in his hand and that he had placed his hand upon his breast. All existence was astonished at this, as were the people of the Concourse on High, the inhabitants of the cities of grandeur, and the inhabitants of the kingdom of names and the dominion of eternity. They all offered prayers for him, proclaimed God’s greatness before his face, descended upon him, circled around him, and inhaled the fragrances of his love. Were I to recount what appeared on that day, you would neither accept it nor be able to comprehend it. God is witness to what I say.

716 It is recounted in Persian that, after the arrival of the Most Glorious One, the aforementioned Siyyid asked for spiritual food. He said, “Come and sit before Me.” After he had seated himself, He spoke certain words. By God, the intoxication of the wine of meanings in the divine words so enraptured him that he became oblivious to himself and to both worlds. If both worlds sought to recount the details of that gathering or to describe that Kawthar of meanings which flowed from the ocean of the utterance of the eternal Countenance, they would assuredly find themselves powerless. The words of God so affected the heart of him who had turned toward God that his entire frame was set ablaze with the fire of divine love. God alone knows what was bestowed upon him. He tasted what no one has known except God, the All-Knowing, the All-Aware.

717 Afterward, the gathering ended and the Most Glorious Beauty departed. But that Siyyid manifested a wondrous state at every moment, and the love of God so seized him that he eventually ceased eating and drinking. He remained in this condition for a time, until one day he composed verses in praise of God and gave them to the companions. They still exist, and anyone who reads them will become aware of the fire that consumed the very being of that embodiment of holiness. Then, one day, he arose at the first light of dawn and turned toward the Most Great House. With his turban he swept the most pure courtyard of the House, and afterward went and took a blade. He also went to the house of Āqā Muḥammad Riḍā and paid a visit, then hastened to the place of sacrifice for the Beloved, detached from all who are in the heavens and the earths. At that moment, all the exalted angels were gathered around him, their attention fixed upon him, and they went with him toward the place of sacrifice. Outside the city, near the river, facing the House, he cut his blessed throat with his own hand, out of longing for the love of his Lord, passionate devotion to Him, and a desire for reunion with Him, content with His decree and turning toward the sanctuary of reunion with Him.

718 Afterward, news of this event spread through the city until everyone had heard it. The chief of the musketeers, with a group of Arabs and Persians, went to that most pure spot and saw that sacred figure lying there, the blade in his hand, having sacrificed his life in this manner. All who saw him were astonished, and this very deed caused several of his enemies to repent of their actions and attain the love of God. In no age had such a thing occurred. Now consider that the name of this blessed soul was also Ishmael, but this Ishmael did not return alive from the place of sacrifice for the Beloved: he gave his life in His path. By God, this deed is a proof to all who are in the heavens and on earth, but you do not comprehend it, nor do those like you. What have you to do with these realms? Go and speak of a fabricated successorship; investigate that matter and write a treatise.

719 Likewise, there are other souls who have given their lives in this Revelation and continued to speak in remembrance of the Beloved until they reached the place of sacrifice. Out of hatred for God, you have made no mention of these souls, who shine resplendent among the people like rising suns, and you write, “What answer should I give to this person and that?” Those who have disbelieved in the verses of God and followed you and your like have indeed suffered loss in this world and the next. The Fire is their dwelling place: how wretched is the dwelling of the deniers!

720 As for what you have written, “What answer should I give, when these people attach themselves to someone new every day, regard someone as true, and then declare him false?”—this one whom you have called true has always been a created being. He was honored with one of the garments of the names, and after his rebellion, pride, and turning away, that garment was taken from him. Rein in your pen, for because of what has flowed from it, the inhabitants of the realms of eternity curse you. To this bears witness the truthful, speaking, trustworthy Tongue of God. He says that, in another cycle, the people of Sinai become dead and as nothing at a sprinkling from that all-protecting crimson Light. Yet you now ask how a mirror, as you suppose him to be, can become false, or a fruit can fall. It is expressly stated that many a tree of affirmation, through turning away in the next Revelation, becomes a tree of negation. Yet now you have attached yourself to and held fast to what you imagine to be a fruit. Even if it were a true fruit, by turning away it would fall from the divine Lote-Tree and return to its original abode in the Fire.

721 Here an utterance of the Point of the Bayān, the Countenance of the All-Merciful and the Person of the All-Glorious—may the souls of all who dwell in the realms of existence and contingency be sacrificed for Him—is cited, so that perhaps you may feel shame and not stretch out a hand of aggression toward the pavilion of divine knowledge and recognition. Although you and your like are numbered among the deprived in God’s sight, the utterance has been, and will remain, addressed to those who are near to Him. He said, and His word is truth: “Make, O God, that Tree wholly hers, so that the fruits of what God has created within it may appear within it for Him through whom God has willed to manifest what He has willed. For I, by Your might, have not desired that there should be upon that Tree any branch, leaf, or fruit that will not bow down before Him on the day of His appearance, or glorify You through Him as befits the loftiest heights of His manifestation and the most exalted sublimity of His concealment. And if, O my God, You should behold upon Me any branch, leaf, or fruit that does not bow down before Him on the day of His appearance, cut it off, O God, from that Tree, for it is not of Me and does not return to Me.” End of quotation.

722 May my self be sacrificed for Him, and my soul be sacrificed for Him, and my inmost secret be sacrificed for Him, and my outward being be sacrificed for Him, and my inward being be sacrificed for Him; may the earthly kingdom and the heavenly kingdom be sacrificed for Him, and power and the divine realm be sacrificed for Him, and glory and ruby be sacrificed for Him, and manifestation and the human realm be sacrificed for Him, and all that has been created in the realms of eternity be sacrificed for Him. For He—may my soul be sacrificed for Him—moved His pen only in celebration of this wondrous Remembrance, this supremely exalted and unassailable Revelation, this transcendent and lofty height, this radiant, shining Sun, and this manifest, resplendent, mighty, inviolable Cause. Reflect a little on what that Point of Existence and Beauty of the Adored One says. He says, “O my Lord, I have not desired that there should remain upon this Tree any branch, leaf, or fruit that does not bow down before Him on the day of His appearance, and does not glorify You through that Manifestation of revelation as befits the loftiest heights of His manifestation and the most exalted sublimity of His concealment.” And He says, “If, O my Lord, You should behold upon Me any branch, leaf, or fruit that does not bow down before Him on the day of His appearance, then cut it off, O Lord, from this Tree. Truly, it has not been of Me and does not return to Me.”

723 Reflect a little. The fruit of the Tree of Truth in the former Revelation is that all the people of the Bayān should dwell beneath the shade of this Tree. This was the essential purpose of all that was ordained in the former Tree. Now, whoever has attained that station is numbered among its fruits, twigs, branches, and whatever has been ordained for it; anyone else is fallen and cut off. Now lament a little for the fruit that has taken hold of something other than God. Even if it is a true fruit, the Point of the Bayān expressly says, “If it does not acknowledge the True One on the day of His appearance, it has not been, and will not be, of Me.” Despite this clear, evident, manifest utterance, you wonder how a fruit can become false or fall!

724 Now behold the greatness of the Cause of God and the standing of yourself and your guide. Say: O child in the school of ignorance, you have not yet reached maturity, for had you matured, you would not have occupied yourself with these words. Shame on you, on what you possess, on what you have adduced as proof, and on what delights you today! The mature are those who have recognized the Manifestation of the Lord of the worlds and are established upon the thrones of steadfastness and dignity. For you and your like, tearing through these veils is exceedingly difficult. Yet God—exalted be His praise—has souls who, through His breaths, tear through every veil. These are the souls each one of whom holds sway over all the people of the Bayān, as do their words, their deeds, and their recognition. Thus the Point of the Bayān says that the year-old embryo of the next Revelation is mightier than all who are in the Bayān. By God, besides whom there is no god, if they reflected upon this very utterance of the Point of the Bayān, they would all detach themselves from everyone in the heavens and on earth, and from their own souls, selves, and beings, and turn toward the One.

725 This utterance is exceedingly momentous, if you would reflect. For if the Cause were not mighty, how could such an utterance have appeared from the Revealer of the Bayān? Thus We testify that God, the Most High, the Most Great, has spoken the truth. For unless one is mightier than all creation, one cannot tear through these veils of vain imaginings or shatter these mighty idols through the power of the Friend of all the worlds. How far apart are the station of these servants and that of the servants of former times! These are servants upon whom the inhabitants of the Concourse on High, and then the realities of all things, invoke blessings, but you do not perceive.

726 As for what you have written with the pen of malice and slander, that some people accuse the Most Glorious Beauty of disobedience, and your having written what the pen is ashamed to mention: everything you have said is an outright lie. That lying prompter has prompted you with all these words and written them. Whoever spoke thus has fabricated a lie against God, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting. Now the angels of holiness address you from the Most Great Paradise, saying, “O you who associate partners with God, silence your tongue! From a drop of the oceans of His holiness we were created and raised up, and God has made us pure of the guidance of anyone other than Him and sanctified us from the mention of all besides Him. Thus we assuredly glorify Him and proclaim His holiness in this most great and mighty abode.”

727 Then the angels of transcendence address you from the Most Exalted Paradise, saying, “Take up your pen, O you who associate partners with God! By God, through a breath from the winds of His transcendence we were raised up and created. Thus we proclaim His transcendence, glorify Him, and declare His holiness in this station, which God has purified of your mention, your praise, your recognition, and the recognition of all the worlds.” Then the angels of detachment address you in the All-Glorious Paradise, saying, “Be ashamed, O you who turn away from God! God has created us from the oceans of His detachment, and we have severed ourselves from every mention but His. Thus we walk in this Most Great Paradise, remembering and praising Him every morning and evening.”

728 Then the inhabitants of the Paradise of the Command address you, saying, “O you who are heedless of the remembrance of God and wage war against His own Self! We were created by a command that flowed from His mighty, wondrous Pen. He is indeed the One at whose command the Most Exalted Pen inscribed on the Tablets whatever He desired. He is indeed the Ruler over whatever He wills. He is not questioned about what He does, while all besides Him are questioned, if you are among those who know. He indeed chooses freely in His own right, doing whatever He wishes through His sovereignty. No one has the right to object to Him; any objection only turns back upon your own wretched, wayward self. We have counted none among the religious communities of the past more veiled than you. By God, the fire of your malice has kindled the fire of hell! Fear God, and do not attribute to Him what your own self and desires dictate to you, and do not be among those who associate partners with Him. By God, every atom bears witness to His holiness, all things to His transcendence, the inhabitants of the Concourse on High to His detachment, and the inhabitants of the cities of eternity to the oneness of His Essence. If He were to pronounce belief in His oneness upon unbelief itself, He would have the power through His all-compelling, all-powerful sovereignty. If He wished to transform the sins of all contingent beings with a word from Himself, He would assuredly have the power through His command. Powerlessness belongs only to you and those like you, O you who have deprived yourself of the breaths of God in these most wondrous days.”

729 Shame on you and your understanding! Have you not recognized that judgment proceeds from Him, while no one’s judgment applies to Him? He is indeed the Command from all eternity, and no one’s command holds sway over Him, for all are destitute before Him and were created by His mighty, wondrous word. Is it fitting for a leaf that has fallen from the Lote Tree and yellowed through the air of its own self to object to a Tree whose every leaf proclaims that there is no God but Him, the Almighty, the Most Exalted, the All-Knowing, the All-Wise? Is it fitting for nothingness to object to the Sovereign of eternity, or to prescribe its own laws for Him? No, by His own exalted, mighty Self! Listen, then, to My words, and seek forgiveness throughout your life, that God may perhaps forgive you, purify you of what has afflicted you, detach you from your self and desires, and bring you near to what is better for you than sovereignty over the heavens and the earth. By God, in what you have said you intended only to bring the Cause of God to ruin. We ask God to bring you to ruin, and those who supported you in what you have done and committed. He indeed has power over all things.

730 You have left this servant no choice: if no answer is given, there is a fear that some weak souls may be deprived of the True One through these fabricated words. Yet if an answer is given—by God, besides whom there is no other god—one’s innate nature is unwilling to become heedless of the remembrance of God and turn its attention to the words of those who associate partners with Him. But what can be done? The duty is to answer what you have said, so that those fabrications may not become a veil between His servants and the Sovereign of the Day of Promise.

731 In prompting you with these words, he had no purpose but to keep some souls from turning toward Paradise and to catch His servants in the snare of words. Everyone knows how strenuously He exerted Himself during His twelve years’ residence in Iraq, until He eliminated certain shameful practices from among this community. And if some did commit them—God forbid—it was in secret. By God, O heedless one, you do not know against what station you have raised these objections! If you knew, you would perish that very instant.

732 The chief reason and greatest cause of the All-Glorious Beauty’s displeasure with Mīrzā Yaḥyā—by God, besides whom there is no other god—was that he took possession of the wife of the Primal Point, may the souls of all besides Him be sacrificed for Him, although this is forbidden in all the heavenly Books. His shamelessness reached such a point that, while he specifically forbade marriage to his own wives in his writings, he nevertheless stretched out the hand of transgression and betrayal toward the wife of the Manifestation of the all-knowing King. Shame on him and his faithfulness! If only he had confined himself to his own act; instead, after committing it himself, he made her available to those who associate partners with God. All the people of the Bayān have heard and know of this. His associates should be asked about his misdeeds.

733 Beyond all this, what is astonishing is that the very passage he has written is clear evidence and conclusive proof of the truth of this Cause, yet you have still not perceived it. You yourself know that this Person was not one of the learned or the ascetics, nor counted among the mystics and the turbaned clergy, and that, according to you, He associated with the likes of Moshīr al-Dowleh. Then, all at once, the heavenly stream of divine meanings flows from Him, without dependence on any source, and the suns of divine wisdom rise and shine from the horizon of His will. O unfair one! This is a station above every station, if you are among the fair-minded. You have belittled this greatest favor and, as you imagine, spoken these words to lead people astray. You have erred, O sinful transgressor! Every discerning person of reason who hears them has certainly become, and will become, firmer and more steadfast in the Cause of God.

734 In any case, devise another plan for yourself, for these insinuations affect souls given to whispering evil, not souls that belong to the All-Merciful. Clouded, constrained hearts may become heedless of the remembrance of the Sovereign of Oneness through these false, unworthy words, but detached souls turn wholeheartedly toward the All-Merciful. Now I ask you: if someone arises from among a people who have never been known for learning or renowned among others for excellence and justice, and gives voice to the gems of God’s words, as you have heard and continue to hear, is this person greater, or someone educated among the learned? There is no doubt that the former is superior, for He has appeared solely through divine power.

735 It is well that the True One—exalted be His glory—has spoken through your own tongue words that have been, and will be, sufficient for the discerning to recognize souls such as these. The True One has always been the object of such objections, just as they objected to the Spirit of God for sitting and associating with sinners and tax collectors. By God, despite the counsels of the Point of the Bayān—exalted be His honor—afflictions have befallen this Revelation that have befallen none of the Manifestations of the past. The afflictions of the first and the last do not equal this very defiled sheet that you have sent to the Most Holy Court, although you have had, and still have, no knowledge of any of these matters, and have spoken out of sheer desire and self alone. This is one of the calamities that have been witnessed, yet afflictions have befallen this Revelation that no one but God can count.

736 Thus, in the Tablet to Jināb-i-Jūd—upon him be the glory of God—a verse was revealed that, by God, has melted the hearts of the people of every land. He says—exalted be His majesty—“You have seen Me, associated with Me, and known the ocean of My calm and the mountain of My patience. Consider what has made Me raise My cry between the heavens and the earth.” But what use is there in conveying divine verses to you? It is like a nightingale singing to someone deaf. We ask God to cleanse the earth of your defilement and the defilement of those who were with you. He indeed answers the prayers of those who ask. Your worth has become clear to this extent: at the word of the vilest and most abject of people, you have turned away from the True One and clung to the tree of self and desire.

737 You had also written, “It is being said in gatherings that the All-Glorious Beauty has set about fomenting corruption and discord. It has become common talk that He first set up His brother as the Qāʾim and hid him away, baking bread for Himself in his name; now He has cast him out and wants to eat nuts.” End of quotation.

738 May God curse the one who prompted you, and may God curse the one who lied and fabricated, then acted unjustly and led others into transgression, then disbelieved and brought misery. By God, he has found people very foolish. Everyone knows that He came to Iraq at the command of the government of Iran, while Mīrzā Yaḥyā fled after the incident in the Land of Ṭā and came to Iraq. Say: O idolater! The One who expended His life in the path of God was in full view of the people, and at every moment the arrows of fate rained upon Him in numbers that no one but God, the King, the All-Knowing, the All-Informed, could count. Most of the people rose against Him during His days in Iraq. Every day some called for Him to be seized and sent to the Land of Ṭā; some said, “They will drown Him in the river”; and others said, “The government will exile Him.” God indeed witnessed and knew all this. The eyes of all things wept for Him because of the suffering and adversity that afflicted Him, yet you wrote what you wrote. Surely God’s curse rests upon the liars.

739 All things bear witness and testify that during the years of residence in Iraq there was never any hope of survival, and at every moment new afflictions arose. Yet the likes of those vile souls speak of bread and nuts. Consider how utterly bereft of ordinary reason you have become. By God, anyone who reads your words sees God’s wrath plainly and manifestly in you. Glory be to God! Someone unable to write two words of Persian in a graceful manner has objected to the One by whose will the Most Exalted Pen was created and from whom came the sound of its writing, through which the hearts of all that has been and will be were drawn. Shame on you and on what has proceeded from you!

740 Yet it would not be surprising if these heedless people preferred these very words of yours to the words of the True One, just as for endless years they have preferred the calf to the True One and worshipped it, and are still doing so. It is a great pity for a human being to utter such unworthy words, for words reflect the human soul. Thus the words of the people of the Most Great View are adorned with the ornament of courtesy. We ask God not to leave us stripped of this robe, through which human worth becomes manifest among all created beings, as does the distinction between humans and animals. Then we ask Him to draw us near to Him, detach us from all besides Him, purify us of the odors of delusion and imitation, and make us among those who said, “God is our Lord,” and then stood firm, undeterred by the mockery of all creatures or the gloating of the heedless.

741 O God, my God! Open the eyes of these people, that they may see You manifest among Your creatures and shining in Your kingdom. My God, had You opened their eyes, You would not have been afflicted by their mockery, the stones of their suspicions, and the arrows of their imaginings, just as You opened the eyes of Your loved ones and made known to them what no others knew. Had You lifted the veils for these people as You lifted them from the faces of Your loved ones, they would not have remained veiled or raised objections. But because You concealed Yourself from these and revealed Yourself to Your loved ones, the clamor of the heedless among Your creatures and the cries of the deluded among the people of Your kingdom have risen. I therefore ask You by Your own Self to lift for them the veils that have kept them from recognizing You and the Manifestation of Your Self, so that all may gather on the shore of the ocean of Your oneness and at the seat of the glory of Your holiness and singleness. You indeed have power over whatever You will.

742 Those who have stated that He made bread in his name—by God, I am ashamed to mention such vile, ill-mannered souls—and it is that wicked Siyyid Muḥammad himself. Such remarks have never been, nor will they ever be, the remarks of noble people. Anyone who has uttered such a statement is like someone saying that the sun has been illumined by darkness, that the nightingale has learned to sing from the crow, or that a ruby has acquired its radiance and color from a stone. But what use is it? That heedless one is unaware, and they have branded as liars all those who were present, lest a truthful word be uttered and the people hear it.

743 Siyyid Muḥammad, who, by God the Almighty, used to take pride in standing in the blessed presence, has now come forward and attributed such fabrications to the True One. Praise be to God that we servants have spoken nothing but truth and honesty, and never will speak otherwise. We have not exchanged faith for worldly gain, nor have we turned our eyes away from the True One. All give thanks that they have attained faith in God in this Revelation, while this servant says: “If only I had not been born of my mother! And if I had been born, had not been suckled; and if I had been suckled, my back had not been strengthened. If only I had been among those lost!” No soul possessing even less than a mote of the love of God has ever been, or ever will be, content to witness such things. To God we have committed the matter, and in Him we have placed our trust. We ask, from the wonders of His bounty, that He enable us to affirm His unity and acknowledge His singleness, and detach us from all besides Him. He indeed has power over all things. Then we ask Him, by the Name through which truth was distinguished from falsehood, to enable you to remember Him and recognize the Manifestation of His Self, and to purify you of the insinuations of these liars.

744 By God, besides whom there is no other god, afflictions have befallen us in this land that no one can count. He has repeatedly said: “All that I suffered in prison, in chains and fetters, and at the hands of the idolaters, who took Me from land to land and dragged Me, bareheaded and barefoot, from place to place—none of this counts as affliction. The greatest affliction, which has never had and will never have its like in creation, is for the True One to fall into the hands of an oppressor known for justice. For that unjust man, relying on the fools of the earth, has neither fear nor concern, and does whatever self and desire command him.” As can be seen, the person whom I nurtured and protected so much afterward, out of arrogance toward God, aversion to Him, and opposition to Him, inflicted what no oppressor had ever inflicted. The cause of this oppression was his confidence in the heedless servants, for had he been certain that the people possessed discernment and would not endorse his deeds, he would not have committed such acts.

745 It has always been the case with most servants that the more favor they have received, the more they have risen in ingratitude. The story of Satan was mentioned earlier: he was known by a name in each of the heavens, was described in the most perfect terms in the kingdom on high, and for centuries stood, bowed, sat, and prostrated himself in worship. Yet afterward he came to sit in ambush, keeping the servants from turning toward the All-Merciful. If only the idolatrous souls in these days had contented themselves with that much! No, by Him in whose hand is my soul, the thirst of their hatred will never be quenched except by drinking my blood. Satan himself has sought refuge before the All-Merciful from them and their deeds, but the people neither understand nor perceive.

746 It is not clear what was meant by the nuts that flowed from your ill-omened pen. Perishable things and worldly vanities were never at issue. Apparently, the reference was to the sums of money that the Sublime Porte sent in each city specifically for the attendants. If that is what was meant, these have now passed to the one who associates partners with God, and they have attained their beloved. Shame on them, their sense of shame, and their fidelity! Having cut themselves off from God’s door, they have turned to every door, sought refuge there, and complained of the Ancient Beauty until they attained their object. What a fine truth you have found! Cling firmly to it, that perhaps, by following those souls, you may enter the lowest depths of the fires.

747 O heedless one! As your own impious self knows, those vile souls have abased themselves to such a degree solely for worldly vanities, and have dishonored the Cause. Yet you have regarded such souls as true and turned away from the True One, who has never paid heed to anyone—indeed, even His attendants have paid them no heed. May God curse the one who witnessed God’s proof and denied it, and recognized His transcendence above all besides Him yet turned away from Him.

748 O people of the Bayān! The objections that this rejected one wrote at the prompting of Mīrzā Yaḥyā and Siyyid Muḥammad have been specifically mentioned so that you may know how much harm has been inflicted and understand the Primal Point’s lamentation and mourning over the later Revelation. Since that blessed Beauty knew what the idolatrous souls would do, He admonished everyone throughout the Bayān with countless counsels, emphasizing them to a degree beyond which nothing is possible. Thus it was revealed by the Most Exalted Pen that all should sanctify and purify themselves with the utmost holiness and purity, and likewise their clothing and household furnishings, lest at the time of the Revelation God’s gaze fall upon anything displeasing and thereby cause even that much sorrow to His most pure heart. In light of these counsels, consider the condition of the people of the Bayān: they have deliberately written treatises of refutation and sent them to the seat of the throne. This statement suffices for those possessed of understanding, honor, and fidelity.

749 By God, what has befallen Us has befallen none of the Manifestations of the past. Yet despite all the afflictions, calamities, and ordeals that have befallen Us, We remain thankful and patient, with no sorrow whatsoever. But Our heart burns over the dishonoring of the Cause of God. By the True One, everyone must lament and mourn for the divine Cause, for they have so dishonored the Cause of God that they have called a depraved woman of the earth “the best of women.” Consider how the True One has exposed the falsity of these people. By God, through this act alone the proof against all has been made complete, for most people know that depraved woman and are acquainted with her circumstances. And through that “best of women,” recognize the “best of men”—namely, the person who has described her as the foremost of women. By God, every atom has cursed him, yet he remains unaware within himself and is among those who rejoice. His shamelessness has reached such a point that he has called his own wives “the best of women,” and has taken possession of the wife of the Primal Point and given her away. Woe to them, then, for the punishment of a tremendous day!

750 At this point these wondrous, most sweet verses were revealed from the tongue of God, the Most High, the All-Glorious: “This is what has now descended from the dominion of lofty glory. O people, know that We have chosen the mother of the Primal Point, and she has been recorded before the Throne as among the choicest of handmaidens. Applying this name to anyone other than her has been forbidden. Thus has it been inscribed by the Most Exalted Pen in the Tablet of Decree, preserved within the treasuries of your Lord’s unfailing protection. She is indeed the best of women, and after her this designation applies to the wife of the Point who has not left the stronghold of purity and whom the hands of the treacherous have not touched. Thus has the matter been decreed. As for the one who acted treacherously, she has departed from the Point and severed her relationship with God, who has ordained all things in a clear Tablet. Say: The idolaters have imagined that We intended to abrogate what was revealed to the Point of the Bayān.”

751 Say: By my Lord, the All-Merciful, if We wished to do as they have imagined, no one would have the right to object to God, who created all things through a command from Himself. He is indeed powerful over all things. Whatever We wish is what God has wished, and whatever appears from Me is what has appeared from Him. Every knowledgeable believer in His oneness bears witness to this. But through this Revelation God has intended to confirm what was revealed by the Point of the Bayān and to place His laws upon the necks of the Pharaohs among the hosts of tyranny. His own Self, the True One, bears witness to this. We have intended to sacrifice Ourself in His path, just as He sacrificed Himself in My path. Fear God, O people, and do not fabricate lies against Him through whom the Sun of the Bayān has shone and His decree has appeared among all created beings. We will assuredly establish His laws and demonstrate the truth of His writings on earth with power and manifest authority.

752 Today the best of women is the mother of the Primal Point, and applying this name to the handmaidens of God has been forbidden. Permission has likewise been given to apply this name after her to the wife of the Point who resides in Fārs, who has not left the stronghold of divine purity, who has observed the sanctity of God, and whose robe of purity the hands of the treacherous have not reached. By My own Self, the True One, she is the chaste Virgin, the Most Exalted Consort, and the Most Great Purity. Serve her, O My loved ones, with manifest sincerity, for she is what remains of God among you, if you are among those who understand. We ask God to keep her steadfast in her present state and to protect her from the touch of those who violate sanctity, whom the winds of self and desire move as they please. They are indeed in manifest error. And We ask Him to establish her firmly in this Cause. He is powerful over all things.

753 O people, in what I say I have not intended My own self, but the Self of God, if you judge fairly. We convey to you what your Lord, the Most High, the Almighty, has willed. Respect for the family of God is incumbent upon all; showing them consideration in every circumstance has been deemed desirable, as has showing consideration to those from whose midst the Person of the All-Merciful has appeared. Honor and revere them, O people, and do not be among the heedless. All this bounty holds true so long as the bond of relationship is not severed.

754 You know, O my God, that I love these souls and all that is related to Your own Self. From them I perceive the fragrances of the shirt of Your all-mercifulness and the sweet scents of the robe of Your glory and Your gifts, and I long to meet them, to gather with them, and to enjoy their companionship. I therefore ask You, O my God, by the Name that You have made sovereign over all names between earth and heaven, through which You have shattered the idols of self and desire, and through which You have washed those who know You and long for You, purifying them from the scents of all besides You in the kingdom of creation, to kindle the torches of Your love in the hearts of these souls, so that through them the inhabitants of Your realm may be guided—those who have neglected Your remembrance and praise and have strayed far from the shore of nearness to You and meeting with You.

755 O my God, do not keep them from what has appeared among them, and do not deprive them of what You have showered in these days from the clouds of Your mercy and Your grace. Let them hear, O my God, at every moment, Your melodies and songs that have risen from the seat of the throne of Your oneness and the seat of the glory of Your singleness. Accept from them, O my God, what they have left behind in Your days, in which the Sun of Your beauty has shone from the horizon of Your will and the Sovereign of the Messengers has walked among them through Your might and grandeur. You see, O my God, that weeping has now prevented me from remembering and praising You and from praising those whom You have related to the Manifestation of Your own Self, for there has come to my mind the remembrance of my Beloved, my Desire, and my Hope, and the remembrance of His days and His ways.

756 By Your glory, O my God, through me the very name of faithfulness has been lost between earth and heaven, for my Beloved sacrificed Himself in Your path, while I remain alive in Your realm among Your creatures. Yet You know, O my God, that at every moment I long to offer up my spirit and my life in His path, and at every instant I await this. But I do not know why I have been prevented from attaining what I desired. By Your glory, my patience has now come to an end through love of meeting You and longing for union with You. My soul will rejoice only when You behold my face reddened with my blood. Whenever I have cast myself into the hands of my enemies, You have preserved me through the sovereignty of Your will; and whenever I have placed my body beneath the hands of those possessed of hatred, You have protected me through Your power and might.

757 Since You have deprived me of what I desired in Your path, bring me near to the land upon which my Beloved and the Beloved of all worlds, my Desire and the Desire of those who know You, once dwelt, so that I may find solace in it, kiss its dust, and perceive from it the fragrances of Your holiness and the breezes of Your mercy. Perhaps through this my soul may be stilled from its agitation, my spirit from its burning, and my body from its trembling. I ask Your forgiveness, O my God, for all this, for He is even now alive in the All-Glorious Horizon, witnessing and seeing. To this bear witness my soul, my spirit, my being, my pen, my tongue, my outer and inner self, my beginning and my end. There is no God but You, the Almighty, the Most Exalted, the All-Powerful, the All-Protecting, the All-Knowing, the All-Wise.

758 Know, O people, that this Youth of the All-Merciful has at all times intended to establish whatever was revealed by His previous Manifestation, the Point of the Bayān—may the souls of all in existence be sacrificed for Him. I have acted only in accordance with His good pleasure, just as He acted only in accordance with the good pleasure of this Youth. Moreover, whatever appears from this Will has been His very will and purpose, and His own Self is sufficient as a witness. But the idolaters of the Bayān have indulged in conjecture and wandered through the deadly wildernesses of supposition, even alleging that this Youth has said that the Point of the Bayān was created from a single letter of Mine. No, by God! Whatever has been revealed, its Revealer has been and will be the True One—exalted be His sovereignty, exalted be His power, exalted be His grandeur, exalted be His might, exalted be His glory, exalted be His loftiness, exalted be His station, exalted be His splendor, and exalted be His Cause.

759 Say: O shameless ones of the earth! Whatever is revealed in this Revelation has been and will be precisely the words of the Primal Point, and this Youth has always loved nothing but pure servitude. By Him in whose hand is my soul, servitude has been the beloved of my heart, to such a degree that whenever I behold any spot on earth, I long to fall prostrate upon it before God, my Beloved and the Beloved of those who know Him. Would that upon every atom of its dust I had prostrated myself before God, my Lord and the Lord of all worlds! The exalted stations mentioned in the Tablets have been spoken of with regard to the Cause of God, His station, His glory, and His majesty, just as the Point of the Bayān has said: “He speaks in all things, saying, ‘I am God; there is no God but Me. O My creation, worship Me alone.’” Were it not with regard to this station, then by His own beloved Self, I would have spoken of nothing but pure servitude to God, the True One. Such is the matter, but the people do not perceive it.

760 At this point a prayer revealed from the heaven of all-glorious majesty especially for one of the loved ones is cited, so that all may recognize that He has appeared in every degree more perfect than all the Manifestations, even in the station of servitude.

761 Glory be to You, O God, my God! The tongues of all contingent beings testify to Your sovereignty and power, and to my poverty and need before the manifestations of Your wealth. Look, then, O my God, upon this sinner, who has always gazed toward Your forgiveness, his heart turned toward the horizon of Your grace and Your gifts. O my God, from the very first day when You created me by Your command and brought me to life through the breezes of Your all-merciful generosity, I have turned to no one but You. I arose to confront the enemies for the sake of Your sovereignty and power, and summoned everyone to the shore of the ocean of Your oneness and the heaven of the glory of Your singleness. Throughout my days, I have sought not to protect myself from the tyrants among Your creatures, but to exalt Your remembrance among Your creation. Because of this, there has befallen me what none of Your creatures has borne.

762 How many days, O my God, I was alone among the sinful among Your servants, and how many nights, O my Beloved, I was a captive among the heedless among Your creatures! Amid hardship and adversity, I spoke Your praise between Your heaven and Your earth, and proclaimed the wonders of Your remembrance in the kingdom of Your command and creation. Although everything that has proceeded from me is unworthy of the sovereignty of Your glorious oneness and unbefitting Your station and power, yet, by Your glory, O my Beloved, I have found no existence of my own before the city of Your glory. Whenever I wish to offer You praise, my heart restrains me, for no one other than You can soar in the atmosphere of the kingdom of Your nearness or ascend to the heaven of the dominion of Your presence. By Your glory! I see that, were I to prostrate myself before a handful of dust until the end that has no end, because of its relationship to Your name, the Maker, I would not consider myself far from drawing near to it. I see that my deed would be unworthy of it, being confined instead within the limits of my own self. Were I to serve one of Your servants, standing before him for as long as Your kingdom endures and Your dominion remains, because of his relationship to Your name, the Creator, then, by Your glory, I would find myself falling short in serving him and deprived of what befits him, for in this station nothing is seen but their relationship to Your names and attributes.

763 How can one whose condition is such make mention of Him at a gesture of whose finger the names and their kingdom, and the attributes and their dominion, were created, and at another gesture of whom the letter kāf was joined to the letter nūn, and from this appeared that which the loftiest hearts of those drawn near among Your chosen ones, and the most radiant faculties of the sincere among Your loved ones, are powerless to comprehend?

764 By Your glory, O my Beloved! I am bewildered by the manifestations of Your handiwork and the dawning places of Your power. I see myself powerless to know even Your least sign; how much less can I know Your own Self! I therefore ask You, O my God, by Your name through which You have made the lovers soar in the atmosphere of Your will and guided those who yearn to the paradise of Your nearness and reunion with You, to let the fragrances of assurance waft from the paradise of Your care over those of Your loved ones who are in distress, in these days when the winds of trial have surrounded them from every direction. Souls have been shaken by the force of Your decree, and the pillars of existence have trembled at what has descended upon them from the heaven of Your ordainment. Their agitation has reached such a degree that the lamp of Your love and remembrance is almost extinguished in the niche of their hearts. You indeed have power over whatever You will, and You are indeed the Ever-Forgiving, the Generous.

765 O my God and my Lord! You hear the lamentation of those who love You and their cries from every region over what has befallen them at the hands of those whose hearts are deprived of the breaths of Your love. They have no helper to assist them and no supporter to come to their aid. Nor is there anyone to restrain their enemies from harming them; thus they do whatever they desire and act as they please. Aid, then, O my God, with Your wondrous assistance, Your loved ones who have sought help from none but You and turned to no one besides You, whose eyes have awaited the wonders of Your gifts and Your favors. Have mercy on them, O my God, through the wonders of Your mercy, and bring them into the stronghold of Your protection and care. You, O my God, have always been the refuge of the fearful and the shelter of those in distress.

766 I ask You not to deprive these weak ones of the wonders of Your generosity and bounty, and not to leave them in the hands of those whose beings You created from nothing but the fire of Your wrath and Your subduing might. They have never sensed the fragrances of compassion and fairness, and the world has so beguiled them with its deceptions that they have denied Your proof, associated partners with You, disbelieved in Your signs, and shed the blood of Your loved ones and Your trusted ones. By Your glory, O my Beloved! They have committed what no one before them has committed, and have thereby deserved Your wrath and the scourges of Your subduing might. Seize them through Your sovereignty, and set over them one who will show them no mercy unless they return to You, enter beneath the shadow of Your care, and turn to You in repentance. You have always been powerful and will forever be mighty. You are indeed the All-Powerful, the Most Exalted, the Just, the All-Wise.

767 Glory be to You, O God, my God! Aid this Wronged One who has been afflicted at the hands of the unjust among Your creatures and those among Your enemies who associate partners with You, even though he has never drawn a breath except by Your permission and command. I was asleep, O my God, in the cradle of stillness. The winds of Your grace and favors passed over me, and through them You awakened me by Your sovereignty and Your gifts. You raised me up among Your servants to praise You and exalt Your word. Then most of Your creatures opposed me.

768 By Your glory, O my God! I had never expected from them what they have shown, after You had given them the glad tidings of this Revelation in the scriptures of Your Cause and the Tablets of Your decree. Not a word has descended from You through which You have not taken from Your creatures and Your creation a covenant concerning this Youth. I am therefore bewildered, O my God, and do not know what to do among these people. Whenever I fall silent concerning the wonders of Your remembrance, the Spirit makes me speak between Your heaven and Your earth. Whenever I am still, what blows from the right hand of Your purpose and will stirs me. I find myself like a leaf moved by the winds of Your decree and carried wherever You will, by Your command and permission.

769 Through what has proceeded from me, every discerning person knows with certainty that the matter is not in my hands but in Yours, and that the reins of choice are not in my grasp but in Your grasp and power. Nevertheless, O my God, the people of Your realm have gathered against me, and at every moment they bring down upon me what terrifies the inmost beings of Your chosen ones and Your trusted ones. I therefore ask You, O my God, by Your name through which You have guided the lovers to the heavenly river of Your grace and favors, and drawn those who yearn to the paradise of Your nearness and presence, to open the eyes of Your creatures so that in this Revelation they may witness the manifestation of the glory of Your singleness and the dawning of the lights of Your countenance and beauty. Purify them, O my God, of conjectures and delusions, that they may sense the fragrances of holiness from the garment of Your Revelation and Your Cause. Perhaps they will not inflict upon me what would deprive their own souls of the breaths of the expressions of Your all-merciful grace in the days of the appearance of the Manifestation of Your Self and the Dawning Place of Your Cause, nor commit what would make their own beings deserving of the manifestations of Your subduing might and wrath.

770 You know, O my God, that I lived among the people of the Bayān as one of them, associated with them with ardent longing, and summoned them to You at evening and at dawn through the wonders of Your revelation and inspiration. Yet there has befallen me at their hands what the inhabitants of the cities of Your creation are powerless to recount. By Your glory, O my Beloved! No morning came without my becoming a target for the arrows of their malice, and no evening came without the spears of their hatred striking me. Although You had made me aware of what was within them and given me power over them, I concealed it and remained patient, looking toward Your appointed time.

771 When the promise came and the appointed time was fulfilled, I moved the hem of the veil by an immeasurably small degree. Then all who dwell in the dominion of command and creation were terrified, except those whom You had created from the fire of Your love, the air of longing for You, the water of Your care, and the dust of Your grace. Upon them the people of the Concourse on high and the inhabitants of the cities of eternity invoke blessings. Praise be to You, O my God, for protecting those who affirm Your oneness, destroying those who associate partners with You, and distinguishing everyone from one another through another word that issued from the mouth of Your purpose and appeared from the Pen of Your will. Because of this, servants who had been created by the word of Your command and raised up by Your will opposed me. Their rejection reached such a degree that they disbelieved in You and Your signs and waged war against Your own Self.

772 By Your glory, O my Beloved! The Pen can never recount what has befallen the Manifestation of Your Cause, the Dawning Place of Your revelation, and the Dayspring of Your inspiration at their hands. Praise be to You in all of this! By Your glory, O my God, I have longed for what has been ordained in the heaven of Your decree and the kingdom of Your ordainment, for whatever befalls me in Your path is the beloved of my being and the desire of my soul. This has come about only through Your might and power. I am the one, O my God, who through Your love has become independent of all who are in the heavens and on earth. Through it I shall never be dismayed, even should the affliction of all the worlds befall me.

773 If only this were the hour when my blood is shed upon the face of the earth before You, and You behold me in the condition in which You beheld those of Your servants who are near to You and the chosen ones among the best of Your creation. Praise be to You, O my God, for what You have decreed through the sovereignty of Your judgment and what You decree through Your ordainment and sanction. I ask You, O my Beloved, by Your name through which You have raised the banners of Your Cause and the lights of Your countenance have shone, to send down upon me and upon the sincere among Your servants every good thing You have ordained in the Tablets. Then appoint for us a seat of truth in Your presence, O You in whose hand is the dominion of all things. You are indeed the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the All-Merciful.

774 The hope is that, through divine grace, all may attain the robes of humanity in this spiritual springtime, exchange their old, worn, threadbare, and tattered garments for new, divine garments, and arise to uphold the Cause of the True One to the utmost of their ability and power. May the people of Bahāʾ be wings for one another, so that all may soar in this spiritual atmosphere and this luminous expanse of holiness. May they speak only what God has ordained, walk only in accordance with His good pleasure, and speak of no matter except with God’s permission. They should not content themselves with their own understanding of the meanings of the divine words, for none but God knows the meanings of His own words as they truly are. Thus, some have, without permission, said certain things concerning the branches of the divine Tree that have not been pleasing to God, for such statements cause corruption and dissension in the Cause, especially in these days when most of those who turn away are waiting for some corruption to arise in the Cause.

775 Fear God, O people, and do not be among those who transgress. Remember what We have commanded you, not what your own selves and desires have commanded you. Fear God and be among the God-fearing. We caused him to speak when six successive years of his life had passed, that he might be a proof of this most great and mighty Announcement. The True One caused him to speak so that he might be a proof of His Cause. Those who have spoken such words have become heedless of the greatness of this Cause, for if He wishes to cause all things to speak, He is able to do so, and He indeed has power over all things. Before a thousand, [corrupted original text] anyone other than the One who has appeared appears.
