Thus was the command sent down at the beginning of this Revelation, if you are among those who understand.

776 God has raised up, and will raise up, guides for this Cause who speak on His behalf, turn toward Him at all times, and make no move without His permission. They will not exchange a single one of My melodies for all who are in the heavens and on earth. They are My guides on earth, My chosen ones in the lands, and My trusted ones among the servants. Upon them rest My mercy, My glory, My remembrance, and My praise. They are honored servants.

777 Let this much be known: until now, the details of this most wondrous, most glorious Revelation have not been set forth. Were they set forth, all would surely testify that He is the One whom no one before Him has surpassed and whom no one among those to come will surpass. Whoever makes a claim before the thousand has fabricated a lie against God, for this corrupts the Cause, and the Temple of the Will cannot be established upon a throne of mighty glory. At the beginning of this exalted Revelation, verses were revealed on this subject, and some of them are recorded in certain Tablets. Consider them, that you may stand firm in the Cause and be among the steadfast.

778 Furthermore, let it be known that what is meant by the Valley of Nabīl mentioned in the Tablets has been, and will be, this Most Great Revelation itself. That valley is the holy land created above the levels of paradise, and none but the people of Bahāʾ can enter it. By God, they are the people of the Valley of Nabīl, the Valley of Grandeur, the Valley of Independence, the Valley of Majesty, the Valley of Power, the Valley of Might, the Valley of Eternity, the Valley of Sanctification, the Valley of Transcendence, and the Valley of Glory. All others are outside the Valley of Nabīl, bewildered and afflicted in the wilderness of self and desire. The very being of Nabīl then disavows them, their works, their deeds, and whatever proceeds from them. To this all things bear witness, if you know.

779 Beware, O people, lest you distort the words of God from their proper places. Bear witness to what God has witnessed, and be brothers seated upon couches facing one another. Unite in such a way that no one can detect among you the scents of discord. Through your unity, the Cause of God will become manifest among His creatures, its traces will be established among His servants, and its lights will be demonstrated among the peoples of the world. The hope is that all may gather around the all-embracing Word, occupy themselves with the remembrance and praise of the True One, and appear on earth in perfect detachment. May they be so ablaze with the fire of divine love that all may be kindled by its heat and turn their gaze toward the realm of holiness.

780 Whatever they do not understand of the divine verses, they should ask about from their mine and source. The purpose of this statement is that no disagreement over the meaning of the words should arise among the loved ones of God, and that they should know that the divine words all proceed from a single point and return to it. Beware lest you perceive discord among them. Although outwardly they may be revealed in differing forms, inwardly they return and lead to a single word and a single point. Thus, in certain passages it has been stated that no one knows the meaning of the divine words except God. There is no doubt that this statement is true, for a single letter among the revealed letters has had, and will have, a particular effect and particular meanings in each of the infinite worlds, according to the requirements of that world. And [corrupted original text] the divine letters and words have each been, and will be, a treasury of knowledge bestowed by God. None but God has known what is treasured within them. No one has been, or will be, capable of knowing them as they deserve to be known.

781 Likewise, in another passage it has been stated that all understand the divine verses, for if they did not possess this capacity, the authority of the verses as proof could not be demonstrated. These two statements differ outwardly, but to those endowed with insight and keen vision it is clear that the statement that all understand means according to their own capacity and measure, not according to the capacity and measure of what has been sent down from God, the All-Powerful, the All-Protecting, the Self-Subsisting.

782 Today, those who strive to violate the sanctity of the Bayān, as you have seen and heard, have put forward claims to a Cause in view of the foolishness of their followers. God’s curse be upon those who have transgressed the bounds of the Bayān, violated the sanctity of God, broken His covenant, and disbelieved in the Primal Point and in His Beloved and His Desired One, who has appeared in His name, the All-Glorious, between earth and heaven, and from whom the hearts of those brought near and the breasts of the sincere have received light. But they have been mistaken in what they imagined. Through God’s power and His might, the fortress of the Cause will be guarded by the hand of power. He indeed has power over all things.

783 At the close of this discourse, the Dove of the Cause sang upon the branches of the Tree of lofty glory, saying: “O people of the Bayān, fear God, and do not spread corruption on earth after it has been set right, nor commit indecency. Purify yourselves of everything forbidden in the Book of God, the Most High, the Most Great. Beware lest you shed blood. Whoever has shed blood is not of Me, and God disavows him.” This is what was sent down at the beginning of this Revelation from the kingdom of your Lord, the All-Merciful, the Compassionate.

784 Beware lest you commit what your minds and hearts abhor. Fear God, O company of believers. Detach yourselves from the world and all that is in it, and do not dispose of people’s property without their permission. Thus have We sent down before and now, from One who is Mighty and Beautiful. Be God’s trusted ones in the lands, such that, were you to pass through cities of gold, your eyes would not turn toward them. Such should be your conduct, O company of those who affirm His oneness. Aid your Lord, the All-Merciful, through your deeds, so that God’s servants throughout the heavens and the earth may sense from you the fragrances of the True One. Adorn yourselves, O people, with the attributes of God, and do not commit what would diminish the honor of the Manifestation of His own Self among His creatures. Thus has God counseled you in Tablets of preserving might. Do not assail anyone or dispute with any soul. If you find someone receptive, remind him through the word of the Most Great; and if you find someone who turns away, turn away from him, then turn toward God, your Lord. He will indeed suffice you against all the peoples of the world.

785 Unite, O My loved ones, and gather around the law of My Cause. Do not be among those who disagree. The nearest of you to God are those who most fear Him and are most humble before Him, if you are among those who understand. By God, the True One, that you should suffer injustice is dearer to Me than that you should wrong anyone. This is of My nature and among My finest attributes, if you are among those who are certain. Be patient, O My loved ones, in hardship and adversity. If an oppressor wrongs you, leave judgment upon him to God, in whose hand is the dominion of all things. He indeed has power to do whatever He wills, and He is the most severe of avengers.

786 We have counseled you in most of the Tablets not to assail anyone, yet you have done what has brought harm upon the Tree of lofty glory. O people, fear God and do not transgress what you have been commanded in the Tablets. This is indeed better for you than what you possess, if you are among those who understand. Avoid those who have disbelieved, associated partners with God, disputed His verses, and made war against His own exalted and mighty Self. Then be patient as We have been patient, and do not wage war against any of those who associate partners with God. Put your trust in God in all matters. He will indeed aid Me as He aided Me the first time, when I was in prison under chains and fetters. He aided Me in truth with the hosts of the heavens and the earth until He brought Me out of it with manifest authority. In His grasp is the dominion of all things. He aids whomever He wills, however He wills. He is indeed the All-Powerful, the Omnipotent.

787 Beware, O people, lest you cling to names and object to the One who created them by a command from Himself. There is no God but Him, the All-Powerful, the All-Knowing, the All-Wise. Say: He created the names just as He created all things, if you are among those who know. Everyone endowed with power is helpless before the sovereignty of His power; everyone endowed with knowledge is ignorant before His knowledge; and everyone endowed with wealth is poor at His door, which He has opened to all created beings. Beware lest the names keep you from their Creator. Shun those who have clung to them and remained devoted to them. Say: The prophet is the one who has heard My tidings and believed in My own Self; the messenger is the one who has conveyed My messages; the imam is the one who has stood before My face and attained My days; the guardian is the one who has entered the stronghold of My guardianship and detached himself from all but Me; and the appointed successor is the one who has enjoined upon himself, and then upon the servants, love for Me, remembrance of Me, and praise of Me. Thus has the sun of utterance shone from the horizon of the will of your Lord, the All-Merciful, in this sanctified, inaccessible, exalted, and impregnable Paradise. Praise be to God, My Beloved and the Beloved of those who know Him.

788 And now We address the one who has fabricated lies against God, objected to Him, and sent fiery pages to the seat of holiness, setting down in them what has burned the hearts of the chosen ones and those brought near, so that he may awaken from the sleep of heedlessness and turn toward the holy region, the seat of God, the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the All-Knowing. Although he has committed a sin heavier than the weight of the heavens and the earth, yet, since the oceans of grace have surged in these days, if he repents and returns, perhaps God will forgive him through His mercy, which has preceded all the worlds.

789 Say: O heedless one, if you will listen to this servant, act upon what We tell you, so that you may be among those who attain their goal. Purify yourself of all but God, then say, “In the name of God and by God,” and wash with water. When you have finished, say, “O my God and my Beloved, just as You have cleansed my outward being with this water, cleanse my heart and my inmost being of self and desire, so that I may know You through Your own Self and detach myself from all the worlds.” Then place your face upon the dust in humility before God, the Lord of lords, and say, “O my God and my Master, I ask You by Your name, through which the earthquake has appeared among the tribes of earth and heaven, the clamor has risen among the company of creation, and the souls of the doubters and the realities of the heedless have been shaken, to forgive my transgressions, the like of which the eye of creation has never witnessed. By Your might, O my Beloved, I have come to You with a sin that cannot be equaled by the sins of those created through Your most wondrous, incomparable word.

790 Then have mercy on this sinner, clothe me in the robe of forgiveness, purify me of human limitations, and turn me toward Your Most Great Countenance, so that nothing may remain in my breast but the wonders of the knowledge of You, nor in my heart but the manifestations of Your love, nor shine from my face but the radiance of Your good pleasure. You are indeed powerful to do whatever You will, and You are the All-Protecting, the All-Powerful, the Almighty, the Omnipotent. O my God, this is a sinner who has hastened to the city of Your forgiveness, and this poor one has clung to the hem of the robe of Your wealth and self-sufficiency. O Lord, do not deprive me of what is with You. Then make me wholly devoted to You and humble before the manifestations of the lights of the glory of Your singleness. You have ever been the Possessor of ancient grace, and You are powerful over all things.”

791 And We ask God, through this, to open your eyes, make known to you what you did not know before, and teach you that through which you may be made independent of all but Him. With Him is the knowledge of the heavens and the earth, and He indeed encompasses all things.

Volume 034: Middle Edirne (March 1866 – September 1867) (8 of 8)
Volume 036: Late Edirne (September 1867 – August 1868) (1 of 7)
